---
title: 'ЕГЭ 2 - Household Chores  '
description: 'Articles from CNN'
---
import InlineCode from '../../components/InlineCode.astro';

<InlineCode code={`<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
<style>
body{font-family:'Segoe UI',sans-serif;background:#f8f9fa;padding:20px;position:relative}

/* Кнопка-переключатель меню */
.nav-toggle{
  position:fixed;
  right:20px;
  top:20px;
  z-index:1001;
  width:48px;
  height:48px;
  border-radius:50%;
  border:2px solid #e2e8f0;
  background:#fff;
  cursor:pointer;
  display:flex;
  align-items:center;
  justify-content:center;
  font-size:22px;
  color:#334155;
  box-shadow:0 4px 15px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);
  transition:all .25s ease;
  line-height:1;
}
.nav-toggle:hover{
  border-color:#3b82f6;
  color:#1e40af;
  background:#eff6ff;
  transform:scale(1.05);
}

/* Сама панель меню */
.nav-menu{
  position:fixed;
  right:20px;
  top:80px;
  z-index:1000;
  display:flex;
  flex-direction:column;
  gap:8px;
  background:#fff;
  padding:16px;
  border-radius:12px;
  box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);
  border:1px solid #e2e8f0;
  max-height:calc(100vh - 100px);
  overflow-y:auto;
  transition:transform .3s ease, opacity .3s ease;
  transform:translateX(0);
  opacity:1;
}

/* Скрытое состояние меню */
.nav-menu.hidden{
  transform:translateX(calc(100% + 40px));
  opacity:0;
  pointer-events:none;
}

.nav-btn{
  padding:8px 14px;
  border:2px solid #e2e8f0;
  border-radius:8px;
  background:#fff;
  cursor:pointer;
  font-weight:600;
  color:#334155;
  transition:all .2s;
  white-space:nowrap;
  text-align:left;
  font-size:14px;
}
.nav-btn:hover{
  border-color:#3b82f6;
  color:#1e40af;
  background:#eff6ff;
}

.section{scroll-margin-top:80px;margin:60px 0 40px 0;border:none;border-top:3px solid #e2e8f0;padding-top:40px}
.section h1{font-size:42px;font-weight:800;color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 30px 0;text-align:center;letter-spacing:-0.5px}
.content-placeholder{background:#fff;padding:30px;border-radius:12px;margin:20px 0;min-height:200px;border:2px dashed #cbd5e1;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:#64748b;font-size:16px}

/* На мобильных меню по умолчанию скрыто */
@media (max-width: 768px){
  .nav-menu{
    transform:translateX(calc(100% + 40px));
    opacity:0;
    pointer-events:none;
  }
  .nav-menu.visible{
    transform:translateX(0);
    opacity:1;
    pointer-events:auto;
  }
  .section h1{font-size:32px}
}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<!-- Кнопка переключения меню -->
<button class="nav-toggle" onclick="toggleMenu()" aria-label="Скрыть/показать меню">☰</button>

<div class="nav-menu" id="navMenu">
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('Vocabulary')">Vocabulary</button>
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('confusing-words')">Confusing words</button>
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('grammar-1')">Grammar 1</button>
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('grammar-2')">Grammar 2</button>
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('grammar-3')">Grammar 3</button>
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('prepositions')">Prepositions</button>
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('reading')">Reading</button>
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('listening')">Listening</button>
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('wordbuilding')">Wordbuilding</button>
  <button class="nav-btn" onclick="goTo('questions')">Questions</button>
</div>

<script>
  function toggleMenu(){
    const menu = document.getElementById('navMenu');
    const btn = document.querySelector('.nav-toggle');
    const isMobile = window.innerWidth <= 768;

    if(isMobile){
      menu.classList.toggle('visible');
      btn.textContent = menu.classList.contains('visible') ? '✕' : '☰';
    } else {
      menu.classList.toggle('hidden');
      btn.textContent = menu.classList.contains('hidden') ? '☰' : '✕';
    }
  }

  function goTo(id){
    document.getElementById(id).scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth', block:'center'});
    // На мобильных автоматически сворачиваем меню после выбора
    if(window.innerWidth <= 768){
      const menu = document.getElementById('navMenu');
      menu.classList.remove('visible');
      document.querySelector('.nav-toggle').textContent = '☰';
    }
  }
</script>

<!-- ================= Vocabulary ================= -->

class="hw-btn" style="background:#f59e0b;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) Doing household <span class="vt-word">chores<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>chore</b><br>домашняя обязанность, дело</span></span> every weekend keeps the house organized and prevents mess from accumulating in every room. 🏠</div><div class="ex-item">2) I need to separate the <span class="vt-word">laundry<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>laundry</b><br>стирка, грязное бельё</span></span> into whites and colors to avoid dye transfer and keep clothes looking bright and new. 👕</div><div class="ex-item">3) Use the right <span class="vt-word">detergent<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>detergent</b><br>моющее средство, порошок</span></span> for delicate fabrics, or they might shrink or lose their soft texture during the wash cycle. 🧴</div><div class="ex-item">4) Please run the <span class="vt-word">vacuum<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>vacuum</b><br>пылесос</span></span> over the living room carpet before guests arrive, because pet hair and crumbs are visible on the floor. 🌪️</div><div class="ex-item">5) After sweeping, use a damp <span class="vt-word">mop<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>mop</b><br>швабра</span></span> <span class="prep">to</span> clean the kitchen tiles thoroughly and leave them shining without streaks. 💧</div><div class="ex-item">6) Keep a sturdy <span class="vt-word">broom<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>broom</b><br>метла, веник</span></span> near the back door so you can quickly sweep away dirt and leaves before they get tracked inside. 🧹</div><div class="ex-item">7) Store dry goods in airtight containers inside the <span class="vt-word">cupboard<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>cupboard</b><br>шкаф, кухонный шкафчик</span></span> to protect them from moisture, pests, and stale air. 📦</div><div class="ex-item">8) Treat that red wine <span class="vt-word">stain<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>stain</b><br>пятно, загрязнение</span></span> immediately with cold water and salt, or it will permanently damage the tablecloth. 🍷</div><div class="ex-item">9) This kitchen <span class="vt-word">appliance<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>appliance</b><br>бытовой прибор</span></span> has a self-cleaning function, but you still need to wipe the exterior regularly to remove grease and fingerprints. 🍳</div><div class="ex-item">10) Throw the empty bottle in the recycling <span class="vt-word">bin<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>bin</b><br>мусорное ведро</span></span> to help reduce landfill waste and support sustainable habits at home. ♻️</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Weekend Cleaning 🗓️</h4><div class="practice-text">Every Saturday, I clean my house. First, I do my <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">chores 🏠</span><span class="tooltip">📌 обязанности</span></span>: laundry, vacuuming, and dusting. I sort the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">laundry 👕</span><span class="tooltip">📌 бельё</span></span> by color and add the right <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">detergent 🧴</span><span class="tooltip">📌 порошок</span></span>. While the machine works, I sweep the floors with my <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">broom 🧹</span><span class="tooltip">📌 метла</span></span>. Then I use the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">mop 💧</span><span class="tooltip">📌 швабра</span></span> for the kitchen and bathroom. For carpets, I use the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">vacuum 🌪️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пылесос</span></span> to remove dust and pet hair. If I see a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">stain 🍷</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пятно</span></span>, I clean it quickly. After that, I wipe the kitchen <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">appliance 🍳</span><span class="tooltip">📌 прибор</span></span>. Finally, I empty the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">bin ♻️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 ведро</span></span> and organize the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">cupboard 📦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 шкафчик</span></span>. A clean home starts with simple steps!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#ef4444;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Nouns</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">Doing household ___ every weekend keeps the house organized.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q1" id="hw_n_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) chores</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q1" id="hw_n_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) laundry</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q1" id="hw_n_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) detergent</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Laundry</b> = бельё, стирка.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ chores = обязанности. vacuum = пылесос.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">Use the right ___ for delicate fabrics to avoid damage.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q3" id="hw_n_q3a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) mop</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q3" id="hw_n_q3b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) detergent</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q3" id="hw_n_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) broom</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Cupboard</b> = кухонный шкаф.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ appliance = прибор. bin = мусорное ведро.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">Treat that red wine ___ immediately with cold water.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q8" id="hw_n_q8a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) stain</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q8" id="hw_n_q8b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) appliance</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q8" id="hw_n_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) chores</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Stain</b> = пятно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ appliance = прибор. chores = обязанности.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">This kitchen ___ has a self-cleaning function.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q9" id="hw_n_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) appliance</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q9" id="hw_n_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) bin</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q9" id="hw_n_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) mop</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Appliance</b> = бытовой прибор.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ bin = ведро. mop = швабра.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">Throw the bottle in the recycling ___ to reduce waste.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q10" id="hw_n_q10a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_n_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) bin</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q10" id="hw_n_q10b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) detergent</label><input type="radio" name="hw_n_q10" id="hw_n_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_n_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) vacuum</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Bin</b> = мусорное ведро.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ detergent = моющее средство. vacuum = пылесос.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

class="hw-btn" style="background:#10b981;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) Use a stiff brush to <span class="vt-word">scrub<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>scrub</b><br>тереть, чистить щёткой</span></span> the bathroom tiles <span class="prep">with</span> cleaning solution to remove built-up soap scum and mold. 🧽</div><div class="ex-item">2) After cleaning, gently <span class="vt-word">polish<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>polish</b><br>полировать, натирать</span></span> the wooden table <span class="prep">with</span> a soft cloth to restore its shine and protect the surface from scratches. ✨</div><div class="ex-item">3) Remember to <span class="vt-word">wipe<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>wipe</b><br>вытирать</span></span> the kitchen counters <span class="prep">with</span> an antibacterial spray after cooking to keep the area hygienic and ready for the next meal. 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item">4) Lightly <span class="vt-word">dust<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>dust</b><br>вытирать пыль</span></span> the bookshelves and picture frames <span class="prep">with</span> a microfiber cloth, as dust can trigger allergies if left for too long. 🌬️</div><div class="ex-item">5) <span class="vt-word">Sweep<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>sweep</b><br>подметать</span></span> the porch before it rains, so wet leaves won't stick to the concrete and become difficult to remove later. 🍂</div><div class="ex-item">6) Carefully <span class="vt-word">fold<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>fold</b><br>складывать</span></span> each shirt <span class="prep">into</span> neat rectangles before placing them in the drawer to save space and prevent wrinkles. 👔</div><div class="ex-item">7) You should <span class="vt-word">iron<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>iron</b><br>гладить</span></span> your uniform the night before so you're not rushed in the morning and look professional for work. 🧵</div><div class="ex-item">8) Let's <span class="vt-word">tidy<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidy</b><br>прибирать, наводить порядок</span></span> the living room <span class="prep">up</span> quickly — the guests will arrive in thirty minutes and we need everything looking presentable. 🛋️</div><div class="ex-item">9) After the clothes are dry, <span class="vt-word">load<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>load</b><br>загружать, загружать в машину</span></span> them <span class="prep">into</span> the dryer immediately to avoid mildew smell and keep fabrics fresh. 🔄</div><div class="ex-item">10) <span class="vt-word">Unload<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unload</b><br>выгружать</span></span> the dishwasher carefully <span class="prep">before</span> dinner starts so you can set the table without waiting for clean plates. 🍴</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Cleaning Day 🧹</h4><div class="practice-text">I start my cleaning day by deciding what to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy 🛋️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 прибирать</span></span> first. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">sweep 🍂</span><span class="tooltip">📌 подметать</span></span> the floors, then <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dust 🌬️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 вытирать пыль</span></span> all surfaces. If there is dirt, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">scrub 🧽</span><span class="tooltip">📌 тереть</span></span> it carefully. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe 🍽️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 вытирать</span></span> the counters and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">polish ✨</span><span class="tooltip">📌 полировать</span></span> the furniture. After washing clothes, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">load 🔄</span><span class="tooltip">📌 загружать</span></span> the dryer and later <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unload 🍴</span><span class="tooltip">📌 выгружать</span></span> it. I always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">fold 👔</span><span class="tooltip">📌 складывать</span></span> and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">iron 🧵</span><span class="tooltip">📌 гладить</span></span> carefully. A good routine makes everything feel fresh!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#0d9488;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Verbs</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">Use a stiff brush to ___ the bathroom tiles with cleaning solution.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q1" id="hw_v_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) scrub</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q1" id="hw_v_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) polish</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q1" id="hw_v_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) dust</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Scrub</b> = тереть, чистить щёткой.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ polish = полировать. dust = вытирать пыль.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">After cleaning, gently ___ the wooden table with a soft cloth.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q2" id="hw_v_q2a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) wipe</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q2" id="hw_v_q2b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) polish</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q2" id="hw_v_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) sweep</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Polish</b> = полировать, натирать.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ wipe = вытирать. sweep = подметать.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">Remember to ___ the kitchen counters with antibacterial spray after cooking.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q3" id="hw_v_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) wipe</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q3" id="hw_v_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) fold</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q3" id="hw_v_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) iron</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Wipe</b> = вытирать.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ fold = складывать. iron = гладить.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">Lightly ___ the bookshelves with a microfiber cloth.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q4" id="hw_v_q4a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) dust</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q4" id="hw_v_q4b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) tidy</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q4" id="hw_v_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) load</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Dust</b> = вытирать пыль.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ tidy = прибирать. load = загружать.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">___ the porch before it rains, so wet leaves won't stick.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q5" id="hw_v_q5a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) Sweep</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q5" id="hw_v_q5b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) Unload</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q5" id="hw_v_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) Polish</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Sweep</b> = подметать.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ unload = выгружать. polish = полировать.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">Carefully ___ each shirt into neat rectangles before placing in the drawer.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q6" id="hw_v_q6a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) fold</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q6" id="hw_v_q6b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) iron</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q6" id="hw_v_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) scrub</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Fold</b> = складывать.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ iron = гладить. scrub = тереть.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">You should ___ your uniform the night before work.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q7" id="hw_v_q7a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) iron</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q7" id="hw_v_q7b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) tidy</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q7" id="hw_v_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) dust</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Iron</b> = гладить.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ tidy = прибирать. dust = вытирать пыль.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">Let's ___ the living room up quickly before guests arrive.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q8" id="hw_v_q8a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) tidy</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q8" id="hw_v_q8b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) load</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q8" id="hw_v_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) unload</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Tidy up</b> = прибирать, наводить порядок.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ load = загружать. unload = выгружать.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">After clothes are dry, ___ them into the dryer immediately.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q9" id="hw_v_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) load</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q9" id="hw_v_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) unload</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q9" id="hw_v_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) wipe</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Load</b> = загружать.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ unload = выгружать. wipe = вытирать.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">___ the dishwasher carefully before dinner starts.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q10" id="hw_v_q10a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_v_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) Unload</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q10" id="hw_v_q10b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) Load</label><input type="radio" name="hw_v_q10" id="hw_v_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_v_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) Polish</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unload</b> = выгружать.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ load = загружать. polish = полировать.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) Let's <span class="vt-word">clean up<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clean up</b><br>убирать, приводить в порядок</span></span> the kitchen <span class="prep">after</span> cooking so it doesn't attract pests and stays hygienic for tomorrow's meals. 🧼</div><div class="ex-item">2) We need to <span class="vt-word">tidy up<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidy up</b><br>наводить порядок</span></span> the garage this weekend because it's filled with old boxes and we can't find the tools. 🛠️</div><div class="ex-item">3) Please <span class="vt-word">put away<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>put away</b><br>убирать на место</span></span> your toys <span class="prep">before</span> bedtime, otherwise someone might trip over them in the dark. 🧸</div><div class="ex-item">4) Don't <span class="vt-word">throw out<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>throw out</b><br>выбрасывать</span></span> those old clothes — we can donate them <span class="prep">to</span> a local charity instead of sending them to landfill. ♻️</div><div class="ex-item">5) After meals, <span class="vt-word">wipe down<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>wipe down</b><br>протирать начисто</span></span> all surfaces <span class="prep">with</span> disinfectant to kill bacteria and maintain a sanitary eating area. 🦠</div><div class="ex-item">6) Help me <span class="vt-word">clear away<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clear away</b><br>убирать со стола, уносить</span></span> the dishes <span class="prep">to</span> the sink so we can enjoy the rest of the evening without worrying about mess. 🍲</div><div class="ex-item">7) Let's <span class="vt-word">sort out<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>sort out</b><br>разбирать, сортировать</span></span> the recycling <span class="prep">into</span> separate bins to make the process easier for the waste collection team. 🗑️</div><div class="ex-item">8) After the laundry is dry, <span class="vt-word">hang up<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>hang up</b><br>развешивать</span></span> the shirts <span class="prep">on</span> wooden hangers to preserve their shape and avoid ironing later. 👗</div><div class="ex-item">9) You made a mistake painting the wall, so you'll have to <span class="vt-word">do it over<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>do over</b><br>переделывать</span></span> from scratch to match the rest perfectly. 🎨</div><div class="ex-item">10) Remember to <span class="vt-word">take out<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>take out</b><br>выносить</span></span> the rubbish <span class="prep">before</span> collection day, or the bin will overflow and smell terrible. 🚛</div><div class="ex-item">11) Can you <span class="vt-word">put it away<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>put away</b><br>убирать на место</span></span> properly? The toys are scattered all over the floor again. 🧸</div><div class="ex-item">12) I need to <span class="vt-word">throw them out<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>throw out</b><br>выбрасывать</span></span> — these old magazines are just collecting dust on the shelves. 📰</div><div class="ex-item">13) She always <span class="vt-word">cleans it up<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clean up</b><br>убирать</span></span> immediately after cooking, never leaving dirty dishes in the sink overnight. 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item">14) Please <span class="vt-word">tidy it up<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidy up</b><br>прибирать</span></span> before the guests arrive — the living room looks like a tornado hit it! 🌪️</div><div class="ex-item">15) We should <span class="vt-word">sort them out<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>sort out</b><br>разбирать</span></span> by color and fabric type before putting them in the washing machine. 👕</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Household Flow 🔄</h4><div class="practice-text">Every evening, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean up 🧼</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать</span></span> the kitchen and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe down 🦠</span><span class="tooltip">📌 протирать</span></span> all counters thoroughly. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clear away 🍲</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать со стола</span></span> dishes and help <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">sort out 🗑️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 сортировать</span></span> recycling into different bins. I always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">put away 🧸</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать на место</span></span> items in their proper spots — if I leave things out, they create clutter. When the floors are dirty, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy up 🛋️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 прибирать</span></span> the whole room systematically. On wash day, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">hang up 👗</span><span class="tooltip">📌 развешивать</span></span> clothes to dry immediately. I never <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">throw out ♻️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 выбрасывать</span></span> useful items without checking if they can be donated or recycled. When I make a mistake while cleaning or organizing, I just <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">do it over 🎨</span><span class="tooltip">📌 переделывать</span></span> carefully. And of course, I always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">take out 🚛</span><span class="tooltip">📌 выносить</span></span> the trash before it overflows. If something is dirty, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean it up 🧽</span><span class="tooltip">📌 чистить</span></span> right away. If toys are scattered, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">put them away 🧸</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать их</span></span> immediately. If surfaces are dusty, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe them down 🧹</span><span class="tooltip">📌 протирать их</span></span>. If clothes are wrinkled, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy them up 👔</span><span class="tooltip">📌 приводить в порядок</span></span>. <b>Tidy it up</b> = прибрать это (местоимение внутри).</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ tidy up it = неверный порядок. up tidy it = неверный порядок.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">15</div><div class="qz-sent">We should ___ by color and fabric type before washing.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pv_q15" id="hw_pv_q15a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pv_q15a" class="qz-opt">A) sort them out</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pv_q15" id="hw_pv_q15b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pv_q15b" class="qz-opt">B) sort out them</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pv_q15" id="hw_pv_q15c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pv_q15c" class="qz-opt">C) out sort them</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) It's time to <span class="vt-word">do the laundry<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>do the laundry</b><br>стирать, заниматься стиркой</span></span> because the hamper is overflowing and we'll need clean clothes for the week ahead. 👕</div><div class="ex-item">2) Please <span class="vt-word">make the bed<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>make the bed</b><br>заправлять кровать</span></span> immediately after waking up, as it instantly makes the bedroom look neater and more inviting. ️</div><div class="ex-item">3) After dinner, the kids must help <span class="vt-word">clear the table<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clear the table</b><br>убирать со стола</span></span> <span class="prep">of</span> plates and glasses so adults can relax and enjoy coffee. 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item">4) Before guests arrive, I always <span class="vt-word">set the table<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>set the table</b><br>накрывать на стол</span></span> carefully with matching napkins and cutlery to create a welcoming atmosphere. 🕯️</div><div class="ex-item">5) Someone needs to <span class="vt-word">take out the trash<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>take out the trash</b><br>выносить мусор</span></span> today, otherwise it will start to smell and attract flies into the kitchen. 🗑️</div><div class="ex-item">6) Let's quickly <span class="vt-word">put things in order<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>put things in order</b><br>привести всё в порядок</span></span> <span class="prep">before</span> the inspection starts, or we might get fined for a messy workspace. 📋</div><div class="ex-item">7) If everyone helps, we can easily <span class="vt-word">keep things tidy<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>keep things tidy</b><br>поддерживать порядок</span></span> without spending hours on deep cleaning every weekend. ✨</div><div class="ex-item">8) Thanks for offering to <span class="vt-word">lend a hand<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>lend a hand</b><br>помочь, оказать поддержку</span></span> with the heavy lifting — it would have taken me twice as long alone. 🤝</div><div class="ex-item">9) When the project gets overwhelming, it helps when everyone <span class="vt-word">pitches in<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>pitch in</b><br>внести свой вклад, подключиться</span></span> <span class="prep">with</span> whatever task they can handle best. 🧩</div><div class="ex-item">10) I prefer to <span class="vt-word">do the ironing<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>do the ironing</b><br>гладить</span></span> in the evening while watching TV, because it's a repetitive chore that doesn't require much mental effort. 📺</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4> Practice Text: Teamwork at Home 🤝</h4><div class="practice-text">A clean home needs teamwork. Every morning, I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">make the bed 🛏️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 заправлять кровать</span></span>. After breakfast, my family helps <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clear the table 🍽️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать со стола</span></span>. I usually <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">do the laundry 👕</span><span class="tooltip">📌 стирать</span></span> on Saturdays, while others <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">take out the trash 🗑️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 выносить мусор</span></span>. When cooking, we all <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">set the table ️</span><span class="tooltip"> накрывать на стол</span></span> nicely. If it gets messy, we <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">put things in order </span><span class="tooltip"> привести в порядок</span></span> quickly. We try to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">keep things tidy ✨</span><span class="tooltip">📌 поддерживать порядок</span></span> daily. If someone needs help, we gladly <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">lend a hand 🤝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 помочь</span></span>. Everyone <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">pitches in 🧩</span><span class="tooltip">📌 подключиться</span></span>, and in the evening, I finally <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">do the ironing </span><span class="tooltip">📌 гладить</span></span>. Teamwork makes the dream work!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#7c3aed;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title"> Test: Expressions</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">It's time to ___ because the hamper is overflowing.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q1" id="hw_ex_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) do the laundry</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q1" id="hw_ex_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) make the bed</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q1" id="hw_ex_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) set the table</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Do the laundry</b> = стирать, заниматься стиркой.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ make the bed = заправлять кровать. set the table = накрывать на стол.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">Please ___ immediately after waking up to make the bedroom look neater.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q2" id="hw_ex_q2a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) make the bed</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q2" id="hw_ex_q2b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) clear the table</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q2" id="hw_ex_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) take out the trash</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Make the bed</b> = заправлять кровать.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ clear the table = убирать со стола. take out the trash = выносить мусор.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">After dinner, the kids must help ___ of plates and glasses.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q3" id="hw_ex_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) clear the table</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q3" id="hw_ex_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) put things in order</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q3" id="hw_ex_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) keep things tidy</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clear the table</b> = убирать со стола.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ put things in order = привести в порядок. keep things tidy = поддерживать порядок.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">Before guests arrive, I always ___ carefully with matching napkins.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q4" id="hw_ex_q4a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) set the table</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q4" id="hw_ex_q4b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) lend a hand</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q4" id="hw_ex_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) pitch in</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Set the table</b> = накрывать на стол.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ lend a hand = помочь. pitch in = подключиться.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">Someone needs to ___ today, otherwise it will start to smell.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q5" id="hw_ex_q5a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) take out the trash</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q5" id="hw_ex_q5b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) do the ironing</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q5" id="hw_ex_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) make the bed</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Take out the trash</b> = выносить мусор.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ do the ironing = гладить. make the bed = заправлять кровать.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">Let's quickly ___ before the inspection starts.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q6" id="hw_ex_q6a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) put things in order</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q6" id="hw_ex_q6b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) keep things tidy</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q6" id="hw_ex_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) clear the table</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Put things in order</b> = привести всё в порядок.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ keep things tidy = поддерживать порядок. clear the table = убирать со стола.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">If everyone helps, we can easily ___ without spending hours on deep cleaning.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q7" id="hw_ex_q7a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) keep things tidy</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q7" id="hw_ex_q7b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) lend a hand</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q7" id="hw_ex_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) pitch in</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Keep things tidy</b> = поддерживать порядок.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ lend a hand = помочь. pitch in = подключиться.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">Thanks for offering to ___ with the heavy lifting.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q8" id="hw_ex_q8a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) lend a hand</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q8" id="hw_ex_q8b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) pitch in</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q8" id="hw_ex_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) do the ironing</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Lend a hand</b> = помочь, оказать поддержку.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ pitch in = внести вклад. do the ironing = гладить.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">When the project gets overwhelming, it helps when everyone ___ with whatever task they can handle.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q9" id="hw_ex_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) pitches in</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q9" id="hw_ex_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) keeps things tidy</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q9" id="hw_ex_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) puts things in order</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Pitch in</b> = внести свой вклад, подключиться.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ keep things tidy = поддерживать порядок. put things in order = привести в порядок.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">I prefer to ___ in the evening while watching TV.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q10" id="hw_ex_q10a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ex_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) do the ironing</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q10" id="hw_ex_q10b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) set the table</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ex_q10" id="hw_ex_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ex_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) clear the table</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Do the ironing</b> = гладить.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ set the table = накрывать на стол. clear the table = убирать со стола.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

class="hw-btn" style="background:#ec4899;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) I <span class="vt-word">tend to<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tend to do</b><br>иметь тенденцию делать что-то</span></span> clean the kitchen first, because it's the busiest room in my house. 🍳</div><div class="ex-item">2) She <span class="vt-word">managed to<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>manage to do</b><br>суметь, успеть сделать</span></span> finish all chores before noon by working fast. ⏰</div><div class="ex-item">3) Try to <span class="vt-word">avoid<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>avoid doing</b><br>избегать делать что-то</span></span> leaving dirty dishes overnight, or they become harder to clean. 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item">4) I'm <span class="vt-word">considering<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>consider doing</b><br>обдумывать, рассматривать возможность</span></span> buying a robot vacuum to save time on cleaning floors. 🤖</div><div class="ex-item">5) We <span class="vt-word">plan on<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>plan on doing</b><br>планировать сделать</span></span> doing a deep clean this weekend to prepare for guests. 🧹</div><div class="ex-item">6) I <span class="vt-word">intend to<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>intend to do</b><br>намереваться сделать</span></span> organize my closet this Saturday so I can find my clothes easily. 👗</div><div class="ex-item">7) I <span class="vt-word">hope to<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>hope to do</b><br>надеяться сделать</span></span> finish cleaning before my friends arrive for dinner. 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item">8) I <span class="vt-word">promise to<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>promise to do</b><br>обещать сделать</span></span> take out the trash tonight so the kitchen smells fresh. 🗑️</div><div class="ex-item">9) I <span class="vt-word">decided to<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>decide to do</b><br>решить сделать</span></span> start cleaning earlier in the day to have more free time later. ☀️</div><div class="ex-item">10) I <span class="vt-word">suggest<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>suggest doing</b><br>предлагать сделать</span></span> cleaning the bathroom together — it goes faster with two people! 🛁</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Smart Cleaning 🧭</h4><div class="practice-text">I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tend to 🍳</span><span class="tooltip">📌 иметь тенденцию</span></span> clean the kitchen first. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">managed to ⏰</span><span class="tooltip">📌 суметь</span></span> finish before lunch. I try to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">avoid 🍽️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 избегать</span></span> leaving dirty dishes. I'm <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">considering 🤖</span><span class="tooltip">📌 обдумывать</span></span> buying a robot vacuum. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">plan on 🧹</span><span class="tooltip">📌 планировать</span></span> deep cleaning this weekend. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">intend to 👗</span><span class="tooltip">📌 намереваться</span></span> organize my closet. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">hope to 🍽️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 надеяться</span></span> finish before guests arrive. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">promise to 🗑️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 обещать</span></span> take out the trash. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">decided to ☀️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 решить</span></span> start cleaning earlier. I <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">suggest 🛁</span><span class="tooltip">📌 предлагать</span></span> cleaning together. Smart habits make cleaning easier!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#db2777;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Patterns</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">I tend ___ the kitchen first because it's the busiest room.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q1" id="hw_pat_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pat_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) to clean</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q1" id="hw_pat_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) cleaning</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q1" id="hw_pat_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) clean</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>tend to + V</b> = иметь тенденцию.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ tend + V-ing неверно. tend + V (без to) неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">She managed ___ all chores before noon.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q2" id="hw_pat_q2a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) finishing</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q2" id="hw_pat_q2b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pat_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) to finish</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q2" id="hw_pat_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) finish</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>manage to + V</b> = суметь сделать.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ manage + V-ing неверно. manage + V (без to) неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">Try to avoid ___ dirty dishes overnight.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q3" id="hw_pat_q3a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) to leave</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q3" id="hw_pat_q3b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pat_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) leaving</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q3" id="hw_pat_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) leave</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>avoid + V-ing</b> = избегать действия.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ avoid + to V неверно. avoid + V (без ing) неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">I'm considering ___ a robot vacuum to save time.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q4" id="hw_pat_q4a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) to buy</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q4" id="hw_pat_q4b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pat_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) buying</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q4" id="hw_pat_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) buy</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>consider + V-ing</b> = обдумывать действие.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ consider + to V неверно. consider + V (без ing) неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">We plan on ___ a deep clean this weekend.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q5" id="hw_pat_q5a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) to do</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q5" id="hw_pat_q5b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pat_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) doing</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q5" id="hw_pat_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) do</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>plan on + V-ing</b> = планировать.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ plan on + to V неверно. plan on + V (без ing) неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">I intend ___ my closet this Saturday.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q6" id="hw_pat_q6a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pat_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) to organize</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q6" id="hw_pat_q6b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) organizing</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q6" id="hw_pat_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) organize</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>intend to + V</b> = намереваться.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ intend + V-ing неверно. intend + V (без to) неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">I hope ___ cleaning before guests arrive.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q7" id="hw_pat_q7a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) finishing</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q7" id="hw_pat_q7b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pat_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) to finish</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q7" id="hw_pat_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) finish</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>hope to + V</b> = надеяться.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ hope + V-ing неверно. hope + V (без to) неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">I promise ___ the trash tonight.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q8" id="hw_pat_q8a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) taking out</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q8" id="hw_pat_q8b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_pat_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) to take out</label><input type="radio" name="hw_pat_q8" id="hw_pat_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_pat_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) take out</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! class="hw-btn" style="background:#eab308;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) Store your tools <span class="vt-word">tidily<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidily</b><br>аккуратно, опрятно</span></span> in labeled boxes so you can find exactly what you need without rummaging through messy drawers. 📦</div><div class="ex-item">2) The stain wasn't removed <span class="vt-word">adequately<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>adequately</b><br>достаточно, надлежащим образом</span></span> because we used the wrong type of detergent for that specific fabric. 🧴</div><div class="ex-item">3) If you assemble the shelf <span class="vt-word">improperly<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>improperly</b><br>неправильно, ненадлежащим образом</span></span>, it might collapse under weight and damage your floor or walls. 🛠️</div><div class="ex-item">4) The guests behaved <span class="vt-word">disrespectfully<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>disrespectfully</b><br>неуважительно</span></span> by leaving muddy shoes on the clean hallway rug without wiping them first. 👞</div><div class="ex-item">5) It's <span class="vt-word">unfair<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unfairly</b><br>несправедливо</span></span> to expect one person to handle all the cleaning after a large party when everyone helped cook the food. ⚖️</div><div class="ex-item">6) You spelled the cleaning product names <span class="vt-word">incorrectly<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>incorrectly</b><br>неправильно</span></span> on the shopping list, so I accidentally bought floor polish instead of glass cleaner. 📝</div><div class="ex-item">7) Complaining <span class="vt-word">unreasonably<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unreasonably</b><br>необоснованно</span></span> about minor dust won't solve anything — it's better to just grab a cloth and wipe it down quickly. 🌪️</div><div class="ex-item">8) He <span class="vt-word">misguidedly<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>misguidedly</b><br>ошибочно, по неверному совету</span></span> mixed two different cleaning chemicals, creating a dangerous gas that required opening all windows immediately. ☣️</div><div class="ex-item">9) The plumber <span class="vt-word">unexpectedly<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>unexpectedly</b><br>неожиданно</span></span> arrived early for the repair, which was actually convenient because we were home all day. 🔧</div><div class="ex-item">10) The heavy sofa was placed <span class="vt-word">inconveniently<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>inconveniently</b><br>неудобно</span></span> in front of the window, blocking natural light and making the room feel cramped and dark. 🛋️</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Mindful Habits ⚡</h4><div class="practice-text">To maintain a good home, organize things <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidily 📦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 аккуратно</span></span>. Don't let mess grow because it won't resolve <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">adequately 🧴</span><span class="tooltip">📌 должным образом</span></span> on its own. If assembled <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">improperly 🛠️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неправильно</span></span>, things break. Never act <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">disrespectfully 👞</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неуважительно</span></span> toward shared spaces. It's <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unfairly ⚖️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 несправедливо</span></span> to dump work on one person. Label items <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">incorrectly 📝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неправильно</span></span> and you waste time. Don't complain <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unreasonably 🌪️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 необоснованно</span></span>. <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">Misguidedly ☣️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 ошибочно</span></span> mixing chemicals is dangerous. If help arrives <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unexpectedly 🔧</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неожиданно</span></span>, welcome it. Place furniture <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">inconveniently 🛋️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неудобно</span></span> and you'll regret it. Small mindful choices make big differences!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#ca8a04;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Adverbs</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">She arrived ___ for the interview and made a great first impression.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q1" id="hw_adv_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) punctually</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q1" id="hw_adv_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) unpunctually</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q1" id="hw_adv_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) inconsistently</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Punctually</b> = пунктуально, вовремя.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ unpunctually = непунктуально. inconsistently = непоследовательно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">The team worked ___ and finished the project two days early.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q2" id="hw_adv_q2a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) inefficiently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q2" id="hw_adv_q2b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) efficiently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q2" id="hw_adv_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) unnecessarily</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Efficiently</b> = эффективно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ inefficiently = неэффективно. unnecessarily = без необходимости.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">He exercises ___, sometimes daily, sometimes not at all.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q3" id="hw_adv_q3a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) regularly</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q3" id="hw_adv_q3b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) irregularly</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q3" id="hw_adv_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) patiently</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Irregularly</b> = нерегулярно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ regularly = регулярно. patiently = терпеливо.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">She submits her work ___, which makes planning difficult.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q4" id="hw_adv_q4a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) inconsistently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q4" id="hw_adv_q4b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) consistently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q4" id="hw_adv_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) punctually</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inconsistently</b> = непоследовательно, нестабильно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ consistently = последовательно. punctually = пунктуально.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">You're ___ complicating a simple task; just follow the steps.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q5" id="hw_adv_q5a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) necessarily</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q5" id="hw_adv_q5b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) unnecessarily</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q5" id="hw_adv_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) appropriately</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unnecessarily</b> = без необходимости, излишне.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ necessarily = обязательно. appropriately = уместно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">The child waited ___ for his turn, without complaining.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q6" id="hw_adv_q6a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) patiently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q6" id="hw_adv_q6b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) impatiently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q6" id="hw_adv_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) inadvertently</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Patiently</b> = терпеливо.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ impatiently = нетерпеливо. inadvertently = нечаянно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">He ___ deleted the important file; it was a mistake.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q7" id="hw_adv_q7a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) appropriately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q7" id="hw_adv_q7b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) inadvertently</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q7" id="hw_adv_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) responsibly</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inadvertently</b> = нечаянно, по неосторожности.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ appropriately = уместно. responsibly = ответственно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">___, the train was cancelled, so we missed the meeting.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q8" id="hw_adv_q8a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) fortunately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q8" id="hw_adv_q8b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) unfortunately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q8" id="hw_adv_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) necessarily</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unfortunately</b> = к сожалению.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ fortunately = к счастью. necessarily = обязательно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">It's ___ to wear shorts to a formal business meeting.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q9" id="hw_adv_q9a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) appropriately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q9" id="hw_adv_q9b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) inappropriately</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q9" id="hw_adv_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) punctually</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inappropriately</b> = неуместно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ appropriately = уместно. punctually = пунктуально.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">Leaving the door unlocked was ___ and put everyone at risk.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q10" id="hw_adv_q10a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) responsibly</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q10" id="hw_adv_q10b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adv_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) irresponsibly</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adv_q10" id="hw_adv_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adv_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) efficiently</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Irresponsibly</b> = безответственно.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ responsibly = ответственно. efficiently = эффективно.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Clean Room 📝</h4><div class="practice-text">A <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy 🌟</span><span class="tooltip">📌 опрятный</span></span> space feels much better. After cleaning, it's completely <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean 🍋</span><span class="tooltip">📌 чистый</span></span>. An <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">incomplete ⏳</span><span class="tooltip">📌 незавершённый</span></span> job feels frustrating. If the floor is <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unswept 🍂</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неподметённый</span></span>, it looks neglected. A <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">disorderly 📄</span><span class="tooltip">📌 неорганизованный</span></span> room wastes time searching. Nothing is <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">perfect ✅</span><span class="tooltip">📌 идеальный</span></span>, but effort matters. Keeping chemicals out of reach is <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">safe ⚠️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 безопасный</span></span>. Don't let supplies become <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">insufficient 📦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 недостаточный</span></span>. If you find a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">misplaced 🔍</span><span class="tooltip">📌 потерянный</span></span> item, return it. Never touch <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">unwashed 🥦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 невымытый</span></span> food without washing hands first!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#0e7490;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Adjectives</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">She's always ___; she arrives 10 minutes early to every meeting.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q1" id="hw_adj_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) punctual</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q1" id="hw_adj_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) unpunctual</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q1" id="hw_adj_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) unrealistic</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Punctual</b> = пунктуальный, вовремя.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ unpunctual = непунктуальный. unrealistic = нереалистичный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">His reply was ___; we needed an answer yesterday.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q2" id="hw_adj_q2a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) timely</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q2" id="hw_adj_q2b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) untimely</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q2" id="hw_adj_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) productive</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Untimely</b> = несвоевременный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ timely = своевременный. productive = продуктивный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">With a clear plan, your study sessions become much ___.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q3" id="hw_adj_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) productive</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q3" id="hw_adj_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) unproductive</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q3" id="hw_adj_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) unreliable</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Productive</b> = продуктивный, результативный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ unproductive = непродуктивный. unreliable = ненадёжный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">The old system was ___; it took twice as long to complete tasks.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q4" id="hw_adj_q4a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) efficient</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q4" id="hw_adj_q4b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) inefficient</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q4" id="hw_adj_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) organized</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inefficient</b> = неэффективный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ efficient = эффективный. organized = организованный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">His desk is always ___; papers are everywhere.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q5" id="hw_adj_q5a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) disorganized</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q5" id="hw_adj_q5b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) organized</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q5" id="hw_adj_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) punctual</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Disorganized</b> = неорганизованный, в беспорядке.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ organized = организованный. punctual = пунктуальный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">Don't be ___; if you promise to call, do it.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q6" id="hw_adj_q6a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) reliable</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q6" id="hw_adj_q6b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) unreliable</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q6" id="hw_adj_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) flexible</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unreliable</b> = ненадёжный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ reliable = надёжный. flexible = гибкий.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">The schedule is ___; we can't change the meeting time.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q7" id="hw_adj_q7a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) flexible</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q7" id="hw_adj_q7b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) inflexible</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q7" id="hw_adj_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) patient</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Inflexible</b> = негибкий, неуступчивый.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ flexible = гибкий. patient = терпеливый.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">He gets angry when things take time; he's very ___.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q8" id="hw_adj_q8a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) patient</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q8" id="hw_adj_q8b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) impatient</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q8" id="hw_adj_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) realistic</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Impatient</b> = нетерпеливый.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ patient = терпеливый. realistic = реалистичный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">Expecting to learn a language in a week is ___.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q9" id="hw_adj_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) unrealistic</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q9" id="hw_adj_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) realistic</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q9" id="hw_adj_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) regular</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Unrealistic</b> = нереалистичный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ realistic = реалистичный. regular = регулярный.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">His attendance has been ___ lately; he misses every other class.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q10" id="hw_adj_q10a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) regular</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q10" id="hw_adj_q10b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_adj_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) irregular</label><input type="radio" name="hw_adj_q10" id="hw_adj_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_adj_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) productive</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Irregular</b> = нерегулярный.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ regular = регулярный. productive = продуктивный.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

mess</b><br>убрать беспорядок</span></span> <span class="prep">before</span> it stains the fabric or damages the wooden table underneath. ☕</div><div class="ex-item">5) It takes courage to <span class="vt-word">tackle clutter<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tackle clutter</b><br>разобрать завалы</span></span> <span class="prep">in</span> the attic or garage, but you'll be amazed at how much usable space you'll uncover. 📦</div><div class="ex-item">6) Always <span class="vt-word">sort laundry<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>sort laundry</b><br>сортировать бельё</span></span> by fabric weight and color temperature <span class="prep">before</span> starting the washing cycle. 👕</div><div class="ex-item">7) After drying, gently <span class="vt-word">fold clothes<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>fold clothes</b><br>складывать одежду</span></span> neatly to avoid deep wrinkles and make drawer organization much easier. 📏</div><div class="ex-item">8) Use a damp cloth to <span class="vt-word">wipe surfaces<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>wipe surfaces</b><br>протирать поверхности</span></span> <span class="prep">after</span> cooking, removing grease splatters and food crumbs that attract insects. 🍳</div><div class="ex-item">9) Apply baking soda paste and let it sit to <span class="vt-word">scrub stains<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>scrub stains</b><br>оттирать пятна</span></span> <span 🧸</div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Mastering Chores 🌟</h4><div class="practice-text">Living well means learning to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">share responsibilities 🤝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 распределять обязанности</span></span>. Everyone should know how to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">keep house 🏡</span><span class="tooltip">📌 вести хозяйство</span></span> and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">maintain cleanliness 🧼</span><span class="tooltip">📌 поддерживать чистоту</span></span>. When accidents happen, quickly <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clear the mess ☕</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убрать беспорядок</span></span>. Don't fear to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tackle clutter 📦</span><span class="tooltip">📌 разобрать завалы</span></span>. Always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">sort laundry 👕</span><span class="tooltip">📌 сортировать бельё</span></span> and <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">fold clothes 📏</span><span class="tooltip">📌 складывать одежду</span></span> carefully. After meals, <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe surfaces 🍳</span><span class="tooltip">📌 протирать поверхности</span></span>. For tough spots, gently <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">scrub stains 🧽</span><span class="tooltip">📌 оттирать пятна</span></span>. Finally, teach kids to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">put things away 🧸</span><span class="tooltip">📌 убирать вещи</span></span>.
