<b>Share responsibilities</b> = распределять обязанности.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ share responsibility = единственное число. share the responsibilities = лишний артикль.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">Learning how to ___ effectively takes practice.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q2" id="hw_col_q2a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) keep house</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q2" id="hw_col_q2b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) keep the house</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q2" id="hw_col_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) keep a house</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Keep house</b> = вести хозяйство.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ keep the house = содержать дом. keep a house = иметь дом.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">Regular vacuuming helps ___ in high-traffic areas.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q3" id="hw_col_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) maintain cleanliness</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q3" id="hw_col_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) maintain the cleanliness</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q3" id="hw_col_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) maintain a cleanliness</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Maintain cleanliness</b> = поддерживать чистоту.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ maintain the cleanliness = лишний артикль. maintain a cleanliness = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">If you spill coffee, immediately ___ before it stains.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q4" id="hw_col_q4a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) clear the mess</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q4" id="hw_col_q4b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) clear mess</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q4" id="hw_col_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) clear a mess</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clear the mess</b> = убрать беспорядок.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ clear mess = пропущен артикль. clear a mess = неопределённый артикль.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">It takes courage to ___ in the attic or garage.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q5" id="hw_col_q5a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_col_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) tackle clutter</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q5" id="hw_col_q5b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) tackle the clutter</label><input type="radio" name="hw_col_q5" id="hw_col_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_col_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) tackle a clutter</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Put things away</b> = убирать вещи на место.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ put away things = неверный порядок. put the things away = лишний артикль.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

translateY(5px)}.blur-wrapper:hover .tooltip{transform:translateX(0) translateY(0)}}</style><div class="hw-wrap"><div class="hw-box"><h2 class="hw-title">🧹 Housework & Cleaning: 🎭 Idioms</h2><button class="hw-btn" style="background:#6366f1;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><div class="ex-card"><h4>Examples & Practice 💬</h4><div class="ex-item">1) "A place for everything and everything in its place." — Keeping items organized saves time and reduces daily stress when looking for keys or documents. 📍<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Старая поговорка, подчёркивающая важность порядка и системности. Каждой вещи должно быть своё место, что упрощает жизнь и поиск предметов.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">2) "I want to start with a <span class="vt-word">clean slate<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clean slate</b><br>чистый лист, начать заново</span></span> this year." — I'm decluttering my home and adopting new habits to feel refreshed and motivated. 📜<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Из школьных времён: ученики стирали грифель с доски ("slate"), чтобы начать урок заново. Означает начать что-то с нуля, без старых проблем.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">3) "Scrubbing this floor takes serious <span class="vt-word">elbow grease<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>elbow grease</b><br>физические усилия, старание</span></span>." — No modern machine replaces manual effort for deeply embedded dirt. 💪<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Идиома 18 века: "elbow grease" означает интенсивную физическую работу руками (трение, оттирание). Подчёркивает, что некоторые задачи требуют усилий.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">4) "He needs to <span class="vt-word">tidy up his act<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>tidy up one's act</b><br>взяться за ум, исправить поведение</span></span> before he loses his job." — Chronic lateness is unacceptable in a professional environment. 🛠️<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Переносное значение: навести порядок не только в вещах, но и в поведении/привычках. Используется при серьёзных предупреждениях.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">5) "When guests arrive unexpectedly, everyone <span class="vt-word">pitches in<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>pitch in</b><br>подключиться, помочь</span></span> to prepare food and arrange seating." — Teamwork makes emergencies manageable. 🤝<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Означать объединять усилия для общего дела. Часто используется в контексте уборки, готовки или решения внезапных задач.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">6) "If you <span class="vt-word">pull your weight<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>pull one's weight</b><br>делать свою долю работы</span></span>, the household runs smoothly without anyone feeling overworked." ⚖️<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Из морского сленга: тянуть свой вес на веслах. Означает честно выполнять свою часть работы в коллективе или семье.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">7) "We decided to <span class="vt-word">set the house in order<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>set the house in order</b><br>привести дела в порядок</span></span> after the renovation was finally complete." — Organizing paperwork, schedules, and storage systems. 🏠<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Означает систематизировать дела, финансы или физическое пространство. Часто используется перед важными событиями или этапами жизни.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">8) "After the argument, they decided to <span class="vt-word">clean up their act<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>clean up one's act</b><br>исправить поведение</span></span> and communicate respectfully moving forward." 🕊️<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Аналогично "tidy up one's act": кардинально изменить поведение, стать более ответственным и дисциплинированным.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">9) "The new manager did a <span class="vt-word">clean sweep<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>do a clean sweep</b><br>полностью очистить, изменить всё к лучшему</span></span> of outdated policies." — Efficiency improved dramatically. 🌬️<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Полное устранение старого/плохого. В уборке — вымести всё до чистоты. В жизни/работе — радикальные позитивные перемены.</div></div></div><div class="ex-item">10) "Apologize sincerely and <span class="vt-word">wipe the slate clean<span class="vt-tooltip"><b>wipe the slate clean</b><br>забыть прошлое, начать заново</span></span> so we can move forward as friends." 🤝<div class="accordion"><div class="accordion-header" onclick="this.parentElement.classList.toggle('active')">📖 Show origin/meaning</div><div class="accordion-content">Означает простить обиды, закрыть старые конфликты и дать отношениям или проекту новый старт без груза прошлого.</div></div></div></div><div class="practice-box"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Life Lessons 📖</h4><div class="practice-text">Believe in "a place for everything and everything in its place 📍". Start each project with a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean slate 📜</span><span class="tooltip">📌 чистый лист</span></span>. Hard work requires real <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">elbow grease 💪</span><span class="tooltip">📌 физические усилия</span></span>. If you make mistakes, it's time to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tidy up your act 🛠️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 взяться за ум</span></span>. When others struggle, always <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">pitch in 🤝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 помочь</span></span>. Make sure you <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">pull your weight ⚖️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 делать свою долю</span></span>. Before big changes, <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">set the house in order 🏠</span><span class="tooltip">📌 привести дела в порядок</span></span>. Fix bad habits to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean up your act 🕊️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 исправить поведение</span></span>. Leaders often do a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">clean sweep 🌬️</span><span class="tooltip">📌 полное обновление</span></span>. Finally, learn to <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wipe the slate clean 🤝</span><span class="tooltip">📌 забыть прошлое</span></span>. Growth comes from order and forgiveness!</div></div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#4f46e5;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: Idioms</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">Believe in "___" — keeping items organized saves time.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q1" id="hw_idiom_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) a place for everything and everything in its place</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q1" id="hw_idiom_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) a place for everything</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q1" id="hw_idiom_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) everything in its place</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>A place for everything and everything in its place</b> = каждой вещи своё место.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ неполная версия пословицы.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">I want to start with a ___ this year and adopt new habits.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q2" id="hw_idiom_q2a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) clean slate</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q2" id="hw_idiom_q2b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) clean start</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q2" id="hw_idiom_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) fresh slate</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clean slate</b> = чистый лист, начать заново.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ clean start = не идиома. fresh slate = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">Scrubbing this floor takes serious ___ — no machine can replace manual effort.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q3" id="hw_idiom_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) elbow grease</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q3" id="hw_idiom_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) hard work</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q3" id="hw_idiom_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) effort</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Elbow grease</b> = физические усилия, старание.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ hard work = не идиома. effort = усилие.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">4</div><div class="qz-sent">He needs to ___ before he loses his job — chronic lateness is unacceptable.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q4" id="hw_idiom_q4a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q4a" class="qz-opt">A) tidy up his act</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q4" id="hw_idiom_q4b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q4b" class="qz-opt">B) clean up his act</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q4" id="hw_idiom_q4c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q4c" class="qz-opt">C) fix his act</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Tidy up one's act</b> = взяться за ум, исправить поведение.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ clean up his act = другая идиома. fix his act = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">5</div><div class="qz-sent">When guests arrive unexpectedly, everyone ___ to prepare food and arrange seating.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q5" id="hw_idiom_q5a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q5a" class="qz-opt">A) pitches in</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q5" id="hw_idiom_q5b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q5b" class="qz-opt">B) helps out</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q5" id="hw_idiom_q5c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q5c" class="qz-opt">C) joins in</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Pitch in</b> = подключиться, помочь.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ helps out = помогать. joins in = присоединиться.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">6</div><div class="qz-sent">If you ___, the household runs smoothly without anyone feeling overworked.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q6" id="hw_idiom_q6a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q6a" class="qz-opt">A) pull your weight</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q6" id="hw_idiom_q6b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q6b" class="qz-opt">B) do your part</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q6" id="hw_idiom_q6c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q6c" class="qz-opt">C) do your share</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Pull one's weight</b> = делать свою долю работы.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ do your part = делать свою часть. do your share = делать свою долю.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">We decided to ___ after the renovation was finally complete.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q7" id="hw_idiom_q7a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) set the house in order</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q7" id="hw_idiom_q7b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) organize the house</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q7" id="hw_idiom_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) put the house in order</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Set the house in order</b> = привести дела в порядок.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ organize the house = организовать дом. put the house in order = неверно.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">8</div><div class="qz-sent">After the argument, they decided to ___ and communicate respectfully.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q8" id="hw_idiom_q8a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q8a" class="qz-opt">A) clean up their act</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q8" id="hw_idiom_q8b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q8b" class="qz-opt">B) tidy up their act</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q8" id="hw_idiom_q8c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q8c" class="qz-opt">C) fix their act</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clean up one's act</b> = исправить поведение.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ tidy up their act = навести порядок. fix their act = исправить.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">9</div><div class="qz-sent">The new manager did a ___ of outdated policies — efficiency improved dramatically.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q9" id="hw_idiom_q9a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q9a" class="qz-opt">A) clean sweep</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q9" id="hw_idiom_q9b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q9b" class="qz-opt">B) full sweep</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q9" id="hw_idiom_q9c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q9c" class="qz-opt">C) complete sweep</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Do a clean sweep</b> = полностью очистить, изменить всё к лучшему.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ full sweep = полная уборка. complete sweep = завершённая уборка.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">10</div><div class="qz-sent">Apologize sincerely and ___ so we can move forward as friends.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q10" id="hw_idiom_q10a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_idiom_q10a" class="qz-opt">A) wipe the slate clean</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q10" id="hw_idiom_q10b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q10b" class="qz-opt">B) clean the slate</label><input type="radio" name="hw_idiom_q10" id="hw_idiom_q10c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_idiom_q10c" class="qz-opt">C) wipe the slate</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! <b>Wipe the slate clean</b> = забыть прошлое, начать заново.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ clean the slate = очистить доску. wipe the slate = стереть доску.</div></div></div></div></div></div>

translateY(5px)}.blur-wrapper:hover .tooltip{transform:translateX(0) translateY(0)}}</style><div class="hw-wrap"><div class="hw-box"><h2 class="hw-title">🧹 Housework & Cleaning: 🇬🇧🇺 British vs American</h2><button class="hw-btn" style="background:#f43f5e;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3>Key Vocabulary Differences</h3><table class="ba-table"><tr><th>🇬🇧 British English</th><th>🇺 American English</th><th>🇷🇺 Translation</th></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">flat</td><td class="ba-american">apartment</td><td>квартира</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">lift</td><td class="ba-american">elevator</td><td>лифт</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">rubbish</td><td class="ba-american">trash</td><td>мусор</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">dustbin</td><td class="ba-american">garbage can</td><td>мусорный бак</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">washing-up liquid</td><td class="ba-american">dish soap</td><td>средство для мытья посуды</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">tea towel</td><td class="ba-american">dish towel</td><td>кухонное полотенце</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">hoover</td><td class="ba-american">vacuum</td><td>пылесос</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">wardrobe</td><td class="ba-american">closet</td><td>шкаф (для одежды)</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">chest of drawers</td><td class="ba-american">dresser</td><td>комод</td></tr><tr><td class="ba-british">bin liner</td><td class="ba-american">trash bag</td><td>мусорный пакет</td></tr></table><div class="ba-practice"><div class="ba-practice-box british"><div class="ba-practice-title">🇬🇧 British English Practice</div><p style="margin:0;line-height:1.8;color:#495057;font-size:15px">After moving into the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">flat</span><span class="tooltip">📌 квартира</span></span>, I use the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">lift</span><span class="tooltip">📌 лифт</span></span> daily. I take the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">rubbish</span><span class="tooltip">📌 мусор</span></span> out and place it in the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dustbin</span><span class="tooltip">📌 мусорный бак</span></span>. I always keep <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">washing-up liquid</span><span class="tooltip">📌 средство для посуды</span></span> and a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">tea towel</span><span class="tooltip">📌 полотенце</span></span> by the sink. I run the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">hoover</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пылесос</span></span> weekly. My clothes go into the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">wardrobe</span><span class="tooltip">📌 шкаф</span></span>, socks in the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">chest of drawers</span><span class="tooltip">📌 комод</span></span>. I change the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">bin liner</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пакет</span></span> daily.</p></div><div class="ba-practice-box american"><div class="ba-practice-title">🇺🇸 American English Practice</div><p style="margin:0;line-height:1.8;color:#495057;font-size:15px">After moving into the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">apartment</span><span class="tooltip">📌 квартира</span></span>, I use the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">elevator</span><span class="tooltip">📌 лифт</span></span> daily. I take the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">trash</span><span class="tooltip">📌 мусор</span></span> out and place it in the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">garbage can</span><span class="tooltip">📌 мусорный бак</span></span>. I always keep <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dish soap</span><span class="tooltip">📌 средство для посуды</span></span> and a <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dish towel</span><span class="tooltip">📌 полотенце</span></span> by the sink. I run the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">vacuum</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пылесос</span></span> weekly. My clothes go into the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">closet</span><span class="tooltip">📌 шкаф</span></span>, socks in the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">dresser</span><span class="tooltip">📌 комод</span></span>. I change the <span class="blur-wrapper" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="blur-text">trash bag</span><span class="tooltip">📌 пакет</span></span> daily.</p></div></div><div class="ba-note">💡 <b>Запомните:</b> Британцы чаще говорят <b>"hoover"</b> (от бренда) для пылесоса, а американцы — <b>"vacuum"</b>. Для мусора британцы используют <b>"rubbish"</b>, а американцы — <b>"trash"</b> или <b>"garbage"</b>. Бытовые мелочи различаются, но оба варианта полностью взаимопонятны.</div></div><button class="hw-btn test" style="background:#e11d48;color:#fff" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test</button><div class="hw-panel"><h3 class="qz-title">🧪 Test: British vs American</h3><p class="qz-sub">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">1</div><div class="qz-sent">🇬🇧 I live in a small ___ on the third floor.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q1" id="hw_ba_q1a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q1a" class="qz-opt">A) flat</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q1" id="hw_ba_q1b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q1b" class="qz-opt">B) apartment</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q1" id="hw_ba_q1c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q1c" class="qz-opt">C) house</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В UK квартира = <b>flat</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ apartment = US вариант. house = дом.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">2</div><div class="qz-sent">🇺 I live in a small ___ on the third floor.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q2" id="hw_ba_q2a" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q2a" class="qz-opt">A) flat</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q2" id="hw_ba_q2b" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q2b" class="qz-opt">B) apartment</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q2" id="hw_ba_q2c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q2c" class="qz-opt">C) house</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В US квартира = <b>apartment</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ flat = UK вариант. house = дом.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">3</div><div class="qz-sent">🇬🇧 Take the ___ to the fifth floor, please.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q3" id="hw_ba_q3a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q3a" class="qz-opt">A) lift</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q3" id="hw_ba_q3b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q3b" class="qz-opt">B) elevator</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q3" id="hw_ba_q3c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q3c" class="qz-opt">C) stairs</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! В US мусор = <b>trash</b>.</div><div class="qz-fb no">❌ rubbish = UK вариант. litter = мусор на улице.</div></div><div class="qz-card"><div class="qz-num">7</div><div class="qz-sent">🇬🇧 I need to buy some ___ for washing dishes.</div><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q7" id="hw_ba_q7a" class="qz-r corr"><label for="hw_ba_q7a" class="qz-opt">A) washing-up liquid</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q7" id="hw_ba_q7b" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q7b" class="qz-opt">B) dish soap</label><input type="radio" name="hw_ba_q7" id="hw_ba_q7c" class="qz-r wrng"><label for="hw_ba_q7c" class="qz-opt">C) soap</label><div class="qz-fb ok">✅ Correct! class="dr-title-nouns">Nouns</h2><button class="dr-btn-nouns learn" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn Constructions</button><div class="dr-panel-nouns"><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ mop vs broom</strong><div class="diff"><b>mop</b> — швабра (для влажной уборки пола)<br><b>broom</b> — веник/щётка (для сухой уборки)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>I need a <b>mop</b> to clean the kitchen floor. 🧹</li><li>Use the <b>broom</b> to sweep the dust. 🧹</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ detergent vs soap</strong><div class="diff"><b>detergent</b> — моющее средство (порошок для стирки, жидкость для посуды)<br><b>soap</b> — мыло (для рук, тела)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>Add <b>detergent</b> to the washing machine. 🧺</li><li>Wash your hands with <b>soap</b>. 🧼</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ stain vs spot vs mark</strong><div class="diff"><b>stain</b> — трудновыводимое пятно (от вина, кофе, жира)<br><b>spot</b> — маленькое пятно/крапинка (лёгкое)<br><b>mark</b> — след/отметка (от карандаша, руки)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>There's a coffee <b>stain</b> on the carpet. ☕</li><li>I see a small <b>spot</b> on your shirt. 👕</li><li>Don't leave <b>marks</b> on the wall! 🖍️</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ appliance vs device</strong><div class="diff"><b>appliance</b> — крупная бытовая техника (стиральная машина, холодильник, плита)<br><b>device</b> — устройство, прибор (пылесос, утюг, блендер)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>We bought a new kitchen <b>appliance</b> — a dishwasher. 🍽️</li><li>This <b>device</b> makes cleaning easier. 🔌</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ mess vs clutter vs chaos</strong><div class="diff"><b>mess</b> — беспорядок, грязь (общее состояние)<br><b>clutter</b> — захламлённость (много вещей разбросано)<br><b>chaos</b> — полный хаос (ничего не найти!)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>The kitchen is a <b>mess</b> after cooking. 🍳</li><li>There's too much <b>clutter</b> on the desk. 📚</li><li>My room is in complete <b>chaos</b>! 😱</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-nouns"><strong>⚠️ chore vs task</strong><div class="diff"><b>chore</b> — рутинная домашняя обязанность (часто неприятная)<br><b>task</b> — задача (может быть любой, не только домашней)<div class="examples-box-nouns"><span class="examples-label-nouns">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-nouns"><li>Washing dishes is my least favourite <b>chore</b>. 🍽️</li><li>I have an important <b>task</b> to complete at work. 💼</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="practice-box-nouns"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Cleaning Day 🧹</h4><div class="practice-text-nouns">Today is cleaning day! First, I need to sweep the floor with a <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">broom</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 веник</span></span>, then wash it with a <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">mop</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 швабра</span></span>. I'll add some <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">detergent</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 моющее средство</span></span> to the water. There's a terrible coffee <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">stain</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 трудновыводимое пятно</span></span> on the carpet that I need to remove. The living room is in a complete <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">chaos</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 полный хаос</span></span> — there's so much <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">clutter</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 захламлённость</span></span> everywhere! I need to organize everything. Washing the dishes is my least favourite <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">chore</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 обязанность</span></span>, but it's a necessary <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">task</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 задача</span></span>. I'll also need to use the vacuum cleaner — it's a very useful <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">device</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 устройство</span></span>. The washing machine is another important kitchen <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">appliance</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 бытовая техника</span></span>. After cleaning, the house won't be such a <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">mess</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-nouns">📌 беспорядок</span></span> anymore!</div></div></div><button class="dr-btn-nouns test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">✏️ Test: Nouns</button><div class="dr-panel-nouns"><h3 class="qz-title-nouns">Test: Nouns</h3><p class="qz-sub-nouns">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">1</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">I need a ___ to wash the kitchen floor.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q1" id="n_q1a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q1a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) mop</label><input type="radio" name="n_q1" id="n_q1b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q1b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) broom</label><input type="radio" name="n_q1" id="n_q1c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q1c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) vacuum</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Mop</b> = швабра (для влажной уборки пола).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Broom = веник (для сухой уборки). C) Vacuum = пылесос (не для мытья).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">2</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">Add some ___ to the washing machine.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q2" id="n_q2a" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q2a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) soap</label><input type="radio" name="n_q2" id="n_q2b" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q2b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) detergent</label><input type="radio" name="n_q2" id="n_q2c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q2c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) water</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Detergent</b> = моющее средство (порошок для стирки).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ A) Soap = мыло (для рук, не для стиральной машины). C) Water = вода (но нужно именно средство).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">3</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">There's a terrible coffee ___ on the carpet!</div><input type="radio" name="n_q3" id="n_q3a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q3a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) stain</label><input type="radio" name="n_q3" id="n_q3b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q3b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) spot</label><input type="radio" name="n_q3" id="n_q3c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q3c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) mark</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Stain</b> = трудновыводимое пятно (от кофе, вина).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Spot = маленькое лёгкое пятно. C) Mark = след/отметка (от карандаша).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">4</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">The washing machine is an important kitchen ___.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q4" id="n_q4a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q4a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) appliance</label><input type="radio" name="n_q4" id="n_q4b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q4b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) device</label><input type="radio" name="n_q4" id="n_q4c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q4c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) tool</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Appliance</b> = крупная бытовая техника (стиральная машина).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Device = мелкое устройство (пылесос, утюг). C) Chaos = полный хаос (сильнее clutter).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">7</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">Washing dishes is my least favourite ___.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q7" id="n_q7a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q7a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) chore</label><input type="radio" name="n_q7" id="n_q7b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q7b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) task</label><input type="radio" name="n_q7" id="n_q7c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q7c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) job</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Chore</b> = рутинная домашняя обязанность (часто неприятная).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Task = задача (может быть любой). C) Job = работа (профессиональная).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">8</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">I have an important ___ to complete at work.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q8" id="n_q8a" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q8a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) chore</label><input type="radio" name="n_q8" id="n_q8b" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q8b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) task</label><input type="radio" name="n_q8" id="n_q8c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q8c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) duty</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Task</b> = задача (может быть рабочей, не только домашней).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ A) Chore = домашняя обязанность. C) Duty = долг/обязанность (более формально).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">9</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">Use the ___ to sweep the dust from the floor.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q9" id="n_q9a" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q9a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) mop</label><input type="radio" name="n_q9" id="n_q9b" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q9b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) broom</label><input type="radio" name="n_q9" id="n_q9c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q9c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) cloth</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Broom</b> = веник/щётка (для сухой уборки, подметания).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ A) Mop = швабра (для влажной уборки). C) Cloth = тряпка (для протирания).</div></div><div class="qz-card-nouns"><div class="qz-num-nouns">10</div><div class="qz-sent-nouns">The kitchen is a ___ after cooking dinner.</div><input type="radio" name="n_q10" id="n_q10a" class="qz-r-nouns corr"><label for="n_q10a" class="qz-opt-nouns">A) mess</label><input type="radio" name="n_q10" id="n_q10b" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q10b" class="qz-opt-nouns">B) clutter</label><input type="radio" name="n_q10" id="n_q10c" class="qz-r-nouns wrng"><label for="n_q10c" class="qz-opt-nouns">C) chaos</label><div class="qz-fb-nouns ok">✅ Correct! <b>Mess</b> = беспорядок, грязь (общее состояние после готовки).</div><div class="qz-fb-nouns no">❌ B) Clutter = захламлённость (много вещей). C) Chaos = полный хаос (сильнее mess).</div></div></div></div></div>

class="dr-title-verbs">Verbs</h2><button class="dr-btn-verbs learn" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn Constructions</button><div class="dr-panel-verbs"><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ scrub vs wipe vs scour</strong><div class="diff"><b>scrub</b> — тереть с усилием (щеткой, губкой)<br><b>wipe</b> — протирать легко (тряпкой, рукой)<br><b>scour</b> — чистить до блеска, очень интенсивно<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li>I need to <b>scrub</b> the dirty floor. 🧽</li><li>Just <b>wipe</b> the table with a cloth. 🧻</li><li>She <b>scoured</b> the pan until it shined. ✨</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ sweep vs mop vs vacuum</strong><div class="diff"><b>sweep</b> — подметать веником/щёткой (сухая уборка)<br><b>mop</b> — мыть шваброй (влажная уборка)<br><b>vacuum</b> — пылесосить<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li><b>Sweep</b> the crumbs from the floor. 🧹</li><li><b>Mop</b> the kitchen — it's sticky! 💦</li><li>I need to <b>vacuum</b> the carpet. 🔌</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ dust vs polish</strong><div class="diff"><b>dust</b> — вытирать пыль (с мебели, полок)<br><b>polish</b> — полировать до блеска (мебель, серебро, обувь)<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li><b>Dust</b> the shelves — they're covered in dust! 🕸️</li><li>He <b>polished</b> his shoes for the interview. 👞</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ tidy vs organize</strong><div class="diff"><b>tidy</b> — быстро прибрать, разложить вещи по местам<br><b>organize</b> — системно организовать (с планом, категориями)<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li><b>Tidy</b> your room before guests arrive! 🛏️</li><li>I need to <b>organize</b> my files by date. 📁</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-verbs"><strong>⚠️ fold vs hang</strong><div class="diff"><b>fold</b> — складывать одежду (футболки, джинсы)<br><b>hang</b> — вешать на плечики (рубашки, платья)<div class="examples-box-verbs"><span class="examples-label-verbs">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-verbs"><li><b>Fold</b> the clean laundry. 👕</li><li><b>Hang</b> your dress — it will wrinkle! 👗</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="practice-box-verbs"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Spring Cleaning 🌸</h4><div class="practice-text-verbs">Today I'm doing spring cleaning! First, I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">sweep</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 подметать</span></span> the floor with a broom to remove all the crumbs. Then I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">mop</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 мыть шваброй</span></span> it with hot water and detergent. The carpet is very dirty, so I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">vacuum</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 пылесосить</span></span> it thoroughly. There's a terrible stain on the pan — I'll have to <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">scrub</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 тереть с усилием</span></span> it hard with a brush! After that, I'll just <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">wipe</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 протирать</span></span> the table with a damp cloth. The shelves are covered in dust, so I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">dust</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 вытирать пыль</span></span> them carefully. My shoes look terrible — I should <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">polish</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 полировать</span></span> them before the party. My room is a mess, so I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">tidy</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 прибрать</span></span> it quickly. But my closet needs serious help — I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">organize</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 организовать</span></span> all my clothes by season. After laundry, I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">fold</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 складывать</span></span> the t-shirts and jeans, and <span class="mask-wrapper-verbs" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-verbs">hang</span><span class="mask-overlay-verbs"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-verbs">📌 вешать</span></span> the dresses and shirts on hangers.</div></div></div><button class="dr-btn-verbs test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">✏️ Test: Verbs</button><div class="dr-panel-verbs"><h3 class="qz-title-verbs">Test: Verbs</h3><p class="qz-sub-verbs">Choose the correct option:</p><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">1</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">I need to ___ the dirty floor with a brush.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q1" id="v_q1a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q1a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) scrub</label><input type="radio" name="v_q1" id="v_q1b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q1b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) wipe</label><input type="radio" name="v_q1" id="v_q1c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q1c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) dust</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Scrub</b> = тереть с усилием (щеткой, губкой).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Wipe = протирать легко (тряпкой). C) Dust = вытирать пыль.</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">2</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">Just ___ the table with a cloth — it's not that dirty.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q2" id="v_q2a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q2a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) scrub</label><input type="radio" name="v_q2" id="v_q2b" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q2b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) wipe</label><input type="radio" name="v_q2" id="v_q2c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q2c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) scour</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Wipe</b> = протирать легко (не грязно, достаточно тряпки).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Scrub = тереть с усилием (слишком много для чистого стола). C) Scour = чистить до блеска (очень интенсивно).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">3</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">___ the crumbs from the floor with a broom.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q3" id="v_q3a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q3a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) Sweep</label><input type="radio" name="v_q3" id="v_q3b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q3b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) Mop</label><input type="radio" name="v_q3" id="v_q3c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q3c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) Vacuum</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Sweep</b> = подметать веником (сухая уборка).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Mop = мыть шваброй (влажная уборка). C) Vacuum = пылесосить (не веником).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">4</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">The kitchen floor is sticky — I need to ___ it.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q4" id="v_q4a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q4a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) sweep</label><input type="radio" name="v_q4" id="v_q4b" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q4b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) mop</label><input type="radio" name="v_q4" id="v_q4c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q4c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) dust</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Mop</b> = мыть шваброй (влажная уборка для липкого пола).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Sweep = подметать (не поможет с липкостью). C) Dust = вытирать пыль (не для пола).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">5</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">I need to ___ the carpet — there's so much dust!</div><input type="radio" name="v_q5" id="v_q5a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q5a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) sweep</label><input type="radio" name="v_q5" id="v_q5b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q5b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) mop</label><input type="radio" name="v_q5" id="v_q5c" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q5c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) vacuum</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Vacuum</b> = пылесосить (для ковра).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Sweep = подметать веником (не для ковра). B) Mop = мыть шваброй (нельзя мыть ковер).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">6</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">The shelves are covered in dust — I need to ___ them.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q6" id="v_q6a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q6a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) dust</label><input type="radio" name="v_q6" id="v_q6b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q6b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) polish</label><input type="radio" name="v_q6" id="v_q6c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q6c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) scrub</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Dust</b> = вытирать пыль (с мебели, полок).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Polish = полировать до блеска (не нужно для пыли). C) Scrub = тереть с усилием (слишком много).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">7</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">He ___ his shoes for the interview — they look brand new!</div><input type="radio" name="v_q7" id="v_q7a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q7a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) dusted</label><input type="radio" name="v_q7" id="v_q7b" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q7b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) polished</label><input type="radio" name="v_q7" id="v_q7c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q7c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) wiped</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Polished</b> = отполировал до блеска (обувь).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Dusted = вытер пыль (не даст блеска). C) Wiped = протер (не достаточно для блеска).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">8</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">___ your room before guests arrive!</div><input type="radio" name="v_q8" id="v_q8a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q8a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) Tidy</label><input type="radio" name="v_q8" id="v_q8b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q8b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) Organize</label><input type="radio" name="v_q8" id="v_q8c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q8c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) Fold</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Tidy</b> = быстро прибрать (разложить вещи по местам).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Organize = системно организовать (займёт много времени). C) Fold = складывать (только одежду).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">9</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">I need to ___ my files by date and category.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q9" id="v_q9a" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q9a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) tidy</label><input type="radio" name="v_q9" id="v_q9b" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q9b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) organize</label><input type="radio" name="v_q9" id="v_q9c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q9c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) hang</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Organize</b> = системно организовать (с планом, категориями).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ A) Tidy = просто прибрать (без системы). C) Hang = вешать (не подходит для файлов).</div></div><div class="qz-card-verbs"><div class="qz-num-verbs">10</div><div class="qz-sent-verbs">After laundry, I'll ___ the t-shirts and ___ the dresses.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q10" id="v_q10a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q10a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) fold / hang</label><input type="radio" name="v_q10" id="v_q10b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q10b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) hang / fold</label><input type="radio" name="v_q10" id="v_q10c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q10c" class="qz-opt-verbs">C) fold / fold</label><div class="qz-fb-verbs ok">✅ Correct! <b>Fold</b> = складывать (футболки), <b>hang</b> = вешать (платья).</div><div class="qz-fb-verbs no">❌ B) Порядок неправильный — футболки складывают, платья вешают. C) Платья нельзя складывать — помнутся!</div></div></div></div></div>

#ef4444}@media(max-width:600px){.dr-wrap-phrasal{padding:20px}.qz-sent-phrasal,.qz-opt-phrasal,.qz-fb-phrasal{margin-left:0;padding-left:12px}}</style><div class="dr-wrap-phrasal"><h2 class="dr-title-phrasal">Phrasal Verbs</h2><button class="dr-btn-phrasal learn" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn Constructions</button><div class="dr-panel-phrasal"><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>⚠️ clean up / tidy up / do up</strong><div class="diff"><b>clean up</b> — убрать грязь, помыть (удалить грязь)<br><b>tidy up</b> — навести порядок, разложить вещи по местам<br><b>do up</b> — прибрать, привести в порядок (разговорное)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li>I need to <b>clean up</b> the kitchen after cooking. 🧽</li><li><b>Tidy up</b> your room — clothes everywhere! 👕</li><li>Let's <b>do up</b> the house before guests arrive. 🏠</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>⚠️ put away / put back / put aside</strong><div class="diff"><b>put away</b> — убрать на место (в шкаф, ящик)<br><b>put back</b> — вернуть обратно (откуда взял)<br><b>put aside</b> — отложить в сторону (на потом)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li><b>Put away</b> the clean dishes. 🍽️</li><li>I borrowed your book — I'll <b>put it back</b> tomorrow. 📚</li><li><b>Put aside</b> some money for vacation. </li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>️ throw out / throw away / get rid of</strong><div class="diff"><b>throw out</b> — выбросить (в мусор)<br><b>throw away</b> — выбросить (синоним throw out)<br><b>get rid of</b> — избавиться от чего-то (не обязательно выбросить)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li><b>Throw out</b> the trash — it smells! 🗑️</li><li>Don't <b>throw away</b> that box — we might need it! 📦</li><li>I need to <b>get rid of</b> these old clothes. </li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>️ wipe down / wipe off / wipe up</strong><div class="diff"><b>wipe down</b> — протереть поверхность (стол, полку)<br><b>wipe off</b> — стереть что-то с поверхности (пятно, грязь)<br><b>wipe up</b> — вытереть жидкость (разлитую воду)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li><b>Wipe down</b> the counters after cooking. 🧻</li><li>I spilled coffee — let me <b>wipe it off</b>. ☕</li><li>The floor is wet — <b>wipe it up</b>! 💦</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>⚠️ clear away / clear up / clear off</strong><div class="diff"><b>clear away</b> — убрать со стола (посуду после еды)<br><b>clear up</b> — прибрать, навести порядок (в комнате)<br><b>clear off</b> — очистить поверхность (от вещей)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li>Can you <b>clear away</b> the dishes? ️</li><li>I need to <b>clear up</b> my messy desk. 📁</li><li><b>Clear off</b> the table — we need space! 🪑</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-phrasal"><strong>⚠️ sort out / sort through</strong><div class="diff"><b>sort out</b> — рассортировать, разобрать (по категориям)<br><b>sort through</b> — просматривать, перебирать (в поисках чего-то)<div class="examples-box-phrasal"><span class="examples-label-phrasal">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-phrasal"><li>I need to <b>sort out</b> my laundry — whites and colors. 🧺</li><li>I'm <b>sorting through</b> old photos. </li></ul></div></div></div><div class="practice-box-phrasal"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Weekend Cleaning 🧹</h4><div class="practice-text-phrasal">This weekend I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">clean up</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 убрать грязь</span></span> the whole house. First, I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">tidy up</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 навести порядок</span></span> my bedroom — there are clothes everywhere! I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">put away</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 убрать на место</span></span> all the clean laundry. Then I'll <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">sort out</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 рассортировать</span></span> the dirty clothes into whites and colors. I spilled some juice, so I need to <span class="mask-wrapper-phrasal" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-phrasal">wipe it up</span><span class="mask-overlay-phrasal"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-phrasal">📌 вытереть жидкость</span></span> quickly! C) Clear off = очистить поверхность (от вещей).</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">8</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">I need to ___ my laundry — whites and colors.</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q8" id="pv_q8a" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q8a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) sort out</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q8" id="pv_q8b" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q8b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) sort through</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q8" id="pv_q8c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q8c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) throw out</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Sort out</b> = рассортировать (по категориям).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ B) Sort through = просматривать, перебирать (в поисках). C) Throw out = выбросить.</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">9</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">I'm ___ old photos — looking for my wedding pictures.</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q9" id="pv_q9a" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q9a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) sorting out</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q9" id="pv_q9b" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q9b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) sorting through</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q9" id="pv_q9c" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q9c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) getting rid of</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Sorting through</b> = просматривать, перебирать (в поисках чего-то).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ A) Sorting out = рассортировать (по категориям). C) Getting rid of = избавляться от.</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">10</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">I need to ___ these old clothes — I never wear them!</div><input type="radio" name="pv_q10" id="pv_q10a" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q10a" class="qz-opt-phrasal">A) put away</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q10" id="pv_q10b" class="qz-r-phrasal wrng"><label for="pv_q10b" class="qz-opt-phrasal">B) sort out</label><input type="radio" name="pv_q10" id="pv_q10c" class="qz-r-phrasal corr"><label for="pv_q10c" class="qz-opt-phrasal">C) get rid of</label><div class="qz-fb-phrasal ok">✅ Correct! <b>Get rid of</b> = избавиться от чего-то.</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ A) Put away = убрать на место. class="dr-wrap-expressions"><h2 class="dr-title-expressions">Expressions</h2><button class="dr-btn-expressions learn" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📘 Learn Constructions</button><div class="dr-panel-expressions"><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ set the table vs clear the table</strong><div class="diff"><b>set the table</b> — накрыть на стол (поставить тарелки, приборы)<br><b>clear the table</b> — убрать со стола (посуду после еды)<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>Please <b>set the table</b> before dinner. 🍽️</li><li>After eating, <b>clear the table</b> and wash the dishes. 🧽</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ lend a hand / pitch in / give a hand</strong><div class="diff"><b>lend a hand</b> — помочь кому-то (временно)<br><b>pitch in</b> — внести вклад в общее дело (вместе с другими)<br><b>give a hand</b> — помочь (синоним lend a hand)<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>Can you <b>lend me a hand</b> with this heavy box? 📦</li><li>Everyone needs to <b>pitch in</b> to clean the house. 🏠</li><li>I'll <b>give you a hand</b> with the dishes. 🍽️</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ do the chores vs do the housework</strong><div class="diff"><b>do the chores</b> — выполнять конкретные обязанности по дому (мытьё посуды, уборка)<br><b>do the housework</b> — делать домашнюю работу в целом (все дела)<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>I hate <b>doing the chores</b> on weekends. 😒</li><li>She <b>does all the housework</b> while he works. 🏡</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ pull one's weight vs do one's share</strong><div class="diff"><b>pull one's weight</b> — делать свою долю работы (не лениться)<br><b>do one's share</b> — выполнять свою часть (обязанностей)<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>Everyone must <b>pull their weight</b> in this team. 💪</li><li>I always <b>do my share</b> of the cleaning. 🧹</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="cf-expressions"><strong>⚠️ tidy up one's act vs clean up one's act</strong><div class="diff"><b>tidy up one's act</b> — взяться за ум, начать делать всё правильно<br><b>clean up one's act</b> — исправиться, перестать плохо себя вести<div class="examples-box-expressions"><span class="examples-label-expressions">Примеры:</span><ul class="examples-list-expressions"><li>You need to <b>tidy up your act</b> or you'll fail! 📚</li><li>He needs to <b>clean up his act</b> and stop being lazy. 😤</li></ul></div></div></div><div class="practice-box-expressions"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Family Responsibilities 👨‍‍👧👦</h4><div class="practice-text-expressions">In our family, everyone needs to <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">pull their weight</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 делать свою долю работы</span></span>. My mom <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">does most of the housework</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 делает домашнюю работу</span></span>, but we all help too. Before dinner, I <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">set the table</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 накрыть на стол</span></span> with plates and cutlery. After eating, my brother <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">clears the table</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 убирает со стола</span></span> and puts the dishes in the dishwasher. When my dad needs help with something heavy, I always <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">lend him a hand</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 помочь</span></span>. On weekends, we all <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">pitch in</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 внести вклад</span></span> to clean the whole house — everyone <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">does their share</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 выполняет свою часть</span></span>. I hate <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">doing the chores</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 выполнять обязанности</span></span>, but it's necessary. My sister is very lazy, so mom told her she needs to <span class="mask-wrapper-expressions" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-expressions">tidy up her act</span><span class="mask-overlay-expressions"></span><span class="practice-tooltip-expressions">📌 взяться за ум</span></span> or she'll lose her allowance. C) "Do the table" — не существует.</div></div><div class="qz-card-expressions"><div class="qz-num-expressions">2</div><div class="qz-sent-expressions">After eating, please ___ and put the dishes in the sink.</div><input type="radio" name="e_q2" id="e_q2a" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q2a" class="qz-opt-expressions">A) set the table</label><input type="radio" name="e_q2" id="e_q2b" class="qz-r-expressions corr"><label for="e_q2b" class="qz-opt-expressions">B) clear the table</label><input type="radio" name="e_q2" id="e_q2c" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q2c" class="qz-opt-expressions">C) make the table</label><div class="qz-fb-expressions ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clear the table</b> = убрать со стола (после еды).</div><div class="qz-fb-expressions no">❌ A) Set the table = накрыть на стол (перед едой). C) "Make the table" — не существует.</div></div><div class="qz-card-expressions"><div class="qz-num-expressions">3</div><div class="qz-sent-expressions">Can you ___ with this heavy box? It's too heavy for me!</div><input type="radio" name="e_q3" id="e_q3a" class="qz-r-expressions corr"><label for="e_q3a" class="qz-opt-expressions">A) lend me a hand</label><input type="radio" name="e_q3" id="e_q3b" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q3b" class="qz-opt-expressions">B) pitch in</label><input type="radio" name="e_q3" id="e_q3c" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q3c" class="qz-opt-expressions">C) do my share</label><div class="qz-fb-expressions ok">✅ Correct! <b>Lend me a hand</b> = помочь мне (временно).</div><div class="qz-fb-expressions no">❌ B) Pitch in = внести вклад в общее дело (не для одного человека). C) Pull their weight = делать свою долю (не про совместную работу).</div></div><div class="qz-card-expressions"><div class="qz-num-expressions">5</div><div class="qz-sent-expressions">I hate ___ on weekends — I'd rather relax!</div><input type="radio" name="e_q5" id="e_q5a" class="qz-r-expressions corr"><label for="e_q5a" class="qz-opt-expressions">A) doing the chores</label><input type="radio" name="e_q5" id="e_q5b" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q5b" class="qz-opt-expressions">B) doing the housework</label><input type="radio" name="e_q5" id="e_q5c" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q5c" class="qz-opt-expressions">C) making the chores</label><div class="qz-fb-expressions ok">✅ Correct! <b>Doing the chores</b> = выполнять конкретные обязанности по дому.</div><div class="qz-fb-expressions no">❌ B) Doing the housework = делать домашнюю работу в целом (более общее). C) "Making the chores" — не существует.</div></div><div class="qz-card-expressions"><div class="qz-num-expressions">6</div><div class="qz-sent-expressions">She ___ while he works — she does everything at home.</div><input type="radio" name="e_q6" id="e_q6a" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q6a" class="qz-opt-expressions">A) does the chores</label><input type="radio" name="e_q6" id="e_q6b" class="qz-r-expressions corr"><label for="e_q6b" class="qz-opt-expressions">B) does all the housework</label><input type="radio" name="e_q6" id="e_q6c" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q6c" class="qz-opt-expressions">C) makes the housework</label><div class="qz-fb-expressions ok">✅ Correct! <b>Does all the housework</b> = делает всю домашнюю работу в целом.</div><div class="qz-fb-expressions no">❌ A) Does the chores = выполняет конкретные обязанности (не всё). C) "Makes the housework" — не существует.</div></div><div class="qz-card-expressions"><div class="qz-num-expressions">7</div><div class="qz-sent-expressions">Everyone must ___ in this team project — no lazy people!</div><input type="radio" name="e_q7" id="e_q7a" class="qz-r-expressions corr"><label for="e_q7a" class="qz-opt-expressions">A) pull their weight</label><input type="radio" name="e_q7" id="e_q7b" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q7b" class="qz-opt-expressions">B) pitch in</label><input type="radio" name="e_q7" id="e_q7c" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q7c" class="qz-opt-expressions">C) do their share</label><div class="qz-fb-expressions ok">✅ Correct! <b>Pull their weight</b> = делать свою долю работы (не лениться).</div><div class="qz-fb-expressions no">❌ B) Pitch in = внести вклад (более позитивно). C) Do their share = выполнять свою часть (менее идиоматично).</div></div><div class="qz-card-expressions"><div class="qz-num-expressions">8</div><div class="qz-sent-expressions">I always ___ of the cleaning — I'm very responsible!</div><input type="radio" name="e_q8" id="e_q8a" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q8a" class="qz-opt-expressions">A) pull my weight</label><input type="radio" name="e_q8" id="e_q8b" class="qz-r-expressions corr"><label for="e_q8b" class="qz-opt-expressions">B) do my share</label><input type="radio" name="e_q8" id="e_q8c" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q8c" class="qz-opt-expressions">C) pitch in</label><div class="qz-fb-expressions ok">✅ Correct! <b>Do my share</b> = выполнять свою часть (обязанностей).</div><div class="qz-fb-expressions no">❌ A) Pull my weight = делать свою долю (не лениться). C) Pitch in = внести вклад (в общее дело).</div></div><div class="qz-card-expressions"><div class="qz-num-expressions">9</div><div class="qz-sent-expressions">You need to ___ or you'll fail your exams!</div><input type="radio" name="e_q9" id="e_q9a" class="qz-r-expressions corr"><label for="e_q9a" class="qz-opt-expressions">A) tidy up your act</label><input type="radio" name="e_q9" id="e_q9b" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q9b" class="qz-opt-expressions">B) clean up your act</label><input type="radio" name="e_q9" id="e_q9c" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q9c" class="qz-opt-expressions">C) do your act</label><div class="qz-fb-expressions ok">✅ Correct! <b>Tidy up your act</b> = взяться за ум, начать делать всё правильно.</div><div class="qz-fb-expressions no">❌ B) Clean up your act = исправиться (перестать плохо себя вести). C) "Do your act" — не существует.</div></div><div class="qz-card-expressions"><div class="qz-num-expressions">10</div><div class="qz-sent-expressions">He needs to ___ and stop being so lazy and messy!</div><input type="radio" name="e_q10" id="e_q10a" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q10a" class="qz-opt-expressions">A) tidy up his act</label><input type="radio" name="e_q10" id="e_q10b" class="qz-r-expressions corr"><label for="e_q10b" class="qz-opt-expressions">B) clean up his act</label><input type="radio" name="e_q10" id="e_q10c" class="qz-r-expressions wrng"><label for="e_q10c" class="qz-opt-expressions">C) do his act</label><div class="qz-fb-expressions ok">✅ Correct! <b>Clean up his act</b> = исправиться, перестать плохо себя вести.</div><div class="qz-fb-expressions no">❌ A) Tidy up his act = взяться за ум (начать делать правильно). C) "Do his act" — не существует.</div></div></div></div></div>
