class="gr1-wrap"><div class="gr1-box"><h2 class="gr1-title"> Задание 1: Grammar - Nouns (Plural Forms)</h2><div class="gr1-section" id="sec-gr1"><h3>️ Впиши нужную форму слова</h3><div class="gr1-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Прочитай предложения и впиши правильную форму слова в поле ввода (множественное число). Исходное слово показано в поле — замени его на нужную форму. Обрати внимание на контекст и маркеры количества.</div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="1"><span class="gr1-q-num">1</span><span class="gr1-q-text">For breakfast, I usually make two toasted sandwiches with cheese and ham. My children love these simple <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">SANDWICH</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q1" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> with a cup of hot tea.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="1">💡 <b>sandwiches</b> — множественное число от sandwich. Слова на <b>-ch</b> получают окончание <b>-es</b>: sandwich → sandwich<b>es</b>, dish → dish<b>es</b>. Маркер «these» указывает на мн.ч.</div></div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="2"><span class="gr1-q-num">2</span><span class="gr1-q-text">The Italian restaurant serves homemade pasta with fresh ingredients. The chef uses five kilos of ripe red <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">TOMATO</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q2" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> every day to make the special sauce.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="2"> <b>tomatoes</b> — множественное число от tomato. Слова на <b>-o</b> после согласной часто получают <b>-es</b>: tomato → tomato<b>es</b>, potato → potato<b>es</b>. Маркер «five kilos».</div></div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="3"><span class="gr1-q-num">3</span><span class="gr1-q-text">The cooking class taught us how to properly use kitchen knives. We learned that sharp <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">KNIFE</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q3" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> are actually safer than dull ones because they require less force.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="3">💡 <b>knives</b> — множественное число от knife. Слова на <b>-fe</b> меняют окончание на <b>-ves</b>: knife → kni<b>ves</b>, wife → wi<b>ves</b>, life → li<b>ves</b>.</div></div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="4"><span class="gr1-q-num">4</span><span class="gr1-q-text">The forest is full of wild berries that are perfect for making jam. My grandmother collects different kinds of <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">BERRY</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q4" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> every summer to preserve them for winter.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="4">💡 <b>berries</b> — множественное число от berry. Слова на <b>-y</b> после согласной меняют <b>y</b> на <b>i</b> и добавляют <b>-es</b>: berry → berr<b>ies</b>, cherry → cherr<b>ies</b>. Маркер «different kinds».</div></div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="5"><span class="gr1-q-num">5</span><span class="gr1-q-text">The school cafeteria offers healthy meals for all students. Many <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">MAN</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q5" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> and women work in the kitchen to prepare delicious food every day.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="5">💡 <b>men</b> — <b>исключение</b>! man → m<b>en</b> (меняется корень). Маркер «Many» указывает на мн.ч. Запомни: man → men, woman → women, child → children.</div></div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="6"><span class="gr1-q-num">6</span><span class="gr1-q-text">Small children should not drink coffee or strong tea. These drinks are not good for their growing <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">CHILD</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q6" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> bodies and developing brains.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="6">💡 <b>children</b> — <b>исключение</b>! child → childr<b>en</b> (меняется корень). Контекст про детей и их здоровье.</div></div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="7"><span class="gr1-q-num">7</span><span class="gr1-q-text">The dentist reminded me about the importance of limiting sugary foods. He explained that sweets damage our <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">TOOTH</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q7" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> and can lead to serious dental problems.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="7">💡 <b>teeth</b> — <b>исключение</b>! tooth → t<b>ee</b>th (меняется корневая гласная). Контекст про стоматолога и здоровье зубов. Аналогично: foot → feet, goose → geese.</div></div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="8"><span class="gr1-q-num">8</span><span class="gr1-q-text">The recipe says to cut the apple into two equal parts before adding it to the pie. Cut each apple into <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">HALF</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q8" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> to make it easier to arrange in the dish.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="8">💡 <b>halves</b> — множественное число от half. Слова на <b>-f</b> меняют окончание на <b>-ves</b>: half → hal<b>ves</b>, leaf → lea<b>ves</b>, shelf → shel<b>ves</b>.</div></div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="9"><span class="gr1-q-num">9</span><span class="gr1-q-text">The grocery store had a special offer on root vegetables. My mother bought five kilos of brown <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">POTATO</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q9" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> for the family dinner.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="9">💡 <b>potatoes</b> — множественное число от potato. Правило <b>-o → -es</b>: potato → potato<b>es</b>. Маркер «five kilos» указывает на количество.</div></div><div class="gr1-q" data-q="10"><span class="gr1-q-num">10</span><span class="gr1-q-text">In traditional British cuisine, many families prepare special dishes for holidays. The <span class="gr1-input-wrap"><span class="gr1-input-hint">WOMAN</span><input type="text" class="gr1-input" id="gr1-q10" placeholder="" oninput="gr1HideHint(this)"></span> in the family usually cook the main meal while the men set the table.</span><div class="gr1-expl" data-expl="10">💡 <b>women</b> — <b>исключение</b>! woman → w<b>ome</b>n (меняется корень). Маркер «The» и контекст про женщин в семье.</div></div><button class="gr1-btn check" onclick="checkGR1()">✓ Check Answers</button><button class="gr1-btn reset" onclick="resetGR1()">↺ Reset</button><div id="gr1-fb" class="gr1-fb"></div></div></div></div><script>(function(){var gr1Answers={'1':'sandwiches','2':'tomatoes','3':'knives','4':'berries','5':'men','6':'children','7':'teeth','8':'halves','9':'potatoes','10':'women'};var gr1Checked=false;var sec1=document.getElementById('sec-gr1');window.gr1HideHint=function(input){var hint=input.previousElementSibling;if(hint&&hint.classList.contains('gr1-input-hint')){if(input.value.length>0){hint.style.opacity='0';input.classList.add('no-hint');}else{hint.style.opacity='1';input.classList.remove('no-hint');}}};window.checkGR1=function(){if(gr1Checked)return;var correct=0;var total=10;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('gr1-q'+q);var userAnswer=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();var correctAnswer=gr1Answers[q];if(userAnswer===correctAnswer){correct++;input.classList.add('correct-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('correct');}else{input.classList.add('wrong-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('wrong');}sec1.querySelector('.gr1-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.add('show');}gr1Checked=true;var fb=document.getElementById('gr1-fb');var pct=Math.round(correct/total*100);var msg='';if(pct===100)msg='🎉 Отлично! Все ответы верные!';else if(pct>=80)msg='👍 Хороший результат! Обрати внимание на ошибки.';else if(pct>=60)msg='💪 Неплохо, но стоит повторить правила.';else msg=' Повтори правила образования множественного числа и попробуй ещё раз.';fb.innerHTML='<b>Результат: '+correct+'/'+total+' ('+pct+'%)</b><div class="gr1-score"><div class="gr1-score-item" style="color:#166534">✅ Верно: '+correct+'</div><div class="gr1-score-item" style="color:#b91c1c"> Неверно: '+(total-correct)+'</div></div><p style="margin-top:10px">'+msg+'</p>';fb.className='gr1-fb show '+(correct===total?'correct':'incorrect');};window.resetGR1=function(){gr1Checked=false;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('gr1-q'+q);input.value='';input.classList.remove('correct-input','wrong-input','no-hint');input.previousElementSibling.style.opacity='1';input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.remove('correct','wrong');sec1.querySelector('.gr1-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.remove('show');}document.getElementById('gr1-fb').className='gr1-fb';};})();</script>




<!-- ================= GRAMMAR 2 ================= -->
<div id="grammar-2" class="section"><h1>Grammar 2</h1>
<!-- ================= ГрамПамятка: ВРЕМЕНА ================= -->
<style>.ar-wrap *{box-sizing:border-box}.ar-wrap{max-width:950px;margin:20px auto;font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;background:#f8f9fa;padding:20px;border-radius:16px;color:#334155}.ar-box{background:#fff;padding:25px;border-radius:16px;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)}.ar-title{color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 15px 0;text-align:center;font-size:23px}.ar-btn{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;width:100%;padding:14px 20px;margin:10px 0 15px;border:none;border-radius:10px;font-size:16px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;transition:.2s;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.15);color:#fff}.ar-btn:hover{opacity:.9;transform:translateY(-1px)}.ar-panel{display:none;overflow:hidden;animation:arFade .3s}.ar-panel.active{display:block}@keyframes arFade{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-8px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}.ar-panel h3{margin:0 0 15px 0;font-size:21px;color:#2c3e50}.ar-badge{display:inline-block;padding:4px 8px;border-radius:15px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;margin:2px}.ar-badge.green{background:#d4edda;color:#155724}.ar-badge.blue{background:#d1ecf1;color:#0c5460}.ar-badge.orange{background:#fff3cd;color:#856400}.ar-badge.purple{background:#e2d9f3;color:#5a2d82}.ar-badge.teal{background:#d1f2eb;color:#0e6655}.ar-badge.pink{background:#fadbd8;color:#943126}.ar-badge.cyan{background:#cffafe;color:#0e7490}.ar-forms{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr);gap:12px;margin:15px 0}.ar-form{background:#f8f9fa;padding:12px;border-radius:8px;border-left:3px solid #adb5bd}.ar-form-title{font-weight:700;font-size:13px;margin-bottom:8px;color:#555;text-transform:uppercase}.ar-form-val{font-size:14px;color:#333;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px;line-height:1.5}.ar-form-val b{color:#e74c3c}.ar-info{margin:3px 0;font-size:14px;line-height:1.5}.ar-info b{color:#2c3e50;display:inline}.ar-info div{display:inline}.ar-formula{background:#f3e5f5;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:4px;color:#6a1b9a;font-weight:600}.ar-formula.active{background:#e8f5e9;color:#155724}.ar-formula.passive{background:#e3f2fd;color:#0d47a1}.ar-formula.cyan{background:#cffafe;color:#0e7490}.ar-examples{margin-top:12px;background:#f1f3f5;padding:10px;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.6}.ar-example{margin-bottom:5px}.ar-table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:14px;margin:10px 0}.ar-table th,.ar-table td{border:1px solid #e0e0e0;padding:10px;text-align:left}.ar-table th{background:#f1f3f5;color:#2c3e50;font-weight:600}.ar-short-list{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:10px;margin:15px 0}.ar-short-item{background:#f8f9fa;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:6px;border:1px solid #ddd;font-size:15px}.ar-tips{padding-left:0;font-size:15px;line-height:1.7;color:#444;margin-top:15px}.ar-tips-item{margin-bottom:10px;padding-left:24px;position:relative}.ar-tips-item::before{content:"◆";position:absolute;left:0;color:#3498db;font-weight:700}.ar-section-title{font-size:16px;font-weight:700;margin:18px 0 10px 0;padding:6px 12px;border-radius:6px;display:inline-block}.ar-section-title.active{background:#e8f5e9;color:#155724;border-left:4px solid #4caf50}.ar-section-title.passive{background:#e3f2fd;color:#0d47a1;border-left:4px solid #2196f3}.ar-section-title.cyan{background:#cffafe;color:#0e7490;border-left:4px solid #06b6d4}</style><div class="ar-wrap"><div class="ar-box"><h2 class="ar-title">📚 Памятка: Времена английского глагола (Active & Passive)</h2><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#3b82f6" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🟢 1. Present Simple</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>🟢 Present Simple</h3><div class="ar-info"><b>📝 Когда используется: </b><div>Факты, привычки, регулярные действия</div></div><div class="ar-info"><b>⏰ Маркеры: </b><div><span class="ar-badge green">always</span><span class="ar-badge green">usually</span><span class="ar-badge green">often</span><span class="ar-badge green">every day</span><span class="ar-badge green">never</span></div></div><div class="ar-section-title active">✅ ACTIVE VOICE (Подлежащее само делает действие)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula active">Подлежащее + V1 (he/she/it: <b>-s/-es</b>)</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/You/We/They <b>write</b><br>He/She/It <b>writes</b></div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/You/We/They <b>don't</b> write<br>He/She/It <b>doesn't</b> write</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Do</b> I/you/we/they write?<br><b>Does</b> he/she/it write?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>do</b>. / No, I <b>don't</b>.<br>Yes, he <b>does</b>. / No, he <b>doesn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Active:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 🍵 She <span class="ar-badge green">usually</span> <b>drinks</b> coffee. <i>(Она обычно пьёт кофе.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 🏫 They <b>go</b> to school <span class="ar-badge green">every day</span>. <i>(Они ходят в школу каждый день.)</i></div></div><div class="ar-section-title passive">🛡️ PASSIVE VOICE (Действие совершается над подлежащим)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula passive">Подлежащее + <b>am/is/are</b> + V3</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I <b>am written</b> to.<br>He/She/It <b>is written</b>.<br>You/We/They <b>are written</b>.</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I <b>am not</b> written to.<br>He/She/It <b>isn't</b> written.<br>You/We/They <b>aren't</b> written.</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Am</b> I written to?<br><b>Is</b> he/she/it written?<br><b>Are</b> you/we/they written?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>am</b>. / No, I'm <b>not</b>.<br>Yes, it <b>is</b>. / No, it <b>isn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Passive:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 🏭 Cars <b>are made</b> in Germany. <i>(Машины производятся в Германии.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 📧 Emails <b>are sent</b> every day. <i>(Письма отправляются каждый день.)</i></div></div></div><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#ef4444" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🔴 2. Past Simple</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>🔴 Past Simple</h3><div class="ar-info"><b>📝 Когда используется: </b><div>Завершённые действия в прошлом</div></div><div class="ar-info"><b>⏰ Маркеры: </b><div><span class="ar-badge orange">yesterday</span><span class="ar-badge orange">last week</span><span class="ar-badge orange">ago</span><span class="ar-badge orange">in 2019</span></div></div><div class="ar-section-title active">✅ ACTIVE VOICE (Подлежащее само сделало действие)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula active">Подлежащее + <b>V2</b> (прошедшая форма)</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All subjects + <b>wrote</b> / <b>worked</b></div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All <b>didn't</b> write (V1!)</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Did</b> all write? (V1!)</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>did</b>. / No, I <b>didn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Active:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 👋 We <b>met</b> <span class="ar-badge orange">yesterday</span>. <i>(Мы встретились вчера.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• ✈️ He <b>left</b> <span class="ar-badge orange">last week</span>. <i>(Он уехал на прошлой неделе.)</i></div></div><div class="ar-section-title passive">🛡️ PASSIVE VOICE (Действие было совершено над подлежащим)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula passive">Подлежащее + <b>was/were</b> + V3</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/He/She/It <b>was written</b><br>You/We/They <b>were written</b></div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/He/She/It <b>wasn't</b> written<br>You/We/They <b>weren't</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Was</b> I/he/she/it written?<br><b>Were</b> you/we/they written?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>was</b>. / No, I <b>wasn't</b>.<br>Yes, they <b>were</b>. / No, they <b>weren't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Passive:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 🎾 The match <b>was postponed</b> <span class="ar-badge orange">yesterday</span>. <i>(Матч отложили вчера.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 📚 The books <b>were written</b> long ago. <i>(Книги были написаны давно.)</i></div></div></div><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#6366f1" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🔵 3. Future Simple</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>🔵 Future Simple</h3><div class="ar-info"><b>📝 Когда используется: </b><div>Планы, предсказания, спонтанные решения в будущем</div></div><div class="ar-info"><b>⏰ Маркеры: </b><div><span class="ar-badge blue">tomorrow</span><span class="ar-badge blue">next week</span><span class="ar-badge blue">soon</span></div></div><div class="ar-section-title active">✅ ACTIVE VOICE (Подлежащее само сделает действие)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula active">Подлежащее + <b>will</b> + V1</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All <b>will write</b> / <b>'ll</b> write</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All <b>won't</b> write</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Will</b> all write?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>will</b>. / No, I <b>won't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Active:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 📞 I <b>will call</b> you <span class="ar-badge blue">tomorrow</span>. <i>(Я позвоню тебе завтра.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 🚗 They <b>will come</b> <span class="ar-badge blue">next week</span>. <i>(Они приедут на следующей неделе.)</i></div></div><div class="ar-section-title passive">🛡️ PASSIVE VOICE (Действие будет совершено над подлежащим)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula passive">Подлежащее + <b>will be</b> + V3</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All <b>will be written</b></div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All <b>won't be</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Will</b> all be written?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, it <b>will</b>. / No, it <b>won't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Passive:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 🌉 The bridge <b>will be opened</b> <span class="ar-badge blue">soon</span>. <i>(Мост скоро откроют.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 📧 The letters <b>will be sent</b> <span class="ar-badge blue">tomorrow</span>. <i>(Письма будут отправлены завтра.)</i></div></div></div><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#14b8a6" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🌊 4. Present Continuous</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>🌊 Present Continuous</h3><div class="ar-info"><b>📝 Когда используется: </b><div>Действие происходит прямо сейчас, в момент речи</div></div><div class="ar-info"><b>⏰ Маркеры: </b><div><span class="ar-badge teal">now</span><span class="ar-badge teal">right now</span><span class="ar-badge teal">at the moment</span></div></div><div class="ar-section-title active">✅ ACTIVE VOICE (Подлежащее само делает действие сейчас)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula active">Подлежащее + <b>am/is/are</b> + V<b>ing</b></div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I <b>am writing</b><br>He/She/It <b>is writing</b><br>You/We/They <b>are writing</b></div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I <b>am not</b> writing<br>He/She/It <b>isn't</b> writing<br>You/We/They <b>aren't</b> writing</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Am</b> I writing?<br><b>Is</b> he/she/it writing?<br><b>Are</b> you/we/they writing?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>am</b>. / No, I'm <b>not</b>.<br>Yes, he <b>is</b>. / No, he <b>isn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Active:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 👀 Look! He <b>is working</b> <span class="ar-badge teal">right now</span>. <i>(Смотри! Он работает прямо сейчас.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 📚 We <b>are studying</b> <span class="ar-badge teal">at the moment</span>. <i>(Мы учимся в данный момент.)</i></div></div><div class="ar-section-title passive">🛡️ PASSIVE VOICE (Действие совершается над подлежащим сейчас)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula passive">Подлежащее + <b>am/is/are</b> + <b>being</b> + V3</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I <b>am being</b> written to<br>He/She/It <b>is being</b> written<br>You/We/They <b>are being</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I <b>am not being</b> written to<br>It <b>isn't being</b> written<br>They <b>aren't being</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Am</b> I being written to?<br><b>Is</b> it being written?<br><b>Are</b> they being written?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, it <b>is</b>. / No, it <b>isn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Passive:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 🍳 Dinner <b>is being cooked</b> <span class="ar-badge teal">right now</span>. <i>(Ужин готовится прямо сейчас.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 🚗 The car <b>is being repaired</b> at the moment. <i>(Машину сейчас ремонтируют.)</i></div></div></div><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#8b5cf6" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">⏳ 5. Past Continuous</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>⏳ Past Continuous</h3><div class="ar-info"><b>📝 Когда используется: </b><div>Действие длилось в определённый момент в прошлом</div></div><div class="ar-info"><b>⏰ Маркеры: </b><div><span class="ar-badge purple">at 5pm yesterday</span><span class="ar-badge purple">while</span><span class="ar-badge purple">when</span></div></div><div class="ar-section-title active">✅ ACTIVE VOICE (Действие длилось в момент в прошлом)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula active">Подлежащее + <b>was/were</b> + V<b>ing</b></div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/He/She/It <b>was writing</b><br>You/We/They <b>were writing</b></div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/He/She/It <b>wasn't</b> writing<br>You/We/They <b>weren't</b> writing</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Was</b> I/he/she/it writing?<br><b>Were</b> you/we/they writing?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>was</b>. / No, I <b>wasn't</b>.<br>Yes, they <b>were</b>. / No, they <b>weren't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Active:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 😴 I <b>was sleeping</b> <span class="ar-badge purple">at 5pm yesterday</span>. <i>(Я спал вчера в 5 вечера.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 🚪 <span class="ar-badge purple">When</span> he came, we <b>were eating</b>. <i>(Когда он пришёл, мы ели.)</i></div></div><div class="ar-section-title passive">🛡️ PASSIVE VOICE (Действие длилось над подлежащим в прошлом)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula passive">Подлежащее + <b>was/were</b> + <b>being</b> + V3</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/He/She/It <b>was being</b> written<br>You/We/They <b>were being</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/He/She/It <b>wasn't being</b> written<br>You/We/They <b>weren't being</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Was</b> it being written?<br><b>Were</b> they being written?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, it <b>was</b>. / No, it <b>wasn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Passive:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 🚪 <span class="ar-badge purple">When</span> he came, the room <b>was being cleaned</b>. <i>(Когда он пришёл, комнату убирали.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 🏠 The house <b>was being built</b> <span class="ar-badge purple">at that time</span>. <i>(Дом строился в то время.)</i></div></div></div><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#ec4899" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">✨ 6. Present Perfect</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>✨ Present Perfect</h3><div class="ar-info"><b>📝 Когда используется: </b><div>Результат к настоящему моменту, опыт</div></div><div class="ar-info"><b>⏰ Маркеры: </b><div><span class="ar-badge pink">already</span><span class="ar-badge pink">yet</span><span class="ar-badge pink">just</span><span class="ar-badge pink">recently</span><span class="ar-badge pink">so far</span></div></div><div class="ar-section-title active">✅ ACTIVE VOICE (Подлежащее уже сделало действие, результат сейчас)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula active">Подлежащее + <b>have/has</b> + V3</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/You/We/They <b>have written</b><br>He/She/It <b>has written</b></div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/You/We/They <b>haven't</b> written<br>He/She/It <b>hasn't</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Have</b> I/you/we/they written?<br><b>Has</b> he/she/it written?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>have</b>. / No, I <b>haven't</b>.<br>Yes, he <b>has</b>. / No, he <b>hasn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Active:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• ✅ I <b>have</b> <span class="ar-badge pink">already</span> <b>finished</b>. <i>(Я уже закончил.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 🚪 He <b>has</b> <span class="ar-badge pink">just</span> <b>arrived</b>. <i>(Он только что прибыл.)</i></div></div><div class="ar-section-title passive">🛡️ PASSIVE VOICE (Результат действия над подлежащим к настоящему)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula passive">Подлежащее + <b>have/has</b> + <b>been</b> + V3</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/You/We/They <b>have been</b> written<br>He/She/It <b>has been</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/You/We/They <b>haven't been</b> written<br>He/She/It <b>hasn't been</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Have</b> they been written?<br><b>Has</b> it been written?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, it <b>has</b>. / No, it <b>hasn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Passive:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 🌉 The project <b>has been completed</b> <span class="ar-badge pink">recently</span>. <i>(Проект недавно завершён.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 📧 The emails <b>have</b> <span class="ar-badge pink">already</span> <b>been sent</b>. <i>(Письма уже отправлены.)</i></div></div></div><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#06b6d4" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🌟 7. Present Perfect Continuous</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>🌟 Present Perfect Continuous</h3><div class="ar-info"><b>📝 Когда используется: </b><div>Действие началось в прошлом, продолжалось какое-то время и либо продолжается до сих пор, либо только что закончилось с видимым результатом</div></div><div class="ar-info"><b>⏰ Маркеры: </b><div><span class="ar-badge cyan">for</span><span class="ar-badge cyan">since</span><span class="ar-badge cyan">all day</span><span class="ar-badge cyan">all morning</span><span class="ar-badge cyan">how long</span><span class="ar-badge cyan">lately</span><span class="ar-badge cyan">recently</span></div></div><div class="ar-section-title cyan">✅ ACTIVE VOICE (Действие длилось и длится / только что закончилось)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula cyan">Подлежащее + <b>have/has</b> + <b>been</b> + V<b>ing</b></div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/You/We/They <b>have been working</b><br>He/She/It <b>has been working</b></div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">I/You/We/They <b>haven't been</b> working<br>He/She/It <b>hasn't been</b> working</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Have</b> I/you/we/they been working?<br><b>Has</b> he/she/it been working?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>have</b>. / No, I <b>haven't</b>.<br>Yes, he <b>has</b>. / No, he <b>hasn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Active:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• ⏰ I <b>have been waiting</b> <span class="ar-badge cyan">for</span> two hours. <i>(Я жду уже два часа — и всё ещё жду.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 📅 She <b>has been studying</b> <span class="ar-badge cyan">since</span> morning. <i>(Она учится с самого утра.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 🌧️ It <b>has been raining</b> <span class="ar-badge cyan">all day</span>. <i>(Дождь идёт весь день.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 😓 You look tired. — Yes, I<b>'ve been running</b>. <i>(Ты выглядишь уставшим. — Да, я бегал — виден результат.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• ❓ <b>How long</b> <b>have</b> you <b>been learning</b> English? <i>(Как давно ты учишь английский?)</i></div></div><div class="ar-section-title passive">🛡️ PASSIVE VOICE</div><div class="ar-info"><b>⚠️ Важно: </b><div>Present Perfect Continuous <b>НЕ ИМЕЕТ</b> формы Passive в современном английском! Вместо этого используется Present Perfect Passive или другие конструкции.</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#e91e63"><div class="ar-form-title">🚫 Нет Passive формы</div><div class="ar-form-val">❌ <i>The work has been being done.</i><br><br>✅ Используй Present Perfect Passive:<br><i>The work <b>has been done</b>.</i></div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">💡 Альтернатива</div><div class="ar-form-val">Если нужно подчеркнуть длительность пассива (очень редко):<br><i>The house <b>has been being</b> renovated for months.</i></div></div></div><div class="ar-info" style="margin-top:15px;"><b>🎯 Present Perfect vs Present Perfect Continuous:</b></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#e91e63"><div class="ar-form-title">✨ Present Perfect</div><div class="ar-form-val">Акцент на <b>результате</b>:<br><i>I <b>have painted</b> the room.</i><br>🎨 Комната ПОКРАШЕНА (результат).</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#06b6d4"><div class="ar-form-title">🌟 Present Perfect Continuous</div><div class="ar-form-val">Акцент на <b>процессе/длительности</b>:<br><i>I <b>have been painting</b> the room.</i><br>🖌️ Я КРАШУ комнату (процесс, руки в краске).</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Сравнение:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• ✅ <i>I <b>have written</b> 3 letters.</i> (Результат — 3 письма готовы.)</div><div class="ar-example">• 🌟 <i>I <b>have been writing</b> letters all morning.</i> (Процесс — всё утро писал.)</div></div><div class="ar-info" style="margin-top:15px;"><b>⚠️ Глаголы, которые НЕ используются в Continuous:</b></div><div class="ar-short-list"><div class="ar-short-item"><b>know</b> 🧠</div><div class="ar-short-item"><b>believe</b> 💭</div><div class="ar-short-item"><b>love</b> ❤️</div><div class="ar-short-item"><b>hate</b> 😠</div><div class="ar-short-item"><b>want</b> 🙏</div><div class="ar-short-item"><b>need</b> ⚠️</div><div class="ar-short-item"><b>understand</b> 💡</div><div class="ar-short-item"><b>remember</b> 🧠</div><div class="ar-short-item"><b>belong</b> 🏠</div><div class="ar-short-item"><b>own</b> 🔑</div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• ❌ <i>I have been knowing him for years.</i><br>✅ <i>I <b>have known</b> him for years.</i> (Present Perfect!)</div><div class="ar-example">• ❌ <i>She has been having a car.</i><br>✅ <i>She <b>has had</b> a car for 5 years.</i></div></div></div><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#f59e0b" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🔙 8. Past Perfect</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>🔙 Past Perfect</h3><div class="ar-info"><b>📝 Когда используется: </b><div>Действие произошло ДО другого действия в прошлом</div></div><div class="ar-info"><b>⏰ Маркеры: </b><div><span class="ar-badge orange">before</span><span class="ar-badge orange">by the time</span><span class="ar-badge orange">after</span><span class="ar-badge orange">by 5pm</span></div></div><div class="ar-section-title active">✅ ACTIVE VOICE (Действие завершилось до другого в прошлом)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula active">Подлежащее + <b>had</b> + V3</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All <b>had written</b> / <b>'d</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All <b>hadn't</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Had</b> all written?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, I <b>had</b>. / No, I <b>hadn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Active:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 👋 She <b>had left</b> <span class="ar-badge orange">before</span> I arrived. <i>(Она ушла до того, как я приехал.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• ⏰ <span class="ar-badge orange">By the time</span> we came, he <b>had gone</b>. <i>(К тому времени, как мы пришли, он уже ушёл.)</i></div></div><div class="ar-section-title passive">🛡️ PASSIVE VOICE (Действие над подлежащим произошло до другого в прошлом)</div><div class="ar-info"><b>📌 Формула: </b><div class="ar-formula passive">Подлежащее + <b>had</b> + <b>been</b> + V3</div></div><div class="ar-forms"><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#4caf50"><div class="ar-form-title">✅ (+)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All <b>had been</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#f44336"><div class="ar-form-title">➖ (−)</div><div class="ar-form-val">All <b>hadn't been</b> written</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#2196f3"><div class="ar-form-title">❓ (?)</div><div class="ar-form-val"><b>Had</b> all been written?</div></div><div class="ar-form" style="border-left-color:#ff9800"><div class="ar-form-title">✂️ Short Ans</div><div class="ar-form-val">Yes, it <b>had</b>. / No, it <b>hadn't</b>.</div></div></div><div class="ar-examples"><b style="font-size:13px;color:#666;">📖 Примеры Passive:</b><br><div class="ar-example">• 🏠 The room <b>had been cleaned</b> <span class="ar-badge orange">by the time</span> we came. <i>(Комнату убрали к тому времени, как мы пришли.)</i></div><div class="ar-example">• 📚 The book <b>had been written</b> <span class="ar-badge orange">before</span> the war. <i>(Книга была написана до войны.)</i></div></div></div><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#64748b" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">📊 9. Comparison Table</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>📊 Сравнительная таблица Active vs Passive</h3><div style="overflow-x:auto;"><table class="ar-table"><thead><tr><th>Время</th><th>✅ Active (Формула)</th><th>🛡️ Passive (Формула)</th><th>Пример Active → Passive</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><b>Present Simple</b></td><td>V / V<b>+s</b></td><td>am/is/are + V3</td><td>She writes → It is written</td></tr><tr><td><b>Past Simple</b></td><td>V<b>2</b> (ed)</td><td>was/were + V3</td><td>She wrote → It was written</td></tr><tr><td><b>Future Simple</b></td><td>will + V1</td><td>will be + V3</td><td>She will write → It will be written</td></tr><tr><td><b>Pres. Continuous</b></td><td>am/is/are + V<b>ing</b></td><td>am/is/are being + V3</td><td>She is writing → It is being written</td></tr><tr><td><b>Past Continuous</b></td><td>was/were + V<b>ing</b></td><td>was/were being + V3</td><td>She was writing → It was being written</td></tr><tr><td><b>Pres. Perfect</b></td><td>have/has + V3</td><td>have/has been + V3</td><td>She has written → It has been written</td></tr><tr><td><b>Pres. Perfect Cont.</b></td><td>have/has been + V<b>ing</b></td><td>— (нет формы)</td><td>She has been writing → —</td></tr><tr><td><b>Past Perfect</b></td><td>had + V3</td><td>had been + V3</td><td>She had written → It had been written</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="ar-info" style="margin-top:15px;"><b>🎯 Как понять, что нужен Passive?</b></div><div class="ar-tips"><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>👤 Подлежащее НЕ само делает действие</b>, а испытывает его на себе (машины производят, письма пишут, дом строят).</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>⚙️ Формула пассива всегда:</b> глагол <b>to be</b> (в нужном времени) + <b>V3</b> (3-я форма основного глагола).</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>⚠️ Непереходные глаголы</b> (<i>happen, arrive, die, sleep, exist</i>) <b>НЕ ИМЕЮТ</b> формы Passive!</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>🔍 В задании смотри на подлежащее:</b> если это <i>popsicle, bridge, letter, project</i> — скорее всего, Passive.</div></div></div><button class="ar-btn" style="background:#475569" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">✂️ 10. Short Forms & Tips</button><div class="ar-panel"><h3>✂️ Short Forms & 💡 Tips</h3><div style="margin:15px 0;"><b style="font-size:14px;color:#555;">Сокращения:</b><div class="ar-short-list"><div class="ar-short-item">will not → <b>won't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">will → <b>'ll</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">has not → <b>hasn't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">have not → <b>haven't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">is not → <b>isn't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">are not → <b>aren't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">was not → <b>wasn't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">were not → <b>weren't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">had not → <b>hadn't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">does not → <b>doesn't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">did not → <b>didn't</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">have been → <b>'ve been</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">has been → <b>'s been</b></div><div class="ar-short-item">had been → <b>'d been</b></div></div></div><div class="ar-tips"><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>🔍 Шаг 1. Найди маркер времени</b> (yesterday, now, by the time, already, for, since). Он подскажет время.</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>👤 Шаг 2. Определи залог:</b> подлежащее само делает (Active) или действие над ним (Passive)?</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>🔢 Шаг 3. Проверь число и лицо:</b> he/she/it → <i>is, was, has</i>; I/you/we/they → <i>are, were, have</i>. Не забудь <b>-s</b> в Present Simple!</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>⚙️ Шаг 4. Для Passive:</b> выбери правильную форму <b>to be</b> + поставь основной глагол в <b>V3</b>.</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>🌟 Present Perfect Continuous:</b> акцент на длительности действия. Маркеры: <i>for, since, all day, how long</i>. Помни: это время <b>НЕ имеет Passive</b>!</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>✨ vs 🌟 Perfect vs Perfect Continuous:</b> Perfect — важен <b>результат</b> (сколько сделано). Perfect Continuous — важен <b>процесс</b> (как долго делали).</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>⚠️ Stative verbs</b> (<i>know, love, want, need, believe</i>) <b>НЕ используются</b> в Continuous! Вместо <i>I have been knowing</i> → <i>I have known</i>.</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>❓ В вопросах и отрицаниях</b> Active: do/does/did + инфинитив (без to). В Passive: вспомогательный глагол перед подлежащим.</div><div class="ar-tips-item"><b>⚠️ Неправильные глаголы:</b> учи таблицу! В Perfect и после did/didn't всегда 1-я форма, в Passive всегда 3-я.</div></div></div></div></div>
<!-- ================= Задание 2 ================= -->
<style>.gr2-wrap{max-width:900px;margin:20px auto;font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;background:#f8f9fa;padding:25px;border-radius:16px;color:#334155}.gr2-box{background:#fff;padding:25px;border-radius:16px;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)}.gr2-title{color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 20px 0;font-size:24px;font-weight:700}.gr2-section{background:#fff;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:14px;padding:20px;margin:20px 0}.gr2-section h3{font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 15px 0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}.gr2-instruction{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#dbeafe,#bfdbfe);border:2px solid #3b82f6;border-radius:12px;padding:15px;margin:15px 0;font-size:14px;color:#1e40af;line-height:1.6}.gr2-q{margin:16px 0;padding:16px;background:#f8fafc;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:10px;transition:.3s}.gr2-q.correct{background:#f0fdf4;border-color:#22c55e}.gr2-q.wrong{background:#fef2f2;border-color:#ef4444}.gr2-q-num{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:28px;height:28px;background:#8b5cf6;color:#fff;border-radius:50%;font-weight:700;font-size:14px;margin-right:8px}.gr2-q-text{font-size:15px;line-height:1.7;color:#334155}.gr2-input-wrap{position:relative;display:inline-block;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 4px}.gr2-input-hint{position:absolute;left:10px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);font-weight:600;color:#9ca3af;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .2s;font-size:14px;font-style:italic;letter-spacing:0.5px}.gr2-input{display:inline-block;width:200px;padding:6px 10px 6px 10px;border:2px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:6px;font-size:14px;vertical-align:middle;transition:.2s;background:#fff}.gr2-input:focus{outline:none;border-color:#8b5cf6;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(139,92,246,.1)}.gr2-input.no-hint{padding-left:10px}.gr2-input.correct-input{border-color:#22c55e;background:#dcfce7;color:#166534;font-weight:600}.gr2-input.wrong-input{border-color:#ef4444;background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c}.gr2-expl{display:none;margin-top:10px;padding:10px 14px;background:#fff;border-left:4px solid #f59e0b;border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;color:#78350f}.gr2-expl.show{display:block;animation:fadeIn2 .3s}.gr2-btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:10px 20px;margin:15px 10px 15px 0;border:none;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;transition:.2s;color:#fff}.gr2-btn:hover{transform:translateY(-1px)}.gr2-btn.check{background:#10b981;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(16,185,129,.3)}.gr2-btn.check:hover{background:#059669}.gr2-btn.reset{background:#64748b;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(100,116,139,.3)}.gr2-btn.reset:hover{background:#475569}.gr2-fb{display:none;margin-top:12px;padding:14px;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.6}.gr2-fb.show{display:block;animation:fadeIn2 .3s}.gr2-fb.correct{background:#dcfce7;color:#15803d;border-left:4px solid #22c55e}.gr2-fb.incorrect{background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c;border-left:4px solid #ef4444}.gr2-score{display:flex;gap:12px;margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap}.gr2-score-item{background:#fff;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0}@keyframes fadeIn2{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}@media(max-width:600px){.gr2-input{width:160px}.gr2-box{padding:15px}}</style><div class="gr2-wrap"><div class="gr2-box"><h2 class="gr2-title">📝 Задание 2: Grammar - Verb Tenses</h2><div class="gr2-section" id="sec-gr2"><h3>⏱️ Преобразуйте слово в нужную временную форму</h3><div class="gr2-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Прочитай предложения и впиши правильную форму глагола в поле ввода. Используй одну из временных форм: Present Simple, Present Continuous, Past Simple, Past Continuous, Future Simple, Present Perfect, Present Perfect Continuous, Past Perfect (актив или пассив). Исходный глагол показан в поле — замени его на нужную форму.</div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="1"><span class="gr2-q-num">1</span><span class="gr2-q-text">The kitchen floor looked terrible after the big party last night. The stubborn stains on the tiles <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">SCRUB</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q1" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> with a special cleaning product. Now everything is shining and clean again.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="1">💡 <b>were scrubbed</b> — Past Simple Passive. Маркер "last night" указывает на прошлое. Подлежащее "stains" (пятна) не может само scrub (тёрть), действие совершается над ним. Множественное число → were + V3.</div></div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="2"><span class="gr2-q-num">2</span><span class="gr2-q-text">My grandfather loves taking care of his antique furniture collection. Right now, he <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">POLISH</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q2" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> the old wooden clock in the hallway. It will look brand new in a few minutes.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="2">💡 <b>is polishing</b> — Present Continuous. Маркер "Right now" указывает на действие в момент речи. Подлежащее "he" → is + V-ing.</div></div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="3"><span class="gr2-q-num">3</span><span class="gr2-q-text">The children made a huge mess while baking cookies together. Their mother <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">WIPE</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q3" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> the sticky counter with a damp cloth after they went to bed. The kitchen was spotless by morning.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="3">💡 <b>wiped</b> — Past Simple. Контекст "after they went to bed" указывает на завершённое действие в прошлом. Мама вытерла стол (активное действие).</div></div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="4"><span class="gr2-q-num">4</span><span class="gr2-q-text">Keeping the living room clean requires regular effort from everyone. Every Saturday morning, she <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">DUST</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q4" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> all the shelves and picture frames in the house. This simple habit prevents dirt from building up.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="4">💡 <b>dusts</b> — Present Simple. Маркер "Every Saturday morning" указывает на регулярное, повторяющееся действие. Подлежащее "she" (3-е лицо ед.ч.) → глагол с окончанием -s.</div></div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="5"><span class="gr2-q-num">5</span><span class="gr2-q-text">It was a very busy Sunday afternoon at the small cafe. While the cook was preparing fresh pastries, the waiter <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">SWEEP</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q5" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> the floor near the entrance. The customers appreciated the clean environment.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="5"> <b>was sweeping</b> — Past Continuous. Союз "While" указывает на длительное действие в прошлом, которое происходило параллельно с другим действием (cook was preparing). Единственное число → was + V-ing.</div></div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="6"><span class="gr2-q-num">6</span><span class="gr2-q-text">The clean laundry has been drying on the balcony all day long. I <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">FOLD</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q6" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> all the clothes and put them neatly in the wardrobe. Now I can finally sit down and relax.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="6"> <b>have folded</b> — Present Perfect. Действие завершено, результат важен сейчас (одежда уже в шкафу, можно отдыхать).</div></div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="7"><span class="gr2-q-num">7</span><span class="gr2-q-text">I have a very important job interview tomorrow morning at nine. I <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">IRON</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q7" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> my best white shirt tonight to look professional. Everything must be perfect for this opportunity.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="7">💡 <b>will iron</b> — Future Simple. Маркер "tonight" указывает на будущее. Это план/решение, принятое в момент речи.</div></div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="8"><span class="gr2-q-num">8</span><span class="gr2-q-text">The guests were arriving in just thirty minutes, and the house was a disaster. Fortunately, the children <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">TIDY</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q8" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> their messy bedroom before their friends came. The toys were arranged on the shelves.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="8">💡 <b>had tidied</b> — Past Perfect. Действие произошло ДО другого действия в прошлом ("before their friends came"). Дети навели порядок ДО прихода друзей.</div></div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="9"><span class="gr2-q-num">9</span><span class="gr2-q-text">The delivery workers have been working since early morning without a break. They <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">UNLOAD</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q9" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> heavy boxes from the truck for the past three hours. The supermarket will open soon.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="9">💡 <b>have been unloading</b> — Present Perfect Continuous. Маркер "for the past three hours" и контекст "since early morning" указывают на длительное действие, которое началось в прошлом и продолжается до сих пор.</div></div><div class="gr2-q" data-q="10"><span class="gr2-q-num">10</span><span class="gr2-q-text">After the family finished their big Sunday dinner together, all the dirty plates and glasses <span class="gr2-input-wrap"><span class="gr2-input-hint">LOAD</span><input type="text" class="gr2-input" id="gr2-q10" placeholder="" oninput="gr2HideHint(this)"></span> carefully into the dishwasher. The machine started working immediately. The kitchen became quiet again.</span><div class="gr2-expl" data-expl="10"> <b>were loaded</b> — Past Simple Passive. Контекст "After the family finished dinner" указывает на прошлое. Тарелки и стаканы не могут сами себя загрузить → пассив. Множественное число → were + V3.</div></div><button class="gr2-btn check" onclick="checkGR2()">✓ Check Answers</button><button class="gr2-btn reset" onclick="resetGR2()">↺ Reset</button><div id="gr2-fb" class="gr2-fb"></div></div></div></div><script>(function(){var gr2Answers={'1':'were scrubbed','2':'is polishing','3':'wiped','4':'dusts','5':'was sweeping','6':'have folded','7':'will iron','8':'had tidied','9':'have been unloading','10':'were loaded'};var gr2Checked=false;var sec2=document.getElementById('sec-gr2');window.gr2HideHint=function(input){var hint=input.previousElementSibling;if(hint&&hint.classList.contains('gr2-input-hint')){if(input.value.length>0){hint.style.opacity='0';input.classList.add('no-hint');}else{hint.style.opacity='1';input.classList.remove('no-hint');}}};window.checkGR2=function(){if(gr2Checked)return;var correct=0;var total=10;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('gr2-q'+q);var userAnswer=input.value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,' ');var correctAnswer=gr2Answers[q].toLowerCase();if(userAnswer===correctAnswer){correct++;input.classList.add('correct-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('correct');}else{input.classList.add('wrong-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('wrong');}sec2.querySelector('.gr2-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.add('show');}gr2Checked=true;var fb=document.getElementById('gr2-fb');var pct=Math.round(correct/total*100);var msg='';if(pct===100)msg='🎉 Отлично! Все ответы верные!';else if(pct>=80)msg='👍 Хороший результат! Обрати внимание на ошибки.';else if(pct>=60)msg='💪 Неплохо, но стоит повторить времена глаголов.';else msg='📚 Повтори правила употребления времен и попробуй ещё раз.';fb.innerHTML='<b>Результат: '+correct+'/'+total+' ('+pct+'%)</b><div class="gr2-score"><div class="gr2-score-item" style="color:#166534">✅ Верно: '+correct+'</div><div class="gr2-score-item" style="color:#b91c1c">❌ Неверно: '+(total-correct)+'</div></div><p style="margin-top:10px">'+msg+'</p>';fb.className='gr2-fb show '+(correct===total?'correct':'incorrect');};window.resetGR2=function(){gr2Checked=false;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('gr2-q'+q);input.value='';input.classList.remove('correct-input','wrong-input','no-hint');input.previousElementSibling.style.opacity='1';input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.remove('correct','wrong');sec2.querySelector('.gr2-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.remove('show');}document.getElementById('gr2-fb').className='gr2-fb';};})();</script>
