class="gr4-wrap"><div class="gr4-box"><h2 class="gr4-title">📝 Задание 4: Grammar - Conditionals & Wishes</h2><div class="gr4-section" id="sec-gr4"><h3>🔀 Поставь глагол в нужную форму</h3><div class="gr4-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Прочитай предложения и впиши правильную форму глагола в поле ввода. Используй: Zero, 1st, 2nd, 3rd Conditionals, а также конструкции <i>I wish / If only / It's (high) time</i>. Исходный глагол показан в поле — замени его на нужную форму.</div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="1"><span class="gr4-q-num">1</span><span class="gr4-q-text">My brother has a very predictable morning routine every day. When he <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">WAKE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q1" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> up at 6 AM, he always starts with a glass of water and some light exercise. This simple habit helps him feel energetic throughout the day.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="1">💡 <b>wakes</b> — <b>Zero Conditional</b> (придаточная часть). Союз «When» и маркер «always» указывают на регулярное действие. Обе части в Present Simple. he → wake + s (3-е лицо ед.ч.).</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="2"><span class="gr4-q-num">2</span><span class="gr4-q-text">Our team has an important presentation scheduled for next Monday. If we <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">NOT PREPARE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q2" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> all the materials this weekend, we will definitely fail in front of the clients. Everyone needs to take their responsibilities seriously.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="2">💡 <b>don't prepare</b> — <b>First Conditional</b> (придаточная часть с if). Главная часть с «will fail» указывает на реальное будущее. В if-части: Present Simple (отрицание: don't + V1).</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="3"><span class="gr4-q-num">3</span><span class="gr4-q-text">The traffic in the city center is terrible during rush hour every morning. You <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">BE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q3" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> late for your important appointment if you leave the house after 8 AM. Setting your alarm clock earlier is the only solution.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="3">💡 <b>will be</b> — <b>First Conditional</b> (главная часть). В if-части Present Simple («if you leave»), в главной — Future Simple (will + V1).</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="4"><span class="gr4-q-num">4</span><span class="gr4-q-text">I spend almost two hours commuting to work every single day. If I <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">HAVE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q4" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> a car, I would save a lot of time and arrive at the office much faster. Unfortunately, public transport is the only option I can afford right now.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="4">💡 <b>had</b> — <b>Second Conditional</b> (придаточная часть с if). Главная часть с «would save» указывает на нереальное настоящее. В if-части: Past Simple (have → had).</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="5"><span class="gr4-q-num">5</span><span class="gr4-q-text">My colleague constantly forgets about his daily tasks and misses deadlines. He <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">BE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q5" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> much more productive at work if he used a proper planner instead of relying on his memory. A simple timetable could change his routine completely.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="5"> <b>would be</b> — <b>Second Conditional</b> (главная часть). В if-части Past Simple («if he used»), в главной — would + V1.</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="6"><span class="gr4-q-num">6</span><span class="gr4-q-text">I was completely exhausted after working overtime last Friday evening. If I <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">NOT STAY</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q6" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> at the office until midnight, I would have had enough energy to go to the gym. Now I feel terrible and need to rest all weekend.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="6">💡 <b>hadn't stayed</b> — <b>Third Conditional</b> (придаточная часть с if). Главная часть с «would have had» и маркер «last Friday» указывают на нереальное прошлое. Отрицание в Past Perfect: hadn't + V3 (stay → stayed).</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="7"><span class="gr4-q-num">7</span><span class="gr4-q-text">The project deadline was yesterday, but our team did not finish in time. We <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">RECEIVE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q7" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> a bonus from the manager if we had completed all the tasks before the deadline. This is a very disappointing situation for everyone.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="7">💡 <b>would have received</b> — <b>Third Conditional</b> (главная часть). В if-части Past Perfect («if we had completed»), в главной — would have + V3.</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="8"><span class="gr4-q-num">8</span><span class="gr4-q-text">I always feel sleepy during my morning classes at university. I really wish I <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">BE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q8" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> more disciplined about going to bed earlier every night. My current sleeping schedule is completely unrealistic and unhealthy.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="8">💡 <b>were</b> — <b>I wish + Past Simple</b> (сожаление о настоящем). Я хочу, чтобы сейчас я был более дисциплинированным, но это не так. С глаголом to be после wish используем <b>were</b> для всех лиц (сослагательное наклонение).</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="9"><span class="gr4-q-num">9</span><span class="gr4-q-text">I missed my doctor's appointment this morning because of heavy traffic. If only I <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">LEAVE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q9" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> the house thirty minutes earlier, I would not have wasted the entire morning waiting in the clinic. Next time I will plan my commute more carefully.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="9"> <b>had left</b> — <b>If only + Past Perfect</b> (сожаление о прошлом, более эмоциональное, чем I wish). Я жалею, что сегодня утром не выехал раньше. Past Perfect: had + V3 (leave → left, неправильный глагол).</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="10"><span class="gr4-q-num">10</span><span class="gr4-q-text">You have been studying for your English exam for eight hours without a break. It is high time you <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">TAKE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q10" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> a rest and went for a short walk outside. Your brain needs some time to process all the new information.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="10">💡 <b>took</b> — <b>It's (high) time + Past Simple</b>. Несмотря на форму прошедшего времени, смысл относится к настоящему или ближайшему будущему («тебе давно пора это сделать»). take → took (неправильный глагол).</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="11"><span class="gr4-q-num">11</span><span class="gr4-q-text">I missed the submission deadline for my essay yesterday evening. I really wish I <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">COMPLETE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q11" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> it before the end of the working day. Now I have to ask the professor for an extension.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="11">💡 <b>had completed</b> — <b>I wish + Past Perfect</b> (сожаление о прошлом). Я жалею, что вчера не завершил эссе до конца рабочего дня. Маркер «yesterday evening» и контекст «missed the deadline» указывают на прошлое. Past Perfect: had + V3 (complete → completed).</div></div><div class="gr4-q" data-q="12"><span class="gr4-q-num">12</span><span class="gr4-q-text">My daily schedule is completely chaotic and I can never find time for exercise. If only I <span class="gr4-input-wrap"><span class="gr4-input-hint">BE</span><input type="text" class="gr4-input" id="gr4-q12" placeholder="" oninput="gr4HideHint(this)"></span> more organized with my routine, I would feel much healthier and more energetic throughout the day.</span><div class="gr4-expl" data-expl="12">💡 <b>were</b> — <b>If only + Past Simple</b> (сожаление о настоящем, более эмоциональное, чем I wish). Я хочу, чтобы сейчас я был более организованным, но это не так. Контекст «is completely chaotic» указывает на настоящее. С глаголом to be после if only используем <b>were</b> для всех лиц (сослагательное наклонение).</div></div><button class="gr4-btn check" onclick="checkGR4()">✓ Check Answers</button><button class="gr4-btn reset" onclick="resetGR4()">↺ Reset</button><div id="gr4-fb" class="gr4-fb"></div></div></div></div><script>(function(){var gr4Answers={'1':'wakes','2':"don't prepare",'3':'will be','4':'had','5':'would be','6':"hadn't stayed",'7':'would have received','8':'were','9':'had left','10':'took','11':'had completed','12':'were'};var gr4Checked=false;var sec4=document.getElementById('sec-gr4');function normalizeGr4(s){return s.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,' ').replace(/['']/g,"").trim();}window.gr4HideHint=function(input){var hint=input.previousElementSibling;if(hint&&hint.classList.contains('gr4-input-hint')){if(input.value.length>0){hint.style.opacity='0';input.classList.add('no-hint');}else{hint.style.opacity='1';input.classList.remove('no-hint');}}};window.checkGR4=function(){if(gr4Checked)return;var correct=0;var total=12;for(var q=1;q<=12;q++){var input=document.getElementById('gr4-q'+q);var userAnswer=normalizeGr4(input.value);var correctAnswer=normalizeGr4(gr4Answers[q]);if(userAnswer===correctAnswer){correct++;input.classList.add('correct-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('correct');}else{input.classList.add('wrong-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('wrong');}sec4.querySelector('.gr4-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.add('show');}gr4Checked=true;var fb=document.getElementById('gr4-fb');var pct=Math.round(correct/total*100);var msg='';if(pct===100)msg='🎉 Отлично! Все ответы верные!';else if(pct>=80)msg='👍 Хороший результат! Обрати внимание на ошибки.';else if(pct>=60)msg='💪 Неплохо, но стоит повторить Conditionals и Wishes.';else msg='📚 Повтори правила употребления условных предложений и попробуй ещё раз.';fb.innerHTML='<b>Результат: '+correct+'/'+total+' ('+pct+'%)</b><div class="gr4-score"><div class="gr4-score-item" style="color:#166534">✅ Верно: '+correct+'</div><div class="gr4-score-item" style="color:#b91c1c">❌ Неверно: '+(total-correct)+'</div></div><p style="margin-top:10px">'+msg+'</p>';fb.className='gr4-fb show '+(correct===total?'correct':'incorrect');};window.resetGR4=function(){gr4Checked=false;for(var q=1;q<=12;q++){var input=document.getElementById('gr4-q'+q);input.value='';input.classList.remove('correct-input','wrong-input','no-hint');input.previousElementSibling.style.opacity='1';input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.remove('correct','wrong');sec4.querySelector('.gr4-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.remove('show');}document.getElementById('gr4-fb').className='gr4-fb';};})();</script>
<!-- ================= Задание 5 ================= -->
<style>.gr5-wrap{max-width:900px;margin:20px auto;font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;background:#f8f9fa;padding:25px;border-radius:16px;color:#334155}.gr5-box{background:#fff;padding:25px;border-radius:16px;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)}.gr5-title{color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 20px 0;font-size:24px;font-weight:700}.gr5-section{background:#fff;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:14px;padding:20px;margin:20px 0}.gr5-section h3{font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 15px 0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}.gr5-instruction{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#dbeafe,#bfdbfe);border:2px solid #3b82f6;border-radius:12px;padding:15px;margin:15px 0;font-size:14px;color:#1e40af;line-height:1.6}.gr5-q{margin:16px 0;padding:16px;background:#f8fafc;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:10px;transition:.3s}.gr5-q.correct{background:#f0fdf4;border-color:#22c55e}.gr5-q.wrong{background:#fef2f2;border-color:#ef4444}.gr5-q-num{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:28px;height:28px;background:#8b5cf6;color:#fff;border-radius:50%;font-weight:700;font-size:14px;margin-right:8px}.gr5-q-text{font-size:15px;line-height:1.7;color:#334155}.gr5-input-wrap{position:relative;display:inline-block;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 4px}.gr5-input-hint{position:absolute;left:10px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);font-weight:600;color:#9ca3af;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .2s;font-size:14px;font-style:italic;letter-spacing:0.5px}.gr5-input{display:inline-block;width:240px;padding:6px 10px 6px 10px;border:2px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:6px;font-size:14px;vertical-align:middle;transition:.2s;background:#fff}.gr5-input:focus{outline:none;border-color:#8b5cf6;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(139,92,246,.1)}.gr5-input.no-hint{padding-left:10px}.gr5-input.correct-input{border-color:#22c55e;background:#dcfce7;color:#166534;font-weight:600}.gr5-input.wrong-input{border-color:#ef4444;background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c}.gr5-expl{display:none;margin-top:10px;padding:10px 14px;background:#fff;border-left:4px solid #f59e0b;border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;color:#78350f}.gr5-expl.show{display:block;animation:fadeIn5 .3s}.gr5-btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:10px 20px;margin:15px 10px 15px 0;border:none;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;transition:.2s;color:#fff}.gr5-btn:hover{transform:translateY(-1px)}.gr5-btn.check{background:#10b981;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(16,185,129,.3)}.gr5-btn.check:hover{background:#059669}.gr5-btn.reset{background:#64748b;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(100,116,139,.3)}.gr5-btn.reset:hover{background:#475569}.gr5-fb{display:none;margin-top:12px;padding:14px;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.6}.gr5-fb.show{display:block;animation:fadeIn5 .3s}.gr5-fb.correct{background:#dcfce7;color:#15803d;border-left:4px solid #22c55e}.gr5-fb.incorrect{background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c;border-left:4px solid #ef4444}.gr5-score{display:flex;gap:12px;margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap}.gr5-score-item{background:#fff;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0}@keyframes fadeIn5{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}@media(max-width:600px){.gr5-input{width:180px}.gr5-box{padding:15px}}</style><div class="gr5-wrap"><div class="gr5-box"><h2 class="gr5-title">📝 Задание 5: Grammar - Reported Speech</h2><div class="gr5-section" id="sec-gr5"><h3>🗣️ Поставь глагол в нужную форму</h3><div class="gr5-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Прочитай предложения и впиши правильную форму глагола в поле ввода, преобразовав прямую речь в косвенную (Reported Speech). Используй правила Backshift, изменения местоимений и маркеров времени. Исходный глагол показан в поле — замени его на нужную форму.</div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="1"><span class="gr5-q-num">1</span><span class="gr5-q-text">My colleague explained his morning routine to me yesterday. He said that he <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">WAKE UP</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q1" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> at 6 AM every single day without fail. This habit helps him accomplish all his tasks before lunch.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="1">💡 <b>woke up</b> — <b>Backshift: Present Simple → Past Simple</b>. В прямой речи было: «I wake up at 6 AM». После вводного глагола в Past Simple время сдвигается на шаг назад.</div></div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="2"><span class="gr5-q-num">2</span><span class="gr5-q-text">I called my sister while she was getting ready for work. She told me that she <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">PRIORITIZE</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q2" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> her daily tasks at that moment. She always tries to manage her time effectively.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="2">💡 <b>was prioritizing</b> — <b>Backshift: Present Continuous → Past Continuous</b>. В прямой речи было: «I am prioritizing my tasks». she → was + V-ing.</div></div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="3"><span class="gr5-q-num">3</span><span class="gr5-q-text">The manager spoke to the team after the meeting. He proudly announced that he <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">COMPLETE</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q3" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> all the important errands before the deadline. Everyone was impressed by his productivity.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="3">💡 <b>had completed</b> — <b>Backshift: Present Perfect → Past Perfect</b>. В прямой речи было: «I have completed all the errands». had + V3.</div></div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="4"><span class="gr5-q-num">4</span><span class="gr5-q-text">My friend called me yesterday evening with bad news. She explained that she <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">MISS</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q4" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> her important appointment the previous day because she was running late. Now she has to reschedule it.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="4">💡 <b>had missed</b> — <b>Backshift: Past Simple → Past Perfect</b>. В прямой речи было: «I missed my appointment yesterday». Маркер «the previous day» (вместо yesterday).</div></div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="5"><span class="gr5-q-num">5</span><span class="gr5-q-text">The teacher spoke to us about the exam schedule next week. She assured us that she <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">PREPARE</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q5" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> a detailed timetable for all the students. We decided to stick to her plan.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="5">💡 <b>would prepare</b> — <b>Backshift: will → would</b>. В прямой речи было: «I will prepare a timetable». Future Simple меняется на Future-in-the-Past.</div></div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="6"><span class="gr5-q-num">6</span><span class="gr5-q-text">The nutritionist asked me a direct question during our consultation. She wanted to know if I <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">FOLLOW</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q6" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> a strict daily routine regularly. I had to admit that my habits were far from perfect.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="6">💡 <b>followed</b> — <b>Backshift в вопросе: Present Simple → Past Simple</b>. В прямой речи было: «Do you follow a routine?». После if используется прямой порядок слов.</div></div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="7"><span class="gr5-q-num">7</span><span class="gr5-q-text">My colleague wanted to know about my morning schedule. He asked me what time I <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">SET</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q7" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> my alarm clock every morning. I told him it was always at 6 AM sharp.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="7">💡 <b>was setting</b> — <b>Backshift в вопросе: Present Continuous → Past Continuous</b>. В прямой речи было: «What time are you setting your alarm?». Сохраняем вопросительное слово и прямой порядок слов.</div></div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="8"><span class="gr5-q-num">8</span><span class="gr5-q-text">The manager gave strict instructions before the meeting ended. He told the employee <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">PUT</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q8" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> off the unimportant tasks and focus on the deadline. The employee promised to follow the advice.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="8">💡 <b>to put</b> — <b>Повелительное наклонение в косвенной речи</b>. В прямой речи было: «Put off the unimportant tasks!». Формула: tell + object + <b>to + V1</b>.</div></div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="9"><span class="gr5-q-num">9</span><span class="gr5-q-text">My mother gave me important advice before I started university. She told me <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">NOT PUT</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q9" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> off my studies until the last minute. Her words helped me manage my time effectively.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="9">💡 <b>not to put</b> — <b>Отрицательное повелительное наклонение</b>. В прямой речи было: «Don't put off your studies!». Формула: tell + object + <b>not to + V1</b>.</div></div><div class="gr5-q" data-q="10"><span class="gr5-q-num">10</span><span class="gr5-q-text">My friend shared her frustrating experience with the new schedule. She complained that she <span class="gr5-input-wrap"><span class="gr5-input-hint">TRY</span><input type="text" class="gr5-input" id="gr5-q10" placeholder="" oninput="gr5HideHint(this)"></span> to keep track of time for weeks without success. She was still looking for a better routine.</span><div class="gr5-expl" data-expl="10"> <b>had been trying</b> — <b>Backshift: Present Perfect Continuous → Past Perfect Continuous</b>. В прямой речи было: «I have been trying to keep track of time». had been + V-ing.</div></div><button class="gr5-btn check" onclick="checkGR5()">✓ Check Answers</button><button class="gr5-btn reset" onclick="resetGR5()">↺ Reset</button><div id="gr5-fb" class="gr5-fb"></div></div></div></div><script>(function(){var gr5Answers={'1':'woke up','2':'was prioritizing','3':'had completed','4':'had missed','5':'would prepare','6':'followed','7':'was setting','8':'to put','9':'not to put','10':'had been trying'};var gr5Checked=false;var sec5=document.getElementById('sec-gr5');function normalizeGr5(s){return s.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,' ').replace(/['']/g,"").trim();}window.gr5HideHint=function(input){var hint=input.previousElementSibling;if(hint&&hint.classList.contains('gr5-input-hint')){if(input.value.length>0){hint.style.opacity='0';input.classList.add('no-hint');}else{hint.style.opacity='1';input.classList.remove('no-hint');}}};window.checkGR5=function(){if(gr5Checked)return;var correct=0;var total=10;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('gr5-q'+q);var userAnswer=normalizeGr5(input.value);var correctAnswer=normalizeGr5(gr5Answers[q]);if(userAnswer===correctAnswer){correct++;input.classList.add('correct-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('correct');}else{input.classList.add('wrong-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('wrong');}sec5.querySelector('.gr5-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.add('show');}gr5Checked=true;var fb=document.getElementById('gr5-fb');var pct=Math.round(correct/total*100);var msg='';if(pct===100)msg='🎉 Отлично! Все ответы верные!';else if(pct>=80)msg='👍 Хороший результат! Обрати внимание на ошибки.';else if(pct>=60)msg='💪 Неплохо, но стоит повторить правила Reported Speech.';else msg=' Повтори правила Backshift и преобразования вопросов/приказов и попробуй ещё раз.';fb.innerHTML='<b>Результат: '+correct+'/'+total+' ('+pct+'%)</b><div class="gr5-score"><div class="gr5-score-item" style="color:#166534">✅ Верно: '+correct+'</div><div class="gr5-score-item" style="color:#b91c1c">❌ Неверно: '+(total-correct)+'</div></div><p style="margin-top:10px">'+msg+'</p>';fb.className='gr5-fb show '+(correct===total?'correct':'incorrect');};window.resetGR5=function(){gr5Checked=false;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('gr5-q'+q);input.value='';input.classList.remove('correct-input','wrong-input','no-hint');input.previousElementSibling.style.opacity='1';input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.remove('correct','wrong');sec5.querySelector('.gr5-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.remove('show');}document.getElementById('gr5-fb').className='gr5-fb';};})();</script>



<!-- ================= PREPOSITIONS ================= -->
<div id="prepositions" class="section">
  <h1>Prepositions</h1>
<style>.dr-wrap-nouns{max-width:900px;margin:20px auto;font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;background:#f8f9fa;padding:20px;border-radius:16px;color:#334155}.dr-box-nouns{background:#fff;padding:25px;border-radius:16px;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.08);margin-bottom:20px}.dr-title-nouns{color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 15px 0}.dr-btn-nouns{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px;width:100%;padding:14px 20px;margin:10px 0 15px;border:none;border-radius:10px;font-size:16px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;transition:.2s;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.1)}.dr-btn-nouns:hover{opacity:.9;transform:translateY(-1px)}.dr-btn-nouns.learn{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#667eea,#764ba2);color:#fff}.dr-btn-nouns.test{background:#ef4444;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(239,68,68,.3)}.dr-btn-nouns.test:hover{background:#dc2626}.dr-panel-nouns{display:none;overflow:visible !important;animation:drFade .3s}.dr-panel-nouns.active{display:block}@keyframes drFade{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-8px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}.ex-card-nouns{background:#fff;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 0;margin:15px 0;overflow:visible !important}.ex-card-nouns h4{margin:0 0 15px 0;color:#2c3e50;font-size:16px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding-left:5px}.ex-item-nouns{padding:12px 15px;margin:8px class="ex-item-nouns">1) Put the clean dishes <span class="vt-word-nouns">in the cupboard<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>in the cupboard</b><br>В шкафу (место хранения)</span></span> 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item-nouns">2) There's a coffee stain <span class="vt-word-nouns">on your shirt<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>on your shirt</b><br>НА рубашке (поверхность)</span></span> ☕</div><div class="ex-item-nouns">3) Throw the wrapper <span class="vt-word-nouns">in the bin<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>in the bin</b><br>В мусорное ведро</span></span> 🗑️</div><div class="ex-item-nouns">4) Use this detergent <span class="vt-word-nouns">for white clothes<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>for white clothes</b><br>ДЛЯ белых вещей (назначение)</span></span> 🧴</div><div class="ex-item-nouns">5) Sweep the kitchen floor <span class="vt-word-nouns">with a broom<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>with a broom</b><br>С помощью метлы (инструмент)</span></span> 🧹</div><div class="ex-item-nouns">6) Don't forget to vacuum <span class="vt-word-nouns">under the sofa<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>under the sofa</b><br>ПОД диваном (место)</span></span> 🛋️</div><div class="ex-item-nouns">7) Hang the wet clothes <span class="vt-word-nouns">on the line<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>on the line</b><br>НА верёвке (сушка)</span></span> 👕</div><div class="ex-item-nouns">8) Wipe the counter <span class="vt-word-nouns">with a damp cloth<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>with a damp cloth</b><br>С помощью влажной тряпки (инструмент)</span></span> 🧽</div><div class="ex-item-nouns">9) The mop is stored <span class="vt-word-nouns">behind the door<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>behind the door</b><br>ЗА дверью (место)</span></span> 🚪</div><div class="ex-item-nouns">10) Sort the laundry <span class="vt-word-nouns">into three piles<span class="vt-tooltip-nouns"><b>into three piles</b><br>НА три кучки (разделение)</span></span> 👕</div></div><div class="practice-box-nouns"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: Saturday Cleaning Day 🧹</h4><div class="practice-text-nouns">Every Saturday, I do a big cleaning. First, I take the clean dishes and put them <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">in</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 в шкаф</span></span> the cupboard. Then I noticed there's a coffee stain <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">on</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 на рубашке</span></span> my shirt — I need to wash it! I throw the empty wrapper <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">in</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 в мусорку</span></span> the bin. For the laundry, I use a special detergent <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">for</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 для белых вещей</span></span> white clothes. I sweep the kitchen floor <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">with</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 с помощью метлы</span></span> a broom and vacuum <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">under</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 под диваном</span></span> the sofa. After washing, I hang the wet clothes <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">on</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 на верёвке</span></span> the line outside. I wipe the kitchen counter <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">with</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 с влажной тряпкой</span></span> a damp cloth. The mop is stored <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">behind</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 за дверью</span></span> the door. Finally, I sort the laundry <span class="mask-wrapper-nouns" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-nouns">into</span><span class="mask-overlay-nouns"></span><span class="tooltip-nouns">📌 на три кучки</span></span> three piles: whites, colors, and darks.</div></div><div class="warn-block-nouns"><strong>⚠️ Важно: Предлоги места и инструмента</strong><b>🔑 IN — внутри чего-то:</b><br>• <b>in the cupboard</b>, <b>in the bin</b><br><i>IN = "в" (внутри контейнера/места хранения)</i><br><br><b>🔑 ON — на поверхности:</b><br>• <b>on your shirt</b>, <b>on the line</b><br><i>ON = "на" (поверхность, линия)</i><br><br><b>🔑 WITH — с помощью инструмента:</b><br>• <b>with a broom</b>, <b>with a damp cloth</b><br><i>WITH = "с помощью" (инструмент)</i><br><br><b>🔑 FOR — для назначения:</b><br>• <b>for white clothes</b><br><i>FOR = "для" (цель, назначение)</i><br><br><b>🔑 UNDER/BEHIND — местоположение:</b><br>• <b>under the sofa</b> (под), <b>behind the door</b> (за)<br><i>UNDER = "под", BEHIND = "за"</i><br><br><b>🔑 INTO — направление/разделение:</b><br>• <b>into three piles</b><br><i>INTO = "на" (разделение на части)</i></div></div><button class="dr-btn-nouns test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test: Nouns</button><div class="dr-panel-nouns"><h

class="ex-item-verbs">8) Let's <span class="vt-word-verbs">tidy up the garage during<span class="vt-tooltip-verbs"><b>tidy up during</b><br>прибраться ВО ВРЕМЯ (период)</span></span> the weekend 🏠</div><div class="ex-item-verbs">9) <span class="vt-word-verbs">Load the dishwasher with<span class="vt-tooltip-verbs"><b>load with</b><br>загрузить С (инструмент/содержимое)</span></span> dirty dishes after dinner 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item-verbs">10) <span class="vt-word-verbs">Unload the groceries from<span class="vt-tooltip-verbs"><b>unload from</b><br>выгрузить ИЗ (источник)</span></span> the car when you get home 🛒</div></div><div class="warn-block-verbs"><strong>⚠️ Важно: Предлоги с глаголами уборки</strong><b>🔑 UNTIL — до результата/времени:</b><br>• <b>scrub UNTIL</b> it's clean, <b>wait UNTIL</b> tomorrow<br><i>UNTIL = "до" (пока не произойдёт что-то)</i><br><br><b>🔑 WITH — с помощью/содержимое:</b><br>• <b>polish WITH</b> a cloth, <b>load WITH</b> dishes<br><i>WITH = "с помощью" или "с" (инструмент/содержимое)</i><br><br><b>🔑 AFTER/BEFORE — время:</b><br>• <b>wipe down AFTER</b> cooking, <b>dust BEFORE</b> guests arrive<br><i>AFTER = "после", BEFORE = "перед"</i><br><br><b>🔑 UP — полностью/вверх:</b><br>• <b>sweep UP</b> (подмести полностью), <b>tidy UP</b> (прибраться)<br><i>UP = усиливает глагол (сделать полностью)</i><br><br><b>🔑 WHILE/DURING — одновременно/во время:</b><br>• <b>fold WHILE</b> watching TV, <b>tidy DURING</b> the weekend<br><i>WHILE = "во время" (одновременно), DURING = "в течение"</i><br><br><b>🔑 FOR — цель/назначение:</b><br>• <b>iron FOR</b> the interview<br><i>FOR = "для" (цель)</i><br><br><b>🔑 FROM — источник:</b><br>• <b>unload FROM</b> the car<br><i>FROM = "из" scrubbed the bathtub ___ it was sparkling clean.</div><input type="radio" name="v_q1" id="v_q1a" class="qz-r-verbs corr"><label for="v_q1a" class="qz-opt-verbs">A) until</label><input type="radio" name="v_q1" id="v_q1b" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q1b" class="qz-opt-verbs">B) for</label><input type="radio" name="v_q1" id="v_q1c" class="qz-r-verbs wrng"><label for="v_q1c" class="vt-tooltip-phrasal"><b>put away</b><br>убирать НА МЕСТО (AWAY = в сторону)</span></span> the clean dishes in the cupboard 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item-phrasal">4) Don't forget to <span class="vt-word-phrasal">throw out<span class="vt-tooltip-phrasal"><b>throw out</b><br>выбрасывать (OUT = наружу)</span></span> the expired food from the fridge 🗑️</div><div class="ex-item-phrasal">5) <span class="vt-word-phrasal">Wipe down<span class="vt-tooltip-phrasal"><b>wipe down</b><br>протирать (DOWN = поверхность)</span></span> the kitchen counters with a damp cloth 🧽</div><div class="ex-item-phrasal">6) Let's <span class="vt-word-phrasal">clear away<span class="vt-tooltip-phrasal"><b>clear away</b><br>убирать, расчищать (AWAY = прочь)</span></span> the dinner plates from the table 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item-phrasal">7) I need to <span class="vt-word-phrasal">sort out<span class="vt-tooltip-phrasal"><b>sort out</b><br>сортировать, разбирать (OUT = разделять)</span></span> the laundry into whites and colors 👕</div><div class="ex-item-phrasal">8) <span class="vt-word-phrasal">Hang up<span class="vt-tooltip-phrasal"><b>hang up</b><br>вешать (UP = наверх)</span></span> the clean shirts in the wardrobe 👔</div><div class="ex-item-phrasal">9) The bathroom looks terrible — we need to <span class="vt-word-phrasal">do it over<span class="vt-tooltip-phrasal"><b>do over</b><br>переделывать (OVER = заново)</span></span> completely! 🚿</div><div class="ex-item-phrasal">10) Can you <span class="vt-word-phrasal">take out<span class="vt-tooltip-phrasal"><b>take out</b><br>выносить (OUT = наружу)</span></span> the rubbish, please? 🗑️</div></div><div class="warn-block-phrasal"><strong>⚠️ Важно: Частицы фразовых глаголов</strong><b>🔑 UP — полностью, до конца:</b><br>• <b>clean UP</b>, <b>tidy UP</b>, <b>hang UP</b><br><i>UP = усилить глагол, сделать полностью</i><br><br><b>🔑 AWAY — на место, убирать:</b><br>• <b>put AWAY</b>, <b>clear AWAY</b><br><i>AWAY = "прочь", "на своё место"</i><br><br><b>🔑 OUT — наружу, выбрасывать:</b><br>• <b>throw OUT</b>, <b>take OUT</b>, <b>sort OUT</b><br><i>OUT = "наружу", "изнутри"</i><br><br><b>🔑 DOWN — вниз, поверхность:</b><br>• <b>wipe DOWN</b><br><i>DOWN = "вниз", протирать поверхность</i><br><br><b>🔑 OVER — заново, переделывать:</b><br>• <b>do OVER</b><br><i>OVER = "снова", "заново"</i></div></div><button class="dr-btn-phrasal test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test: Phrasal Verbs</button><div class="dr-panel-phrasal"><h3 откладывать.</div></div><div class="qz-card-phrasal"><div class="qz-num-phrasal">4</div><div class="qz-sent-phrasal">Wipe ___ the kitchen counters with a damp cloth.</div><input type="radio" Верно! <b>Wipe DOWN</b> = протирать поверхность (DOWN = вниз).</div><div class="qz-fb-phrasal no">❌ wipe up = вытирать разлитое; wipe out = стирать/уничтожать.</div></div><div class="vt-tooltip-adjectives"><b>tired after</b><br>усталый ПОСЛЕ (действия)</span></span> vacuuming the whole house 🧹</div><div class="ex-item-adjectives">5) She's <span class="vt-word-adjectives">happy with<span class="vt-tooltip-adjectives"><b>happy with</b><br>доволен ЧЕМ-ТО (результатом)</span></span> how clean the bathroom is ✨</div><div class="ex-item-adjectives">6) I'm <span class="vt-word-adjectives">bored with<span class="vt-tooltip-adjectives"><b>bored with</b><br>скучно ОТ (действия)</span></span> ironing shirts every week 👔</div><div class="ex-item-adjectives">7) He's <span class="vt-word-adjectives">afraid of<span class="vt-tooltip-adjectives"><b>afraid of</b><br>боится ЧЕГО-ТО</span></span> breaking the expensive dishes 🍽️</div><div class="ex-item-adjectives">8) She's <span class="vt-word-adjectives">proud of<span class="vt-tooltip-adjectives"><b>proud of</b><br>гордится ЧЕМ-ТО</span></span> her spotless kitchen 🏆</div><div class="ex-item-adjectives">9) I'm <span class="vt-word-adjectives">fed up with<span class="vt-tooltip-adjectives"><b>fed up with</b><br>достало, надоело ЧТО-ТО</span></span> cleaning the bathroom every day! 😤</div><div class="ex-item-adjectives">10) We need to be <span class="vt-word-adjectives">ready for<span class="vt-tooltip-adjectives"><b>ready for</b><br>готов К (событию)</span></span> the guests arriving tonight 🎉</div></div><div class="practice-box-adjectives"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: My Helpful Sister 👩‍👧</h4><div class="practice-text-adjectives">My sister is very <b>good</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">at</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 хорош В</span></span> organizing the kitchen — everything has its place. She's always <b>careful</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">with</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 осторожный С</span></span> the fragile plates and glasses. She's <b>responsible</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">for</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 ответственная ЗА</span></span> most of the household chores. After vacuuming the whole house, she's usually <b>tired</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">after</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 усталая ПОСЛЕ</span></span> but <b>happy</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">with</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 довольна ЧЕМ-ТО</span></span> the result. Sometimes she's <b>bored</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">with</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 скучно ОТ</span></span> ironing, but she does it anyway. She's not <b>afraid</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">of</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 боится ЧЕГО-ТО</span></span> hard work and she's <b>proud</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">of</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 гордится ЧЕМ-ТО</span></span> her spotless home. I'm <b>fed up</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">with</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 достало ЧТО-ТО</span></span> cleaning the bathroom, but she does it every week. We need to be <b>ready</b> <span class="mask-wrapper-adjectives" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-adjectives">for</span><span class="mask-overlay-adjectives"></span><span class="tooltip-adjectives">📌 готовы К</span></span> the guests arriving tonight!</div></div><div class="warn-block-adjectives"><strong>⚠️ Важно: Предлоги с прилагательными</strong><b>🔑 AT — способности:</b><br>• <b>good AT</b> cleaning, <b>bad AT</b> organizing<br><i>AT = "в" (способности, навыки)</i><br><br><b>🔑 WITH — с чем-то:</b><br>• <b>careful WITH</b> dishes, <b>happy WITH</b> result, <b>bored WITH</b> ironing, <b>fed up WITH</b> cleaning<br><i>WITH = "с/от/чем-то" (взаимодействие)</i><br><br><b>🔑 FOR — ответственность/готовность:</b><br>• <b>responsible FOR</b> chores, <b>ready FOR</b> guests<br><i>FOR = "за/к" (ответственность или готовность)</i><br><br><b>🔑 AFTER — после действия:</b><br>• <b>tired AFTER</b> vacuuming<br><i>AFTER = "после" (временная последовательность)</i><br><br><b>🔑 OF — эмоции/чувства:</b><br>• <b>afraid OF</b> breaking, <b>proud OF</b> the result<br><i>OF = "чего-то" (эмоциональная реакция)</i></div></div><button class="dr-btn-adjectives test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test: Adjectives</button><div class="dr-panel-adjectives"><h3 class="ex-item-collocations">1) In our family, we <span class="vt-word-collocations">share responsibilities with<span class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>share with</b><br>делить С class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>clear a mess on</b><br>убирать беспорядок НА (поверхности)</span></span> the floor immediately 🧽</div><div class="ex-item-collocations">3) Every weekend, we <span class="vt-word-collocations">tackle clutter around<span class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>tackle clutter around</b><br>разбираться с хламом ВОКРУГ</span></span> the house 📦</div><div class="ex-item-collocations">4) I always <span class="vt-word-collocations">sort laundry into<span class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>sort into</b><br>сортировать НА (категории)</span></span> three piles: whites, colors, and darks 👕</div><div class="ex-item-collocations">5) My mom <span class="vt-word-collocations">wipes surfaces with<span class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>wipe with</b><br>протирать С ПОМОЩЬЮ (инструмент)</span></span> a damp cloth after cooking 🧽</div><div class="ex-item-collocations">6) Dad <span class="vt-word-collocations">scrubs stains off<span class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>scrub off</b><br>отчищать С (поверхности)</span></span> the carpet when needed 🧹</div><div class="ex-item-collocations">7) We always <span class="vt-word-collocations">put things in<span class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>put in</b><br>класть В (место)</span></span> their place after using them 📋</div><div class="ex-item-collocations">8) Someone has to <span class="vt-word-collocations">keep house for<span class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>keep house for</b><br>вести хозяйство ДЛЯ (кого-то)</span></span> the family 🏠</div><div class="ex-item-collocations">9) We <span class="vt-word-collocations">maintain cleanliness throughout<span class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>maintain throughout</b><br>поддерживать ПО ВСЕМУ (месту)</span></span> the house ✨</div><div class="ex-item-collocations">10) I <span class="vt-word-collocations">fold clothes after<span class="vt-tooltip-collocations"><b>fold after</b><br>складывать ПОСЛЕ (действия)</span></span> washing them 👔</div></div><div class="practice-box-collocations"><h4>🎯 Practice Text: A Clean Home 🏡</h4><div class="practice-text-collocations">In our family, we <b>share</b> responsibilities <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">with</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 делить С</span></span> each other — everyone has their own chores. When someone spills juice, we <b>clear</b> a mess <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">on</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 убирать НА</span></span> the floor immediately. Every weekend, we <b>tackle</b> clutter <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">around</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 разбираться ВОКРУГ</span></span> the house together. I always <b>sort</b> the laundry <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">into</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 сортировать НА</span></span> three piles: whites, colors, and darks. My mom <b>wipes</b> the kitchen surfaces <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">with</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 протирать С ПОМОЩЬЮ</span></span> a damp cloth after cooking every evening. Dad <b>scrubs</b> the stains <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">off</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 отчищать С</span></span> the carpet when needed. We always <b>put</b> things <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">in</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 класть В</span></span> their place after using them. Someone has to <b>keep</b> house <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">for</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 вести хозяйство ДЛЯ</span></span> the family — it's a big responsibility! We <b>maintain</b> cleanliness <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">throughout</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 поддерживать ПО ВСЕМУ</span></span> the house, not just in one room. Finally, I <b>fold</b> the clothes <span class="mask-wrapper-collocations" onclick="this.classList.toggle('revealed')"><span class="mask-content-collocations">after</span><span class="mask-overlay-collocations"></span><span class="tooltip-collocations">📌 складывать ПОСЛЕ</span></span> washing them and put them in the wardrobe.</div></div><div class="warn-block-collocations"><strong>⚠️ Важно: Предлоги места и инструмента</strong><b>🔑 WITH — двойное значение:</b><br>• <b>share WITH</b> family (с кем-то) • <b>wipe WITH</b> a cloth (с помощью)<br><br><b>🔑 ON / IN / OFF — поверхности:</b><br>• <b>ON</b> the floor (на) • <b>IN</b> their place (в) • <b>scrub OFF</b> stains (с поверхности)<br><br><b>🔑 THROUGHOUT / AROUND / IN:</b><br>• <b>THROUGHOUT</b> = по всему • <b>AROUND</b> = вокруг • <b>IN</b> = внутри<br><br><b>🔑 INTO — разделение:</b><br>• <b>sort INTO</b> three piles = сортировать НА три кучки</div></div><button class="dr-btn-collocations test" onclick="this.nextElementSibling.classList.toggle('active')">🧪 Test: Collocations</button><div class="dr-panel-collocations"><h3 class="qz-title-collocations">Test: Collocations — Предлоги</h3><p class="qz-sub-collocations">Выберите правильный предлог:</p><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">1</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">We share responsibilities ___ each other.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q1" id="c_q1a" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q1a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) with</label><input type="radio" name="c_q1" id="c_q1b" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q1b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) for</label><input type="radio" name="c_q1" id="c_q1c" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q1c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) at</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>Share WITH</b> = делить с кем-то.</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ share for = делить ДЛЯ (не с кем-то); share at = не используется.</div></div><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">2</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">We clear a mess ___ the floor immediately.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q2" id="c_q2a" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q2a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) in</label><input type="radio" name="c_q2" id="c_q2b" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q2b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) on</label><input type="radio" name="c_q2" id="c_q2c" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q2c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) at</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>ON</b> the floor = на полу (поверхность).</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ in the floor = в полу (не на поверхности); at the floor = у пола (не на нём).</div></div><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">3</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">We tackle clutter ___ the house every weekend.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q3" id="c_q3a" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q3a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) in</label><input type="radio" name="c_q3" id="c_q3b" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q3b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) around</label><input type="radio" name="c_q3" id="c_q3c" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q3c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) at</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>AROUND</b> the house = по всему дому.</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ in the house = в доме (не по всему); at the house = у дома (не внутри).</div></div><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">4</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">I sort the laundry ___ three piles.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q4" id="c_q4a" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q4a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) in</label><input type="radio" name="c_q4" id="c_q4b" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q4b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) on</label><input type="radio" name="c_q4" id="c_q4c" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q4c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) into</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>Sort INTO</b> = сортировать на (разделение).</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ sort in = сортировать В (не разделять); sort on = не используется.</div></div><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">5</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">My mom wipes surfaces ___ a damp cloth.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q5" id="c_q5a" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q5a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) in</label><input type="radio" name="c_q5" id="c_q5b" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q5b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) with</label><input type="radio" name="c_q5" id="c_q5c" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q5c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) for</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>WITH</b> a cloth = с помощью тряпки (инструмент).</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ in a cloth = в тряпке (не с помощью); for a cloth = для тряпки (не инструмент).</div></div><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">6</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">Dad scrubs stains ___ the carpet.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q6" id="c_q6a" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q6a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) in</label><input type="radio" name="c_q6" id="c_q6b" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q6b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) with</label><input type="radio" name="c_q6" id="c_q6c" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q6c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) off</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>Scrub OFF</b> = отчищать с поверхности.</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ scrub in = отчищать В (не с поверхности); scrub with = отчищать С ПОМОЩЬЮ (инструмент, не поверхность).</div></div><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">7</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">We put things ___ their place after using them.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q7" id="c_q7a" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q7a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) in</label><input type="radio" name="c_q7" id="c_q7b" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q7b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) on</label><input type="radio" name="c_q7" id="c_q7c" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q7c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) at</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>IN</b> their place = на своё место (внутри).</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ on their place = НА своём месте (поверхность); at their place = У своего места (рядом).</div></div><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">8</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">Someone has to keep house ___ the family.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q8" id="c_q8a" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q8a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) with</label><input type="radio" name="c_q8" id="c_q8b" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q8b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) for</label><input type="radio" name="c_q8" id="c_q8c" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q8c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) at</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>FOR</b> the family = для семьи (назначение).</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ with the family = С семьёй (совместность, не назначение); at the family = не используется.</div></div><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">9</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">We maintain cleanliness ___ the house.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q9" id="c_q9a" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q9a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) in</label><input type="radio" name="c_q9" id="c_q9b" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q9b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) throughout</label><input type="radio" name="c_q9" id="c_q9c" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q9c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) at</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>THROUGHOUT</b> the house = по всему дому.</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ in the house = в доме (не по всему); at the house = у дома (не внутри).</div></div><div class="qz-card-collocations"><div class="qz-num-collocations">10</div><div class="qz-sent-collocations">I fold the clothes ___ washing them.</div><input type="radio" name="c_q10" id="c_q10a" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q10a" class="qz-opt-collocations">A) before</label><input type="radio" name="c_q10" id="c_q10b" class="qz-r-collocations corr"><label for="c_q10b" class="qz-opt-collocations">B) after</label><input type="radio" name="c_q10" id="c_q10c" class="qz-r-collocations wrng"><label for="c_q10c" class="qz-opt-collocations">C) during</label><div class="qz-fb-collocations ok">✅ Верно! <b>AFTER</b> washing = после стирки.</div><div class="qz-fb-collocations no">❌ before washing = ПЕРЕД стиркой (логически неверно); during washing = ВО ВРЕМЯ стирки (невозможно).</div></div></div></div></div></div>

class="ex-title">📖 Задание 1: Matching Headings</h2><div class="ex-section" id="sec-dd1"><h3>🎯 Задание: Установите соответствие</h3><p style="margin-bottom:15px;color:#64748b;font-size:14px">Перетащите заголовки (1–8) в пропуски текстов (A–G). Один заголовок лишний.<br><em>💡 Подсказка: нажмите на вставленный заголовок, чтобы вернуть его в банк слов.</em></p><div class="word-bank"><h4>💡 Headings (Перетащите отсюда):</h4><div class="word-bank-items" id="dd1Words"><div class="dd1-word" draggable="true" data-word="h1">1. Less effort with gadgets.</div><div class="dd1-word" draggable="true" data-word="h2">2. Sharing the load.</div><div class="dd1-word" draggable="true" data-word="h3">3. Small steps, tidy home.</div><div class="dd1-word" draggable="true" data-word="h4">4. The weekend deep clean.</div><div class="dd1-word" draggable="true" data-word="h5">5. Teaching responsibility early.</div><div class="dd1-word" draggable="true" data-word="h6">6. Procrastination causes trouble.</div><div class="dd1-word" draggable="true" data-word="h7">7. Everything in its place.</div><div class="dd1-word" draggable="true" data-word="h8">8. Calling in the experts.</div></div></div><div class="dd-slots"><div class="dd-slot"><div class="dd-slot-header"><span class="dd-num">A</span><div class="dd1-drop-zone" data-slot="0"></div></div><span class="dd-text">Many families find that sharing household tasks makes cleaning easier for everyone. When partners divide chores like cooking, laundry, and tidying, no one person feels overwhelmed. Children can also pitch in by setting the table or putting away toys. This teamwork helps keep the house tidy without too much effort.</span></div><div class="dd-slot"><div class="dd-slot-header"><span class="dd-num">B</span><div class="dd1-drop-zone" data-slot="1"></div></div><span class="dd-text">For some people, using modern appliances makes chores much simpler. Dishwashers, washing machines, and vacuum cleaners save time and energy. While you still need to wipe surfaces and sweep floors, these tools help maintain cleanliness with less elbow grease.</span></div><div class="dd-slot"><div class="dd-slot-header"><span class="dd-num">C</span><div class="dd1-drop-zone" data-slot="2"></div></div><span class="dd-text">A simple routine of small daily efforts can keep a home tidy. Making the bed each morning, wiping kitchen surfaces after cooking, and putting things away as you go prevents clutter from building up. Spending just 15 minutes each evening tidying helps maintain order.</span></div><div class="dd-slot"><div class="dd-slot-header"><span class="dd-num">D</span><div class="dd1-drop-zone" data-slot="3"></div></div><span class="dd-text">Some prefer to do a thorough clean once a week rather than tidying a little every day. They spend Saturday morning scrubbing bathrooms, wiping down all surfaces, and sorting out cupboards. It's hard work, but they love the feeling of a fresh start and a clean slate.</span></div><div class="dd-slot"><div class="dd-slot-header"><span class="dd-num">E</span><div class="dd1-drop-zone" data-slot="4"></div></div><span class="dd-text">Experts agree that children benefit from helping with chores from an early age. When kids learn to make their beds, put toys away, or clear the table, they develop responsibility and practical skills. Starting with simple tasks and gradually adding more helps them build confidence.</span></div><div class="dd-slot"><div class="dd-slot-header"><span class="dd-num">F</span><div class="dd1-drop-zone" data-slot="5"></div></div><span class="dd-text">Many people struggle with procrastination when it comes to cleaning. They put off tasks like laundry or dusting until the mess becomes too big to ignore. Then they have to use a lot of elbow grease to scrub stains. Breaking chores into smaller steps can make them feel more manageable.</span></div><div class="dd-slot"><div class="dd-slot-header"><span class="dd-num">G</span><div class="dd1-drop-zone" data-slot="6"></div></div><span class="dd-text">Some people believe that having a designated place for everything makes organization much easier. When items always return to their proper spot, finding things becomes faster and the home stays neater. This approach helps reduce daily stress and keeps living spaces functional.</span></div></div><button class="ex-btn check" onclick="checkDD1()">✓ Check Answers</button><button class="ex-btn reset" onclick="resetDD1()">↺ Reset</button><div id="dd1-fb" class="ex-fb"></div></div></div></div><script>(function(){var dd1Dragged=null;var dd1Placed={};var dd1Checked=false;var dd1Answers=['h2','h1','h3','h4','h5','h6','h7'];var dd1Explanations=['A → <b>2) Sharing the load.</b> Текст: "sharing household tasks makes cleaning easier" и "This teamwork helps keep the house tidy".','B → <b>1) Less effort with gadgets.</b> В тексте: "using modern appliances makes chores much simpler".','C → <b>3) Small steps, tidy home.</b> Текст описывает: "Small daily efforts can keep a home tidy".','D → <b>4) The weekend deep clean.</b> В тексте: "do a thorough clean once a week".','E → <b>5) Teaching responsibility early.</b> Текст: "children benefit from helping with chores from an early age".','F → <b>6) Procrastination causes trouble.</b> В тексте: "put off tasks... until the mess becomes too big".','G → <b>7) Everything in its place.</b> Текст: "having a designated place for everything makes organization much easier".'];var sec1=document.getElementById('sec-dd1');sec1.querySelectorAll('.dd1-word').forEach(function(word){word.addEventListener('dragstart',function(e){dd1Dragged=this;this.classList.add('dragging');e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move';e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',this.dataset.word);});word.addEventListener('dragend',function(){this.classList.remove('dragging');});});sec1.querySelectorAll('.dd1-drop-zone').forEach(function(zone){zone.addEventListener('dragover',function(e){e.preventDefault();e.dataTransfer.dropEffect='move';this.classList.add('drag-over');});zone.addEventListener('dragleave',function(){this.classList.remove('drag-over');});zone.addEventListener('drop',function(e){e.preventDefault();this.classList.remove('drag-over');if(dd1Dragged&&!this.classList.contains('has-word')){var wordText=dd1Dragged.textContent;var wordId=dd1Dragged.dataset.word;this.textContent=wordText;this.dataset.wordId=wordId;this.classList.add('has-word');dd1Dragged.classList.add('placed');dd1Dragged.setAttribute('draggable','false');dd1Placed[this.dataset.slot]=wordId;}});zone.addEventListener('click',function(){if(this.classList.contains('has-word')&&!dd1Checked){var wordId=this.dataset.wordId;var originalWord=sec1.querySelector('.dd1-word[data-word="'+wordId+'"]');if(originalWord){originalWord.classList.remove('placed');originalWord.setAttribute('draggable','true');}this.textContent='';this.classList.remove('has-word');delete dd1Placed[this.dataset.slot];}});});window.checkDD1=function(){if(dd1Checked)return;var correct=0;var total=7;var feedback='';for(var i=0;i<7;i++){var zone=sec1.querySelector('.dd1-drop-zone[data-slot="'+i+'"]');var userAnswer=dd1Placed[i]||'';var letter=String.fromCharCode(65+i);if(userAnswer===dd1Answers[i]){correct++;zone.style.borderColor='#22c55e';zone.style.background='#dcfce7';}else{zone.style.borderColor='#ef4444';zone.style.background='#fee2e2';feedback=feedback+'<p>❌ <b>Text '+letter+'</b>: '+dd1Explanations[i]+'</p>';}}dd1Checked=true;var fb=document.getElementById('dd1-fb');if(correct===total){fb.innerHTML='<b>Отлично! Все '+correct+'/'+total+' правильные! 🎉</b><p style="margin-top:8px;">✅ <b>Лишний заголовок:</b> 8) Calling in the experts.</p>';}else{fb.innerHTML='<b>Результат: '+correct+'/'+total+'</b><br>'+feedback+'<p style="margin-top:8px;"><b>Лишний заголовок:</b> 8) Calling in the experts.</p>';}fb.className='ex-fb show '+(correct===total?'correct':'incorrect');};window.resetDD1=function(){dd1Placed={};dd1Checked=false;sec1.querySelectorAll('.dd1-drop-zone').forEach(function(zone){zone.textContent='';zone.classList.remove('has-word');zone.style.borderColor='#cbd5e1';zone.style.background='#fff';});sec1.querySelectorAll('.dd1-word').forEach(function(word){word.classList.remove('placed');word.setAttribute('draggable','true');});document.getElementById('dd1-fb').className='ex-fb';};})();</script>

Many families find that sharing chores makes the work easier for everyone. Experts recommend establishing a simple routine for household tasks. Making the bed each morning and wiping kitchen surfaces after cooking helps prevent clutter from building up <span class="dd-drop-zone" data-slot="A"></span>.</p><p>Some people prefer using modern appliances to reduce cleaning effort. Dishwashers, washing machines, and vacuum cleaners save time and energy, although basic tasks like sweeping floors still require manual work <span class="dd-drop-zone" data-slot="B"></span>.</p><p>For families with children, teaching responsibility through chores is valuable. When kids help with simple tasks like setting the table or putting away toys, they develop practical skills <span class="dd-drop-zone" data-slot="C"></span>.</p><p>Procrastination can make cleaning more difficult. Putting off laundry or dusting until messes become overwhelming <span class="dd-drop-zone" data-slot="D"></span>.</p><p>Having a designated place for everything makes organization easier. When items always return to their proper spot, finding things becomes faster and the home stays neater <span class="dd-drop-zone" data-slot="E"></span>.</p><p>Finally, a weekly deep clean can provide a fresh start. Spending time scrubbing bathrooms and sorting cupboards may be hard work, but the result is a clean slate <span class="dd-drop-zone" data-slot="F"></span>.</p></div><button class="ex-btn check" onclick="checkDD()">✓ Check Answers</button><button class="ex-btn reset" onclick="resetDD()">↺ Reset</button><div id="dd-fb" class="ex-fb"></div></div></div></div><script>var ddDragged=null;var ddPlaced={};var exChecked={dd:false};var ddAnswers={'A':'2','B':'3','C':'4','D':'1','E':'6','F':'7'};var ddExplanations={'A':'<b>A → 2</b> "and helps keep the house tidy..." class="mc3-wrap"><div class="mc3-box"><h2 class="mc3-title"> Задание 3: Reading Comprehension</h2><div class="mc3-section" id="sec-mc3"><h3>🎯 Прочитайте текст и выберите правильный вариант</h3><div class="mc3-content"><div class="mc3-text-col"><h4>Smart Cleaning Habits for Busy Teens<div class="mc3-text-size-controls"><button class="mc3-size-btn" onclick="mc3DecText()" title="Уменьшить шрифт">−</button><span class="mc3-size-display" id="mc3-text-size">16px</span><button class="mc3-size-btn" onclick="mc3IncText()" title="Увеличить шрифт">+</button></div></h4><div id="mc3-text-content"><p>Being a teenager means juggling school, friends, hobbies, and sometimes a part-time job. With so much on your plate, keeping your room and shared spaces clean can feel like an impossible task. However, developing smart cleaning habits early on can save you a lot of stress and elbow grease in the long run.</p><p>One of the biggest mistakes teens make is procrastination. Waiting until your room looks like a disaster zone before tackling it means you will spend hours sorting through piles of clothes, old homework, and random clutter. Instead, try the "five-minute rule": spend just five minutes each evening putting things back where they belong. This small effort prevents messes from snowballing into something overwhelming.</p><p>Another useful strategy is to create a designated place for everything you own. Your phone charger, school bag, favourite hoodie, and sports equipment should each have a specific spot. When you always return items to their proper place, you waste less time searching for them and your space stays organized naturally. This is the secret behind the famous saying: a place for everything, and everything in its place.</p><p>Modern gadgets can also be your best friends when it comes to cleaning. A robot vacuum, for instance, can handle daily floor cleaning while you focus on homework. A good washing machine with a quick cycle saves time on laundry days. Of course, these tools do not eliminate the need for manual work entirely — you still need to wipe surfaces, fold clothes, and take out the trash — but they significantly reduce the physical effort required.</p><p>Sharing responsibilities with family members is another key to success. When everyone pulls their weight — whether it is doing the dishes, walking the dog, or tidying the living room — the workload feels lighter for all. Learning to contribute to household chores now will prepare you for independent living later.</p><p>Finally, do not forget the power of a weekly reset. Setting aside an hour on Sunday to deep clean your room, change the sheets, and organize your desk gives you a clean slate for the week ahead. You will be surprised how much more focused and relaxed you feel in a tidy environment.</p></div></div><div class="mc3-questions-col"><div class="mc3-q-head-wrapper"><h4>Questions<div class="mc3-text-size-controls"><button class="mc3-size-btn" onclick="mc3DecFont()" title="Уменьшить шрифт">−</button><span class="mc3-size-display" id="mc3-font-size">14px</span><button i=0;i<expls.length;i++){expls[i].style.fontSize=(mc3FontSize-1)+'px';}document.getElementById('mc3-font-size').textContent=mc3FontSize+'px';}window.mc3IncText=function(){if(mc3TextSize<24){mc3TextSize+=2;applyTextSize();}};window.mc3DecText=function(){if(mc3TextSize>12){mc3TextSize-=2;applyTextSize();}};window.mc3IncFont=function(){if(mc3FontSize<20){mc3FontSize+=2;applyFontSize();}};window.mc3DecFont=function(){if(mc3FontSize>10){mc3FontSize-=2;applyFontSize();}};sec3.querySelectorAll('.mc3-opt').forEach(function(opt){opt.addEventListener('click',function(){if(mc3Checked)return;var q=this.dataset.q;var o=this.dataset.opt;sec3.querySelectorAll('.mc3-opt[data-q="'+q+'"]').forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove('selected')});this.classList.add('selected');mc3Selected[q]=o;});});window.checkMC3=function(){if(mc3Checked)return;var correct=0;var total=7;for(var q in mc3Answers){var qBlock=sec3.querySelector('.mc3-q[data-q="'+q+'"]');var userOpt=mc3Selected[q]||'';var correctOpt=mc3Answers[q];if(userOpt===correctOpt){correct++;qBlock.classList.add('correct');sec3.querySelectorAll('.mc3-opt[data-q="'+q+'"][data-opt="'+correctOpt+'"]').forEach(function(x){x.classList.add('correct-opt','disabled');});}else{qBlock.classList.add('wrong');if(userOpt){sec3.querySelectorAll('.mc3-opt[data-q="'+q+'"][data-opt="'+userOpt+'"]').forEach(function(x){x.classList.add('wrong-opt','disabled');});}sec3.querySelectorAll('.mc3-opt[data-q="'+q+'"][data-opt="'+correctOpt+'"]').forEach(function(x){x.classList.add('correct-opt','disabled');});}sec3.querySelector('.mc3-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.add('show');sec3.querySelectorAll('.mc3-opt[data-q="'+q+'"]').forEach(function(x){x.classList.add('disabled');});}mc3Checked=true;var fb=document.getElementById('mc3-fb');var pct=Math.round(correct/total*100);var msg='';if(pct===100)msg='🎉 Отлично! Все ответы верные!';else if(pct>=70)msg='👍 Хороший результат! Обратите внимание на ошибки.';else if(pct>=50)msg='💪 Неплохо, но стоит перечитать текст внимательнее.';else msg=' Перечитайте текст и попробуйте ещё раз.';fb.innerHTML='<b>Результат: '+correct+'/'+total+' ('+pct+'%)</b><div class="mc3-score"><div class="mc3-score-item" style="color:#166534">✅ Верно: '+correct+'</div><div class="mc3-score-item" style="color:#b91c1c">❌ Неверно: '+(total-correct)+'</div></div><p style="margin-top:10px">'+msg+'</p>';fb.className='mc3-fb show '+(correct===total?'correct':'incorrect');};window.resetMC3=function(){mc3Selected={};mc3Checked=false;sec3.querySelectorAll('.mc3-q').forEach(function(q){q.classList.remove('correct','wrong');});sec3.querySelectorAll('.mc3-opt').forEach(function(o){o.classList.remove('selected','correct-opt','wrong-opt','disabled');});sec3.querySelectorAll('.mc3-expl').forEach(function(e){e.classList.remove('show');});document.getElementById('mc3-fb').className='mc3-fb';};})();</script>

<div id="wordbuilding" class="section">
  <h1>Wordbuilding</h1>
  
<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="ru"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1.0"><title>Word Formation</title><style>body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;display:flex;justify-content:center;padding:20px;background:#f5f5f5}.container{background:#fff;border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.1);overflow:hidden;max-width:1000px;width:100%}h2{margin:0;padding:15px 8px;text-align:center;vertical-align:middle}th{font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap}td{border-bottom:1px solid #eee;word-break:break-word}tbody tr:hover{background:#f9f9f9}.word{display:inline-block;padding:2px 4px;color:#333;font-size:13px;line-height:1.4}.noun-suffix{color:#1565c0;font-weight:700}.neg-prefix{color:#d32f2f;font-weight:700}.adv-suffix{color:#388e3c;font-weight:700}.adj-suffix{color:#f57c00;font-weight:700}.dash{color:#999;text-align:center}</style></head><body><div class="container"><h2>📚 Word Formation</h2><table><thead><tr><th>💼 Noun</th><th>📝 Verb</th><th>💎 Adjective</th><th>⚡ Adverb</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><span class="word">tid<span class="neg-prefix">un</span>tid<span class="adv-suffix">ily</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">cleanli<span class="noun-suffix">ness</span></span></td><td><span
