class="word">order</span></td><td><span class="word">order</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">dis</span>order<span class="adj-suffix">ly</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">dis</span>order<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">safe<span class="noun-suffix">ty</span></span></td><td><span class="word">safeguard</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>safe</span></td><td><span class="word">safe<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">perfect<span class="noun-suffix">ion</span></span></td><td><span class="word">perfect</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">im</span>perfect</span></td><td><span class="word">perfect<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">sufficien<span class="noun-suffix">cy</span></span></td><td><span class="word">suffice</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>sufficien<span class="adj-suffix">t</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>sufficien<span class="adv-suffix">tly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">adequa<span class="noun-suffix">cy</span></span></td><td><span class="word">adequate</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>adequate</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>adequat<span class="adv-suffix">ely</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">respect</span></td><td><span class="word">respect</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">dis</span>respect<span class="adj-suffix">ful</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">dis</span>respect<span class="adv-suffix">fully</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">fair<span class="noun-suffix">ness</span></span></td><td><span class="word">fair</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>fair</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>fair<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">correct<span class="noun-suffix">ion</span></span></td><td><span class="word">correct</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>correct</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>correct<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">reasonable<span class="noun-suffix">ness</span></span></td><td><span class="word">reason</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>reasonable</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>reason<span class="adv-suffix">ably</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">expect<span class="noun-suffix">ation</span></span></td><td><span class="word">expect</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>expect<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>expect<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span><span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">convenien<span class="noun-suffix">ce</span></span></td><td><span class="word">convene</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>convenient</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">in</span>convenient<span class="adv-suffix">ly</span></span></td></tr><tr><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">mis</span>place<span class="noun-suffix">ment</span></span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">mis</span>place</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">mis</span>plac<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">sweep<span class="noun-suffix">ing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">sweep</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>swept</span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">wash<span class="noun-suffix">ing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">wash</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>wash<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">polish</span></td><td><span class="word">polish</span></td><td><span class="word">polish<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">fold<span class="noun-suffix">ing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">fold</span></td><td><span class="word"><span class="neg-prefix">un</span>fold<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">sort<span class="noun-suffix">ing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">sort</span></td><td><span class="word">sort<span class="adj-suffix">ed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr><tr><td><span class="word">scrub<span class="noun-suffix">bing</span></span></td><td><span class="word">scrub</span></td><td><span class="word">scrub<span class="adj-suffix">bed</span></span></td><td class="dash">—</td></tr></tbody></table></div></body></html>
<!-- ================= Задание 1 ================= --> 
<style>.wf1-wrap{max-width:900px;margin:20px auto;font-family:'Segoe UI',system-ui,sans-serif;background:#f8f9fa;padding:25px;border-radius:16px;color:#334155}.wf1-box{background:#fff;padding:25px;border-radius:16px;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)}.wf1-title{color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 20px 0;font-size:24px;font-weight:700}.wf1-section{background:#fff;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:14px;padding:20px;margin:20px 0}.wf1-section h3{font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:#0f172a;margin:0 0 15px 0;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}.wf1-instruction{background:linear-gradient(135deg,#ede9fe,#ddd6fe);border:2px solid #8b5cf6;border-radius:12px;padding:15px;margin:15px 0;font-size:14px;color:#5b21b6;line-height:1.6}.wf1-q{margin:16px 0;padding:16px;background:#f8fafc;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:10px;transition:.3s}.wf1-q.correct{background:#f0fdf4;border-color:#22c55e}.wf1-q.wrong{background:#fef2f2;border-color:#ef4444}.wf1-q-num{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:28px;height:28px;background:#8b5cf6;color:#fff;border-radius:50%;font-weight:700;font-size:14px;margin-right:8px}.wf1-q-text{font-size:15px;line-height:1.7;color:#334155}.wf1-input-wrap{position:relative;display:inline-block;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 4px}.wf1-input-hint{position:absolute;left:10px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);font-weight:600;color:#9ca3af;pointer-events:none;transition:opacity .2s;font-size:14px;font-style:italic;letter-spacing:0.5px}.wf1-input{display:inline-block;width:180px;padding:6px 10px 6px 10px;border:2px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:6px;font-size:14px;vertical-align:middle;transition:.2s;background:#fff}.wf1-input:focus{outline:none;border-color:#8b5cf6;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(139,92,246,.1)}.wf1-input.no-hint{padding-left:10px}.wf1-input.correct-input{border-color:#22c55e;background:#dcfce7;color:#166534;font-weight:600}.wf1-input.wrong-input{border-color:#ef4444;background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c}.wf1-expl{display:none;margin-top:10px;padding:10px 14px;background:#fff;border-left:4px solid #f59e0b;border-radius:6px;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5;color:#78350f}.wf1-expl.show{display:block;animation:fadeInWF1 .3s}.wf1-btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:10px 20px;margin:15px 10px 15px 0;border:none;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;transition:.2s;color:#fff}.wf1-btn:hover{transform:translateY(-1px)}.wf1-btn.check{background:#10b981;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(16,185,129,.3)}.wf1-btn.check:hover{background:#059669}.wf1-btn.reset{background:#64748b;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(100,116,139,.3)}.wf1-btn.reset:hover{background:#475569}.wf1-fb{display:none;margin-top:12px;padding:14px;border-radius:8px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.6}.wf1-fb.show{display:block;animation:fadeInWF1 .3s}.wf1-fb.correct{background:#dcfce7;color:#15803d;border-left:4px solid #22c55e}.wf1-fb.incorrect{background:#fee2e2;color:#b91c1c;border-left:4px solid #ef4444}.wf1-score{display:flex;gap:12px;margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap}.wf1-score-item{background:#fff;padding:8px 14px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0}@keyframes fadeInWF1{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(-6px)}to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)}}@media(max-width:600px){.wf1-input{width:140px}.wf1-box{padding:15px}}</style><div class="wf1-wrap"><div class="wf1-box"><h2 class="wf1-title">📝 Задание 1: Word Formation</h2><div class="wf1-section" id="sec-wf1"><h3> Образуй нужную часть речи</h3><div class="wf1-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Прочитай предложения и впиши в поле ввода правильную словообразовательную форму слова, данного в скобках (существительное, прилагательное и т.д.). Исходное слово показано в поле — замени его на нужную форму.</div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="1"><span class="wf1-q-num">1</span><span class="wf1-q-text">Keeping <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">TIDY</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q1" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span> in your room helps you find things faster. </span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="1">💡 <b>tidiness</b> — существительное от прилагательного tidy. Суффикс <b>-ness</b> образует абстрактные существительные, означающие состояние или качество.</div></div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="2"><span class="wf1-q-num">2</span><span class="wf1-q-text">Personal <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">CLEAN</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q2" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span> is important for good health. </span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="2">💡 <b>cleanliness</b> — существительное от clean. Специфическое образование с суффиксом <b>-liness</b> (обратите внимание на написание).</div></div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="3"><span class="wf1-q-num">3</span><span class="wf1-q-text">The documents were in perfect <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">ORDER</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q3" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span>, ready for inspection. 📁</span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="3"> <b>order</b> — в данном контексте используется существительное "порядок" (исходная форма). Устойчивое выражение <i>in perfect order</i>.</div></div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="4"><span class="wf1-q-num">4</span><span class="wf1-q-text">Wearing a helmet improves your <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">SAFE</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q4" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span> while cycling. 🚴</span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="4">💡 <b>safety</b> — существительное от прилагательного safe. Меняем окончание <b>-fe</b> на <b>-fety</b> (специфическое образование).</div></div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="5"><span class="wf1-q-num">5</span><span class="wf1-q-text">She strives for <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">PERFECT</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q5" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span> in everything she does. ✨</span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="5">💡 <b>perfection</b> — существительное от прилагательного perfect. Суффикс <b>-ion</b> образует существительные от глаголов и прилагательных.</div></div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="6"><span class="wf1-q-num">6</span><span class="wf1-q-text">The <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">SUFFICE</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q6" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span> of evidence was enough to close the case. ⚖️</span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="6">💡 <b>sufficiency</b> — существительное от глагола suffice. Меняем окончание <b>-e</b> на суффикс <b>-ency</b>.</div></div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="7"><span class="wf1-q-num">7</span><span class="wf1-q-text">The <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">ADEQUATE</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q7" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span> of the supplies was checked before the trip. 🎒</span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="7">💡 <b>adequacy</b> — существительное от прилагательного adequate. Меняем окончание <b>-e</b> на суффикс <b>-acy</b>.</div></div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="8"><span class="wf1-q-num">8</span><span class="wf1-q-text">Mutual <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">RESPECT</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q8" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span> is the foundation of any strong relationship. 🤝</span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="8">💡 <b>respect</b> — существительное (исходная форма). В контексте "взаимное уважение" (mutual respect).</div></div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="9"><span class="wf1-q-num">9</span><span class="wf1-q-text">The judge was praised for her <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">FAIR</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q9" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span> in making decisions. ⚖️</span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="9">💡 <b>fairness</b> — существительное от прилагательного fair. Суффикс <b>-ness</b> образует абстрактные существительные.</div></div><div class="wf1-q" data-q="10"><span class="wf1-q-num">10</span><span class="wf1-q-text">The <span class="wf1-input-wrap"><span class="wf1-input-hint">CORRECT</span><input type="text" class="wf1-input" id="wf1-q10" placeholder="" oninput="wf1HideHint(this)"></span> of the error took only a few minutes. ✅</span><div class="wf1-expl" data-expl="10">💡 <b>correction</b> — существительное от прилагательного/глагола correct. Суффикс <b>-ion</b>.</div></div><button class="wf1-btn check" onclick="checkWF1()">✓ Check Answers</button><button class="wf1-btn reset" onclick="resetWF1()">↺ Reset</button><div id="wf1-fb" class="wf1-fb"></div></div></div></div><script>(function(){var wf1Answers={'1':'tidiness','2':'cleanliness','3':'order','4':'safety','5':'perfection','6':'sufficiency','7':'adequacy','8':'respect','9':'fairness','10':'correction'};var wf1Checked=false;var secWF1=document.getElementById('sec-wf1');window.wf1HideHint=function(input){var hint=input.previousElementSibling;if(hint&&hint.classList.contains('wf1-input-hint')){if(input.value.length>0){hint.style.opacity='0';input.classList.add('no-hint');}else{hint.style.opacity='1';input.classList.remove('no-hint');}}};window.checkWF1=function(){if(wf1Checked)return;var correct=0;var total=10;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('wf1-q'+q);var userAnswer=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();var correctAnswer=wf1Answers[q].toLowerCase();if(userAnswer===correctAnswer){correct++;input.classList.add('correct-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('correct');}else{input.classList.add('wrong-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('wrong');}secWF1.querySelector('.wf1-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.add('show');}wf1Checked=true;var fb=document.getElementById('wf1-fb');var pct=Math.round(correct/total*100);var msg='';if(pct===100)msg='🎉 Отлично! Все ответы верные!';else if(pct>=80)msg='👍 Хороший результат! Обрати внимание на ошибки.';else if(pct>=60)msg='💪 Неплохо, но стоит повторить суффиксы.';else msg='📚 Повтори правила словообразования и попробуй ещё раз.';fb.innerHTML='<b>Результат: '+correct+'/'+total+' ('+pct+'%)</b><div class="wf1-score"><div class="wf1-score-item" style="color:#166534">✅ Верно: '+correct+'</div><div class="wf1-score-item" style="color:#b91c1c">❌ Неверно: '+(total-correct)+'</div></div><p style="margin-top:10px">'+msg+'</p>';fb.className='wf1-fb show '+(correct===total?'correct':'incorrect');};window.resetWF1=function(){wf1Checked=false;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('wf1-q'+q);input.value='';input.classList.remove('correct-input','wrong-input','no-hint');input.previousElementSibling.style.opacity='1';input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.remove('correct','wrong');secWF1.querySelector('.wf1-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.remove('show');}document.getElementById('wf1-fb').className='wf1-fb';};})();</script>

class="wf2-wrap"><div class="wf2-box"><h2 class="wf2-title">📝 Задание 2: Word Formation (Negative Adjectives)</h2><div class="wf2-section" id="sec-wf2"><h3>🚫 Образуй отрицательное прилагательное</h3><div class="wf2-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Прочитай предложения и впиши в поле ввода отрицательную форму прилагательного, используя нужные префиксы (un-, in-, im-, dis-). Исходное слово показано в поле — замени его на нужную форму.</div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="1"><span class="wf2-q-num">1</span><span class="wf2-q-text">His desk was so <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">TIDY</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q1" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span> that he couldn't find his phone. 🗑️</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="1">💡 <b>untidy</b> — префикс <b>un-</b> добавляется к прилагательным tidy, happy, lucky и др.</div></div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="2"><span class="wf2-q-num">2</span><span class="wf2-q-text">The water in the old well looked <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">CLEAN</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q2" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span> and nobody drank it. 💧</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="2">💡 <b>unclean</b> — префикс <b>un-</b> (unclean, unhappy, unusual, unsafe).</div></div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="3"><span class="wf2-q-num">3</span><span class="wf2-q-text">The crowd became <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">ORDER</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q3" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span> after the concert ended. 🚨</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="3">💡 <b>disorderly</b> — префикс <b>dis-</b> (disorderly, dishonest, disagree, disrespectful).</div></div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="4"><span class="wf2-q-num">4</span><span class="wf2-q-text">It's <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">SAFE</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q4" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span> to walk alone in this area at night. 🌙</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="4">💡 <b>unsafe</b> — префикс <b>un-</b> (unsafe, unfair, unknown, unfaithful).</div></div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="5"><span class="wf2-q-num">5</span><span class="wf2-q-text">The first draft was <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">PERFECT</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q5" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span>, so she revised it twice. ✏️</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="5">💡 <b>imperfect</b> — префикс <b>im-</b> используется перед словами, начинающимися на <b>p</b> или <b>b</b> (imperfect, impossible, impolite).</div></div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="6"><span class="wf2-q-num">6</span><span class="wf2-q-text">The information provided was <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">SUFFICIENT</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q6" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span> to make a decision. 📊</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="6"> <b>insufficient</b> — префикс <b>in-</b> используется перед словами, начинающимися на <b>s</b> или <b>c</b> (insufficient, incorrect, invisible).</div></div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="7"><span class="wf2-q-num">7</span><span class="wf2-q-text">The shelter offered <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">ADEQUATE</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q7" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span> protection from the storm. 🌧️</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="7">💡 <b>inadequate</b> — префикс <b>in-</b> (inadequate, inactive, incorrect, incapable).</div></div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="8"><span class="wf2-q-num">8</span><span class="wf2-q-text">It's <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">RESPECT</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q8" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span> to interrupt someone while they're speaking. ️</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="8">💡 <b>disrespectful</b> — префикс <b>dis-</b> (disrespectful, dishonest, disagreeable).</div></div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="9"><span class="wf2-q-num">9</span><span class="wf2-q-text">The referee made an <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">FAIR</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q9" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span> call that changed the game result. 🏟️</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="9">💡 <b>unfair</b> — префикс <b>un-</b> (unfair, unfaithful, unknown).</div></div><div class="wf2-q" data-q="10"><span class="wf2-q-num">10</span><span class="wf2-q-text">The answer marked as right was actually <span class="wf2-input-wrap"><span class="wf2-input-hint">CORRECT</span><input type="text" class="wf2-input" id="wf2-q10" placeholder="" oninput="wf2HideHint(this)"></span>. ❌</span><div class="wf2-expl" data-expl="10">💡 <b>incorrect</b> — префикс <b>in-</b> (incorrect, incapable, incomplete).</div></div><button class="wf2-btn check" onclick="checkWF2()">✓ Check Answers</button><button class="wf2-btn reset" onclick="resetWF2()"> Reset</button><div id="wf2-fb" class="wf2-fb"></div></div></div></div><script>(function(){var wf2Answers={'1':'untidy','2':'unclean','3':'disorderly','4':'unsafe','5':'imperfect','6':'insufficient','7':'inadequate','8':'disrespectful','9':'unfair','10':'incorrect'};var wf2Checked=false;var secWF2=document.getElementById('sec-wf2');window.wf2HideHint=function(input){var hint=input.previousElementSibling;if(hint&&hint.classList.contains('wf2-input-hint')){if(input.value.length>0){hint.style.opacity='0';input.classList.add('no-hint');}else{hint.style.opacity='1';input.classList.remove('no-hint');}}};window.checkWF2=function(){if(wf2Checked)return;var correct=0;var total=10;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('wf2-q'+q);var userAnswer=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();var correctAnswer=wf2Answers[q].toLowerCase();if(userAnswer===correctAnswer){correct++;input.classList.add('correct-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('correct');}else{input.classList.add('wrong-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('wrong');}secWF2.querySelector('.wf2-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.add('show');}wf2Checked=true;var fb=document.getElementById('wf2-fb');var pct=Math.round(correct/total*100);var msg='';if(pct===100)msg='🎉 Отлично! Все ответы верные!';else if(pct>=80)msg='👍 Хороший результат! Обрати внимание на ошибки.';else if(pct>=60)msg='💪 Неплохо, но стоит повторить префиксы.';else msg='📚 Повтори правила образования отрицательных прилагательных и попробуй ещё раз.';fb.innerHTML='<b>Результат: '+correct+'/'+total+' ('+pct+'%)</b><div class="wf2-score"><div class="wf2-score-item" style="color:#166534">✅ Верно: '+correct+'</div><div class="wf2-score-item" style="color:#b91c1c">❌ Неверно: '+(total-correct)+'</div></div><p style="margin-top:10px">'+msg+'</p>';fb.className='wf2-fb show '+(correct===total?'correct':'incorrect');};window.resetWF2=function(){wf2Checked=false;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('wf2-q'+q);input.value='';input.classList.remove('correct-input','wrong-input','no-hint');input.previousElementSibling.style.opacity='1';input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.remove('correct','wrong');secWF2.querySelector('.wf2-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.remove('show');}document.getElementById('wf2-fb').className='wf2-fb';};})();</script>

class="wf3-wrap"><div class="wf3-box"><h2 class="wf3-title">📝 Задание 3: Word Formation (Adverbs & Participles)</h2><div class="wf3-section" id="sec-wf3"><h3>✨ Образуй наречие или причастие</h3><div class="wf3-instruction"><b>Задание:</b> Прочитай предложения и впиши в поле ввода правильную форму слова (наречие или причастие/прилагательное), используя нужные суффиксы (-ly, -ed) и префиксы (un-, in-, mis-). Исходное слово показано в поле — замени его на нужную форму.</div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="1"><span class="wf3-q-num">1</span><span class="wf3-q-text">Please store the chemicals <span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">SAFE</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q1" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span> away from children. 🔒</span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="1">💡 <b>safely</b> — наречие от прилагательного safe. Суффикс <b>-ly</b>.</div></div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="2"><span class="wf3-q-num">2</span><span class="wf3-q-text">The performance was executed <span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">PERFECT</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q2" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span>, earning a standing ovation. 👏</span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="2">💡 <b>perfectly</b> — наречие от perfect. Суффикс <b>-ly</b>.</div></div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="3"><span class="wf3-q-num">3</span><span class="wf3-q-text">Clothes were thrown <span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">TIDY</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q3" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span> across the bedroom floor. 👕</span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="3">💡 <b>untidily</b> — отрицательное наречие. Префикс <b>un-</b> + суффикс <b>-ly</b> (y меняется на i).</div></div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="4"><span class="wf3-q-num">4</span><span class="wf3-q-text">The team was <span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">FAIR</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q4" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span> treated by the referees throughout the match. 📢</span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="4">💡 <b>unfairly</b> — отрицательное наречие. Префикс <b>un-</b> + суффикс <b>-ly</b>.</div></div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="5"><span class="wf3-q-num">5</span><span class="wf3-q-text">She <span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">CORRECT</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q5" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span> assumed the meeting was on Friday. 📅</span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="5">💡 <b>incorrectly</b> — отрицательное наречие. Префикс <b>in-</b> + суффикс <b>-ly</b>.</div></div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="6"><span class="wf3-q-num">6</span><span class="wf3-q-text">The <span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">EXPECT</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q6" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span> visit from my grandmother made my day. 👵</span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="6">💡 <b>unexpected</b> — отрицательное причастие/прилагательное. Префикс <b>un-</b> + суффикс <b>-ed</b>.</div></div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="7"><span class="wf3-q-num">7</span><span class="wf3-q-text"><span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">REASON</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q7" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span> high prices made people look for alternatives. </span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="7">💡 <b>unreasonably</b> — отрицательное наречие. Префикс <b>un-</b> + суффикс <b>-ably</b>.</div></div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="8"><span class="wf3-q-num">8</span><span class="wf3-q-text">The hotel was <span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">CONVENIENT</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q8" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span> located far from the city center. 🗺️</span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="8">💡 <b>inconveniently</b> — отрицательное наречие. Префикс <b>in-</b> + суффикс <b>-ly</b>.</div></div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="9"><span class="wf3-q-num">9</span><span class="wf3-q-text">I found my <span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">MISPLACE</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q9" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span> keys under the sofa cushion. 🔑</span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="9">💡 <b>misplaced</b> — причастие/прилагательное. Префикс <b>mis-</b> (неправильно) + суффикс <b>-ed</b>.</div></div><div class="wf3-q" data-q="10"><span class="wf3-q-num">10</span><span class="wf3-q-text">The <span class="wf3-input-wrap"><span class="wf3-input-hint">WASH</span><input type="text" class="wf3-input" id="wf3-q10" placeholder="" oninput="wf3HideHint(this)"></span> dishes piled up in the sink after the party. 🍽️</span><div class="wf3-expl" data-expl="10">💡 <b>unwashed</b> — отрицательное причастие/прилагательное. Префикс <b>un-</b> + суффикс <b>-ed</b>.</div></div><button class="wf3-btn check" onclick="checkWF3()">✓ Check Answers</button><button class="wf3-btn reset" onclick="resetWF3()"> Reset</button><div id="wf3-fb" class="wf3-fb"></div></div></div></div><script>(function(){var wf3Answers={'1':'safely','2':'perfectly','3':'untidily','4':'unfairly','5':'incorrectly','6':'unexpected','7':'unreasonably','8':'inconveniently','9':'misplaced','10':'unwashed'};var wf3Checked=false;var secWF3=document.getElementById('sec-wf3');window.wf3HideHint=function(input){var hint=input.previousElementSibling;if(hint&&hint.classList.contains('wf3-input-hint')){if(input.value.length>0){hint.style.opacity='0';input.classList.add('no-hint');}else{hint.style.opacity='1';input.classList.remove('no-hint');}}};window.checkWF3=function(){if(wf3Checked)return;var correct=0;var total=10;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('wf3-q'+q);var userAnswer=input.value.trim().toLowerCase();var correctAnswer=wf3Answers[q].toLowerCase();if(userAnswer===correctAnswer){correct++;input.classList.add('correct-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('correct');}else{input.classList.add('wrong-input');input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.add('wrong');}secWF3.querySelector('.wf3-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.add('show');}wf3Checked=true;var fb=document.getElementById('wf3-fb');var pct=Math.round(correct/total*100);var msg='';if(pct===100)msg='🎉 Отлично! Все ответы верные!';else if(pct>=80)msg='👍 Хороший результат! Обрати внимание на ошибки.';else if(pct>=60)msg='💪 Неплохо, но стоит повторить суффиксы и префиксы.';else msg='📚 Повтори правила образования наречий и причастий и попробуй ещё раз.';fb.innerHTML='<b>Результат: '+correct+'/'+total+' ('+pct+'%)</b><div class="wf3-score"><div class="wf3-score-item" style="color:#166534">✅ Верно: '+correct+'</div><div class="wf3-score-item" style="color:#b91c1c">❌ Неверно: '+(total-correct)+'</div></div><p style="margin-top:10px">'+msg+'</p>';fb.className='wf3-fb show '+(correct===total?'correct':'incorrect');};window.resetWF3=function(){wf3Checked=false;for(var q=1;q<=10;q++){var input=document.getElementById('wf3-q'+q);input.value='';input.classList.remove('correct-input','wrong-input','no-hint');input.previousElementSibling.style.opacity='1';input.parentElement.parentElement.parentElement.classList.remove('correct','wrong');secWF3.querySelector('.wf3-expl[data-expl="'+q+'"]').classList.remove('show');}document.getElementById('wf3-fb').className='wf3-fb';};})();</script>

class="tab-content c1"><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">1</span><p class="q-text">🧹 What Why?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I prefer to <mark>tidy up</mark> in the evening because it helps me <mark>start</mark> the next day with a <mark>clean slate</mark>. It also helps me relax before bed.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">4</span><p class="q-text">🧹 What do you do when you see a <mark>stain</mark> on your clothes?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I usually <mark>try</mark> to <mark>scrub</mark> it gently with <mark>detergent</mark>. If it doesn't work, I <mark>remember</mark> to <mark>do the laundry</mark> properly next time.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">5</span><p class="q-text">🧹 How often do you <mark>take out the trash</mark> at home?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>take out the bin</mark> every evening after dinner. It's a small <mark>chore</mark>, but it helps our <mark>flat</mark> stay <mark>tidy</mark> and <mark>clean</mark>.</p></div></div></label></div><div class="tab-content c2"><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">6</span><p class="q-text">👕 Do you <mark>do the laundry</mark> yourself or does someone help you?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I usually <mark>do the laundry</mark> myself on weekends. I <mark>sort</mark> clothes by colour first, then <mark>load</mark> the <mark>vacuum</mark>—sorry, the washing machine!</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">7</span><p class="q-text">👕 What's the hardest part about <mark>folding</mark> clothes?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">The hardest part is <mark>folding</mark> fitted sheets! I <mark>keep</mark> <mark>trying</mark> to <mark>put them in order</mark>, but they always end up <mark>misplaced</mark> in the <mark>cupboard</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">8</span><p class="q-text">👕 Do you <mark>remember</mark> to <mark>hang up</mark> clothes or do you <mark>forget</mark> sometimes?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>remember</mark> to <mark>hang up</mark> shirts, but I sometimes <mark>forget</mark> to <mark>iron</mark> them. I <mark>need</mark> to <mark>start</mark> being more consistent with this <mark>chore</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">9</span><p class="q-text">👕 How do you <mark>sort out</mark> dirty clothes before washing?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>sort out</mark> clothes by fabric type and colour. This helps me <mark>avoid</mark> <mark>unwashed</mark> items and <mark>keep</mark> everything <mark>tidy</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">10</span><p class="q-text">👕 What <mark>detergent</mark> do you use and why?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I use a mild <mark>detergent</mark> because it's <mark>safe</mark> for sensitive skin. It also helps <mark>scrub</mark> <mark>stains</mark> without damaging the fabric.</p></div></div></label></div><div class="tab-content c3"><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">11</span><p class="q-text">🍽️ What do you do after meals to <mark>clear the table</mark>?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">After meals, I <mark>clear the table</mark> and <mark>wipe down</mark> surfaces. I also <mark>help</mark> to <mark>load</mark> the dishwasher or <mark>wash up</mark> by hand.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">12</span><p class="q-text">🍽️ How do you <mark>keep</mark> your kitchen <mark>tidy</mark> during cooking?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>keep things tidy</mark> by <mark>putting away</mark> ingredients as I use them. This helps me <mark>finish</mark> cooking without a big <mark>mess</mark> to <mark>clear away</mark> later.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">13</span><p class="q-text">🍽️ Do you <mark>remember</mark> to <mark>wipe</mark> the <mark>cupboard</mark> doors regularly?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>try</mark> to <mark>wipe</mark> <mark>cupboard</mark> doors weekly. Sometimes I <mark>forget</mark>, but I <mark>need</mark> to <mark>keep</mark> doing it to <mark>maintain cleanliness</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">14</span><p class="q-text">🍽️ What's your strategy for <mark>scrubbing</mark> tough <mark>stains</mark>?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">For tough <mark>stains</mark>, I <mark>try</mark> using <mark>elbow grease</mark> and a bit of <mark>detergent</mark>. If that doesn't work, I <mark>do over</mark> the area until it's <mark>clean</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">15</span><p class="q-text">🍽️ How do you <mark>put away</mark> dishes after washing?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>put away</mark> dishes by category: plates together, cups in the <mark>cupboard</mark>. This helps me <mark>keep things tidy</mark> and find things easily later.</p></div></div></label></div><div class="tab-content c4"><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">16</span><p class="q-text">🛁 What <mark>chores</mark> do you do to <mark>keep</mark> the bathroom <mark>clean</mark>?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>wipe</mark> the sink after use and <mark>scrub</mark> the shower weekly. I also <mark>remember</mark> to <mark>take out</mark> the <mark>bin</mark> regularly.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">17</span><p class="q-text">🛁 How often do you <mark>polish</mark> bathroom fixtures?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>try</mark> to <mark>polish</mark> fixtures once a month. It's not my favourite <mark>chore</mark>, but it helps <mark>maintain cleanliness</mark> and makes the room look <mark>tidy</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">18</span><p class="q-text">🛁 Do you <mark>forget</mark> to <mark>clean up</mark> after using the bathroom?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">Sometimes I <mark>forget</mark> to <mark>wipe down</mark> the sink, but I <mark>remember</mark> to <mark>tidy up</mark> before bed. I <mark>need</mark> to <mark>start</mark> being more consistent.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">19</span><p class="q-text">🛁 What do you do when you find a <mark>stain</mark> on bathroom tiles?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>try</mark> to <mark>scrub</mark> it with <mark>detergent</mark> and a brush. If it's <mark>incomplete</mark>, I <mark>do over</mark> the area until it looks <mark>perfect</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">20</span><p class="q-text">🛁 How do you <mark>keep</mark> bathroom <mark>appliances</mark> <mark>tidy</mark>?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>put away</mark> toothbrushes and <mark>hang up</mark> towels after use. This helps me <mark>maintain cleanliness</mark> and <mark>avoid</mark> <mark>misplaced</mark> items.</p></div></div></label></div><div class="tab-content c5"><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">21</span><p class="q-text">🧽 When was the last time you did a <mark>clean sweep</mark> of your room?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">Last weekend I did a <mark>clean sweep</mark>: I <mark>swept</mark>, <mark>mopped</mark>, and <mark>dusted</mark> everything. It felt great to <mark>wipe the slate clean</mark> and <mark>start</mark> fresh.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">22</span><p class="q-text">🧽 How do you <mark>tackle clutter</mark> in your <mark>wardrobe</mark>?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>sort out</mark> clothes I don't wear and <mark>throw out</mark> or donate them. Then I <mark>put things in order</mark> so everything has <mark>a place</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">23</span><p class="q-text">🧽 What's your approach to <mark>scrubbing</mark> <mark>unswept</mark> floors?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">First I <mark>sweep</mark> to remove dust, then I <mark>mop</mark> with <mark>detergent</mark>. For tough spots, I use <mark>elbow grease</mark> to <mark>scrub</mark> until they're <mark>clean</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">24</span><p class="q-text">🧽 Do you <mark>remember</mark> to <mark>dust</mark> high shelves or do you <mark>forget</mark>?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I sometimes <mark>forget</mark> to <mark>dust</mark> high shelves, but I <mark>try</mark> to <mark>remember</mark> during deep cleaning. It's important to <mark>keep</mark> everything <mark>tidy</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">25</span><p class="q-text">🧽 How do you <mark>keep</mark> your <mark>hoover</mark> or <mark>vacuum</mark> in good condition?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>remember</mark> to <mark>empty</mark> the <mark>bin</mark> after each use and <mark>check</mark> the filter monthly. This helps the <mark>appliance</mark> work <mark>properly</mark> and last longer.</p></div></div></label></div><div class="tab-content c6"><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">26</span><p class="q-text">🗂️ How do you <mark>put things in order</mark> in your <mark>chest of drawers</mark>?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>sort</mark> clothes by type and season, then <mark>fold</mark> them neatly. This helps me <mark>keep things tidy</mark> and find items <mark>conveniently</mark>.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">27</span><p class="q-text">🗂️ What's your system for <mark>keeping</mark> the <mark>cupboard</mark> <mark>orderly</mark>?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>put away</mark> items by category and label shelves. This helps me <mark>maintain cleanliness</mark> and <mark>avoid</mark> <mark>misplaced</mark> things.</p></div></div></label><label class="flip-card"><input type="checkbox" class="flip-input"><div class="flip-inner"><div class="flip-front"><span class="q-num">28</span><p class="q-text">🗂️ Do you <mark>remember</mark> where everything is, or do things get <mark>misplaced</mark>?</p><span class="flip-hint">↻ Tap to flip</span></div><div class="flip-back"><p class="q-text">I <mark>try</mark> to <mark>remember</mark> where things go, but sometimes items get <mark>misplaced</mark>.
